# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Nahase and Paguemen

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 9  
**Columns**: pp. 238–480  

[p. 238] {#po.9.p238}
"በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ አምላክ ። ወርኃ ፡ ነሐሴ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ መዓልቱ ፡ ፲ሰዓት ። ወእምዝ ፡ የሐጽጽ ። አመ ፡ ጸለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God. Blessed month of Nahasê. The (first) day has ten hours. Then (the days) decrease. READING FOR THE FIRST OF NAHASÊ (August 7).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ › ክቡር ፡ አባ ፣ አቦሊ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዮስጦስ ። ለ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ሮም ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፀብእ፣ ወተመ ይጠ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወረከቦ ፣ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ክሕዶ ፡ ለክርስ ቶስ ፡ ወአቆመ ፡ አምልኮ ፤ ጣዖታት ። ወኮነ ፡ ይክል ፤ ከመ ፣ ያጥፍኦ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖ ስ ፡ ወይንሣእ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ እምኔሁ ፤ አላ ፡ ሐለየ ፤ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያዊተ ፡ ዘአልባ
On this day is the holy (and) illustrious Abba Apollon, martyr, son of Justus. This saint possessed the kingdom of Rome. When he was at war and returned to the city of Antioch, he found Diocletian having (already) apostatized from Christ and re-established the worship of idols. He could have annihilated Diocletian and taken (his) kingdom from him. But he thought of the heavenly kingdom, which has no end.


[p. 240] {#po.9.p240}
ቲ ፡ ማኅለቅተ ፣ ወዔጦ ፣ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወአምድኅ ረዝ ፡ አገበሮ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፣ እስከ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ለዮ ስጦስ ፣ ወለብአሲቱ ፡ ታውክልያ ፡ ወለአባ :.አቦሊ ፣ ወልዱ ፣ ዘተዝክሩ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለ ት ። ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሄርሜኔዎስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክን ድርያ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ዕበ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኀቤከ ፣ ዮስጦስ ፡ ወብአሲ ቱ ፡ ወወልዱ ፡ አባ ፡ አቦሊ ፡ ጊጦሙአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሠፁዑ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ወለአመ ፡ ኢ ሰምዑ ፡ ወኢተአዘዙ ፣ ወኢሦፁ ፡ ለአማልክት ፤ ወእመአኮ ፣ ፍልጦሙ ፡ በበይናቲሆ
Diocletian (sought) hard to win him over, but he did not listen to him. Then, Justus pushed Diocletian to the limit, so that (the latter) wrote a letter and sent away Justus, his wife Thecla, and Abba Apollon, his son, whose memory is on this day. He sent them to the land of Egypt, to Hermenewos, judge of the city of Alexandria. He said to him in the letter: "When Justus, his wife, and his son Abba Apollon come to you, (seek) to win them over, so that they may sacrifice to the gods. If they do not listen, do not obey, and do not sacrifice to the gods, then separate them from one another, and send each of them to a (different) city."
ሙ ፡ ወፈንዎሙ ፡ ለለ፳8እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፣ አስተርአዮሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዮስጦስ ፣ ወለብእሲቱ ፡ ወለወልዱ ፡ ወአጽንዖሙ ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ፡፣ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ዘይከውን ፣፤ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ወተክየዶ ሙ ፡ ለማኅደር ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ሰማያዊት ። ወሶበ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወ ርእዮሙ ፡ ሄርሜኔዎስ ፣ መኩንን ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፤ እሙን ቱ ፡ ኃደጉ ፡ መንግሥቶሙ ፤ ወኢደፈረ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትናገሮሙ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሕሙ መ ፣፡ አላ ፡ ተናገሮሙ ፡በነገር ፡ ልምሉም ፡ ወቴጦሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡እ
That night, Our Lord Christ appeared to Justus, his wife, and his son, encouraged them, consoled them, explained to them what would happen to them, and promised them a beautiful heavenly dwelling. When they had arrived at the city of Alexandria, and Hermenewos, the judge, had seen them, he was greatly astonished at them, for they had abandoned their kingdom. The judge did not dare to address infamous words to them, but he spoke to them in gentle language and (sought) hard to win them over, so that they might turn back from their design. But they did not

ምክሮሙ ፡ ወኢሰምፁ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተቄጥዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ እስከ ፡ አፍለ ጦሙ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ለዮስጦስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ አንዴናው ፣ ወለብእሲቱ ፡ ታውክልያ ፡ ኀበ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ፃ ፡ ወለአባ ፣ አቦሊ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በስጣ ፡ ወወሀቦ ሙ ፡ ለለ፳8አእምኔሆሙ ፡ አገብረ ፡ እምአግብርቲሆሙ ፡ከመ ፡ ይትለአክዎሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አባ ፡ አቦሊ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በስጣ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ። ወኩነኖ ፡ ዙነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በዋዕየ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወበመንኩራኩ ራት ፡ ወመቲረ ፡ መለያልያት ፡ ወውግረት ፡ ለአናብስት ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ያጸንዖ ፡ ወያድኅኖ ፡ ወያነሥኦ ፤ እምውስተ ፡ ኩኔኔ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስና ። ወብዙኃን፣ ሰብእ ፡ አለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘበጽሖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አቦሊ ፡ አምኩነኔ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ጥዑይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በአግዚአእነ ፡ ክ ርስቶስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ ሰማዕተ ። ወመኩንንሰ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ወጠብሑ፣፤ አነዳሁ ፣ ወአጥመቁ ፡ አጽርቅተ ፡፣ ዘሠቀ ፡ ጸኾር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሒአእ ፡ ወፄው ፡ ወመዝ መዙ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኑሰላቲሁ ፡ ወአጾርዎ ፡ አነዳሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መታክፍቲሁ ፣ ወአኦድ
listen to him. Immediately, Justus became indignant against the (judge), to the point that he separated them from one another. He sent Justus to the city of 'Andênäw, his wife Thecla to the city of 88, and Abba Apollon, his son, to the city of Bastâ. He gave each of them a servant, (taken) from among their servants, so that (the latter) might serve them. When Abba Apollon had arrived at the city of Bastâ, he confessed Our Lord Christ before the judge. (The latter) condemned him to great tortures: burning by fire, the wheels, the severing of limbs, being thrown to the lions. But the Lord strengthened him; He delivered him and snatched him from the tortures, safe and sound, without injury. Many people who lived there, when they had seen the tortures inflicted on Saint Abba Apollon, and (had verified) that he was safe and sound, without injury, were greatly astonished, believed in Our Lord Christ, and became martyrs. But the judge became angry at the saint. He ordered that his skin be flayed, that rags of haircloth be soaked in vinegar and salt, that all his wounds be rubbed with them, that he be made to carry his skin on his shoulders, and that he be made to make the

ዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሀገር ። ወጸለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ " ኦአግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወ ልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ርድአኒ ፡ ወአድኅነኒ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፣ መጽ አ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወገሰሰ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወፈወሰ ፡ ቀዮጎሰላቲሁ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ቦቱ ። ወቆመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ አስተርአ የ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦኅሩይየ ፡ አቦሊ ፡፤ ጽናዕ ፣ ወኢትፍራህ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እሄሉ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትፌጽም ፡ ገድ ለከ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወዓርገ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ በዓቢይ ፣ ስብሐ ት ። ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አቦሊ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ርአዮ ፡ ለአግዚእአነ ፡ ክ
tour of the whole city. (The saint) prayed and said: "O my Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the Lord, help me and deliver me from this torment." Immediately, the angel Michael came to him, touched his body, and healed his wounds. (The judge) ordered that he be led to prison, until he had reflected on what he would do with him. The saint stood and prayed in the prison. While he was praying, Our Lord Christ - glory to Him! - appeared to him and said to him: "Greetings to you, my chosen one Apollon, be strong, and do not fear, for I myself will be with you until you finish your combat!" When Our Lord had said this to him, He ascended to the heavens in great glory. Saint Abba Apollon rejoiced with great joy when he had seen Our Lord Christ.
ርስቶስ ። ወሀሎ ፡አኔብእሲ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አስኪሮን ፡ ወሎቱ ፡ ፪ውሉድ ፡ ተባዕት ፡ ወአ ምጽአ ፡ ገባራተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንሕሉ ፡ አረፍተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወይሕንጽዎ ፡ ሐዲሰ። ወአዘዞሙ ፥፣ ለ፪ውሉዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቁሙ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፣ ይፌጽሙ ፡ ግብሮሙ ።= ወሶበ ፡ ዓር ጉ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አረፍት ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ወቀተለ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ፲ወሂፅደወ ፣ ወ፪ውሎዶ ፡
There was a rich man called Askiron. He had two boys. He brought in workers to tear down the walls of his house and build a new one. He ordered his two sons to stand near them until (the workers) had finished their work. When they had climbed onto a wall, (it) collapsed and killed seventeen men among (the workers), and the

two sons of this rich man. They told the rich man how it had happened. He tore his clothes, cried, wept, went to Saint Abba Apollon, prostrated himself at his feet, and said to him: "Have pity on me, for my two sons are dead, and with them seventeen men. If you raise them from the dead, I myself will believe in your God."
ወዕበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱ ስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘኃረይከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጻሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ክሥት ፡ ኃይለ ከ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ዕደው ፡ አለ ፣ ሞቱ ፡ ወአንሥኦሙ ፡ እሞት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይአመኑ ፡ አሉ ፡ ኩሎ ሙ፥፡ሰብእ ፡ ወያእምሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘእንበሌከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለከ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወነሥአ ፡ አነዳሁ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ዕደው ፡ እ ንዘ ፡ ይብ” ል ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያ ው ፡ ትትነሥኡ ፡ሕያዋን ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ወአንሥኦሙ ፣ ወኩሎ ሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ይኔጽሩ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ከልሑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ 8ውአእቱ ፡ አምላ ክሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ አቦሊ ። ወሶቤ ሃ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ አስኪሮን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ይእዜኒ ፤ አእመርኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አምላክ ከ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣፤ አመንኩ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወአምኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብ
When he had said this to him, the saint rose, prayed, and said: "O my Lord Jesus Christ, You who chose me so that I might toil for the sake of Your holy name, manifest Your power over (these) men who are dead, and raise them from the dead, so that all these people may believe and know that there is no (other) God but You, for to You is the glory for ever and ever. Amen!" He took his skin and spread it over these men, saying: "In the name of my Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living Lord, rise up all alive." He took their hand and made them stand up. All the people were watching. Immediately, everyone cried out, saying: "Unique is the God of the Christians, Jesus Christ, God of Saint Abba Apollon." Immediately, Askiron prostrated himself before the saint, saying: "Now I know that your great God is Jesus Christ; I believe in Him." All the people of his house believed with him, and the judge was confounded.
አ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወተኃፍረ ፡ መኩንን ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተካየዶ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦኅሩይየ ፡አባ፣ አቦሊ ፣ ኩሉ ፣ ብአሷ ፣ ዘሀሎ ፣ ውስተ ፡፣ ምንዳቤ ፣ ወይስእል ፣ ረድኤተ ፡ በስምከ፣ አነ ፡ እሰምዕ ፡ ስአለቶ ፡ ወአፌጽም ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኀሚሦቶ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘየሐ ንጽ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በስምከ ፡ አነ ፡ አኃድሮ ፣ በውስተ ፡ መንግሥትየ ። ወኩሉ ፣ ዘይጽሕፍ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ስምዕከ ፡፣ አው ፡ ዘያነብቦ ፡ አው ፡ ዘይሰምዖ ፡ አነ ፣ እጽሕፍ ፡ስ ሞ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ለ፪እንስሳ ፡ ወአዓርፎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ”ሕጽነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ወአበልዖ ፣ እምሠናያተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ። ወለአመ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ኃጣውአ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ ወነስሐ ፡ ወይስእል ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ በስምከ ፡ አነ ፡ አኅኀድግ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኃጣውኢሁ ። ወዘአብልዓ ፡ ርኀበ ፡ በስምከ ፡ አኀድግ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አበሳሁ ፣ ወአበልዖ ፡ እምፍሬያተ፣ ገነት ፡ ወሰላምየ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌክ ፡ አሜን ። ወዘንተ ፣፡ ብሂሎ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ሰ ላመ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ።
Our Lord Christ appeared to him, gave him the kidän, and said to him: "O my chosen one Abba Apollon, (of) every man who will be in torment and will ask for help in your name, I myself will hear the prayer and will grant all requests. Every man who builds a church in your name, I myself will make him dwell in my kingdom. (Of) every (individual) who writes the history of your martyrdom, or reads it, or hears it, I myself will write the name on the wings of the Four Animals; I will make him rest in the bosom of Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob; and I will feed him with the excellent (fruits) of the paradise of delights. If he has committed many sins, has repented, and has prayed to me in your name, I myself will forgive his sins. To him who has fed the hungry in your name, I will forgive his faults, and I will feed him with the fruits of paradise. May my peace be with you! Amen." Saying this, Our Savior gave him peace and ascended to the heavens in great glory.


[p. 244] {#po.9.p244}

When the judge was weary of torturing him, he ordered that his holy head be cut off with the sword. He received the crown of martyrdom. His body until now rests in the convent of Handaq, outside the city of Cairo. Through him took place many miracles and wonders and great


[p. 245] {#po.9.p245}
ወፈውስ ፡ ዓቢይ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፣ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፣ ለአቦሊ ፡ ዘኢተመልዓን ፡ ናፍቆ ፡=፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜንን ፡ ብሩሮ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይገድፍ ፡ ወርቆ በእንተ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ አብ ፡ እማኅፀን ፡ ዘአልሀቆ ፡=፡ እስክ ፣ በመጥባሕት ፡ ለአነዳሁ ፡ ዘሐቆ
healings. May the Lord have pity on us through his prayer and may his blessing and intercession be with us! Amen. [Greetings to Apollon, who was not filled with hesitation, In repudiating his money and rejecting his gold! For Jesus, (true) Father, who raised him from the womb (of his mother), He did not deny his faith, To such an extent that before the sinful judge his skin was flayed with the knife.]
በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ኢሐሰወ ፡ ጽድቆ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ለአቦሊ ፣ ማኅበሩ ። ፪፻ወጓወአንስት ፡ ካዕበተ ፡ አሥሩ ፡፡ እስከ ፡ አጥባዕክሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ክሳደክሙ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡፡ አመ ፡ ዘኅቁ ፡ አነዳሁ ፡ ወአጽመ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ሰበሩ ፡፡
[Greetings to you, companions of Apollon, Two hundred and forty (men) and twenty women! (Courageous) to the point of enduring that your necks be cut with the sword. When (Apollon's) skin had been flayed, and his ribs had been broken, Torture which did not terrify your heart.]
ዘኢያደንገፀ ፡ ልበክሙ ፡ ፃዕሩ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካራ ፡ ለሐና ፣ እማ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ፣0 ማርያም ፡ወተዝካ ራ ፡ ለሐና ፡ ነቢይት ፡ ወለተ ፡ ፋኑኤል ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶን ፡ አ ሜን።
On this day also is the memory of Anne, mother of Our Lady Mary, and the memory of Anne, the prophetess, daughter of Phanuel. May the Lord have pity on us through their prayer! Amen.


[p. 246] {#po.9.p246}
ሰላም ፡ ሰላም ፣ ደናግል ፥ ሠላስ ፣ ኢስ ፡ ጢስ ፡ አላሏስ ፡ ወአጋኢስ ፡=፡ አመ ፡ኮና ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡=፡ ኢያውዓየ ፡ ሥጋሆን ፡ ነበልባለ ፡ እሳት ፡ መብዕስ ፡፡
[Greetings, greetings to the three virgins Pistis, 'Alâpis, and 'Agâpis! When they were martyrs for the sake of Jesus Christ, The flame of the burning fire did not burn their bodies, And the smoke did not obscure their beauty.]

[On this day also the Fathers have ordered to make the memory of the illustrious men Joseph and Nicodemus, who were (judged) worthy to become the ministers of the divine mystery. They themselves took down from the top of the cross the body of Our Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living Lord, when He had conformed to the law of human nature, except for sin alone, and had accepted suffering and death for the sake of our salvation. The scarecrow of the Jews did not frighten these saints, so that they asked Pilate for the body of (Jesus), (praying him) to give them his corpse. When Pilate had permitted them to take him down from the cross, after having pulled out the nails from his hands and his feet, they carried him on their shoulders. They heard the voice of the angels, standing near the corpse of his body, in the process


[p. 247] {#po.9.p247}
ሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኃያል ፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ዘኢይመውት ፡ ዘተወ ልደ ፡ እማርያም ፡ እምቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ተሣሀለነ ፡ እግዚኦ ። ቅዱስ ፣ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኃያል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሕያው ፤ ዘኢይመውት ፡ ዘተጠምቀ ፡ በዮርዳኖስ ፡ ወተሰ ቅለ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ተሣሀለነ ፡ እግዚኦ ። ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኃያ ል ፡ቅዱስ ፡ሕያው ፡ ዘኢይመውት ፡ዘተንሥአ ፡ እሙታን ፡ አመ ፡ሣልስት ፡ ዕለ"ት0፣ ዓርገ፣ በስብሐት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በየማነ ፡ አቡሁ ፣ ዳግመ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ በ ስብሐት ፡ ይኩንን ፡ ሕያዋነ ፣ ወሙታነ ፡ ተሣሀለነ ፡ እግዚኦ ። ስብሐት ፣ ለአብ ፡ ስ ብሐት ፡ ለወልድ ፣ ስብሐት ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፣ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓ ለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ለይኩን ፡ ለይኩን ።
of saying: "Holy is the Lord; holy is the Strong; holy is the Living, who does not die, who was born of Mary, of the Holy Virgin; have pity on us, O Lord. Holy is the Lord; holy is the Strong; holy is the Living, who does not die, who was baptized in the Jordan, and was crucified on the wood of the cross; have pity on us, O Lord. Holy is the Lord; holy is the Strong; holy is the Living, who does not die, who rose from the dead on the third day, ascended in glory to the heavens, is seated at the right hand of His Father, will come again in glory, in order to judge the living and the dead; have pity on us, O Lord. Glory to the Father, glory to the Son, glory to the Holy Spirit, Now, forever and for ever and ever! Amen. So be it. So be it."
ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸንዓ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ወዮ ሴፍ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ሰንዱናተ ፡ ወኒቆዲሞስ ፡ ተሣየጠ ፡ አፈዋተ ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ ለአግዚአነ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወዮሴፍ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘመዱ ፡ ለኒቆዲሞ ስ ፡ እኅወ ፡ ቀለዮጳ ፣ ዘመደ ፣ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወኒቆዲሞስ ‹ ፈሪሳዊ ፡ ውእቱ።፣መልአከ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ዘሖረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ሌሊተ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ "ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ሎቱ ፣ ስብሐት ፡ ወአምነ ፣ ምጽአቶ ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ ወነገሮ ፣ እግዚእነ ፣ ከ መ፤ ኢይክል ፡ በዊአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ዘኢተወልደ ፡ እማይ ፡ ወእመ
When they had heard this, their faith in Christ - glory to Him! - was strengthened. Joseph brought linens, and Nicodemus bought perfumes. They buried Our Lord, and interred him in a new tomb. Joseph was a relative of Nicodemus, brother of Cleopas, relative of Stephen, the first martyr. Nicodemus was a Pharisee, a prince of the Jews. It is he who had gone long ago at night to Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - and had believed in his coming from heaven. Our Lord had explained to him that he who would not be born of water and the Holy Spirit could not enter the kingdom of heaven.


[p. 248] {#po.9.p248}
ንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜያተ ፣ ይጌሥጾሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ያስተሐቅ ሩ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ።
Many times he rebuked the Jews when they despised the word of Our Lord.

After his resurrection, while they were going on the road to Emmaus, those whom Our Lord met were these same Cleopas and Nicodemus. They spoke to him of everything that had taken place in Jerusalem, without recognizing him. He himself explained to them that the prophets had prophesied concerning his death and his resurrection. Then, when they recognized him, he hid himself from them. Having returned (to Jerusalem), they recounted (this) to his disciples. The Jews chained Joseph in prison, wanting to kill him, because he had interred Our Lord. While the door of the prison was closed with the seal of Pilate and with the seal of the princes of the priests, Our Lord Jesus entered where Joseph was, being carried on the chariot of the Cherubim. With him were thousands and myriads of angels. The Seraphim stood around him and offered incense before him. The thief on the right stood at his right, being clothed in luminous garments. He interceded for the sinners, for he had found grace before (God). Seeing (this), the guards of the prison were terrified, and terror seized them. Then, Our Lord called Joseph and said to him: "Do not fear the threats of the Jews; behold, I have

come to undo your bonds. I am Jesus of Nazareth. O you, who have been associated with my Passion, see the holes in my hands and my feet and the perforation of my side, so that you may recognize manifestly that it is I." Then, he took Joseph from there, and he led him to the city of Arimathea. Having gone to Pilate and the princes of the priests, the guards of the prison recounted to them everything they had seen and heard from the mouth of Our Lord: how he had delivered Joseph and had taken him with him. Having gone, the princes of the priests found the door of the prison closed. The seal itself was not broken. These saints continued to preach the Gospel with the disciples. A great persecution reached them. Then they died in peace. May the Lord have pity on us through their prayer forever! Amen.]

[I say greetings to the rich Joseph, Who did not participate in the council with the people of the Jews! At the moment when the sun sets, So that the body of Jesus would not spend the night on the cross, He asked (for it) from Pilate with eagerness.]

Had come from the Father for mercy and for salvation! The day when (Jesus) was crucified, while he was covering his nakedness, Having become the companion and associate of Joseph, With perfumes he buried and interred his corpse.]

On this day also is the memory of our Father Simeon, of the convent of Däâgâ, who washed the feet of his Lord. May the Lord have pity on us through his prayer! Amen. In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፤ ፪ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 2ND OF NAHASÊ (August 8).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፣ አትናስያ ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሰብአ ሀገረ ፣ መኑፍ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ብፁላን ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፉ ፣ አበዊሃ ፣ ሐለየት ፣፡ ሕሊ ና ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትረሲ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፣ ወለነጋድያን ፣ ወገብረት ፡
On this day died Saint Athanasia. This saint was from the people of the city of Manouf. Her parents were rich. When her parents died, she had the good thought to make her house the dwelling of monks and pilgrims. She did so. She received all those who came to her, and


[p. 251] {#po.9.p251}
ከመዝ ። ወኮነት ፡ ትትዌከፎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ ወትሁቦሙ ፡ ዘ ይፈቅዱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሐልቀ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ንዋያ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እኩያን ፡ "ዘሕ ጮም ፡ ምግባሮሙ ፡ ወአጽነኑ ፡ ሕሊናሃ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ትገብር ፡ ኃጢ ኦተ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትገብር ፡ ሠናየ ።
gave them what they wanted, so that all her riches were exhausted. Perverse men, whose actions were abominable, gathered to her and bent her mind to commit sin. She committed sin continually, much more than she had accomplished good.
ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ መነኮሳት ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ። ወጸውዕዎ ፡ ለአባ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፣ ሐጺር ፣ ወአይድ ፅዎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኃቤሃ ፡ ወይግበር ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ዘገብረት ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፣ እምሠናያት ፡ ከመ ፡ያድኅን ፡ ነፍሳ ። ወተ አዘዘ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በእንተዝ ፣ ወሰአሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትራድእዎ ፡ በጸሎት ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘሀለወት ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ለዓቃቢተ ፣ ፕኅተ ፡ ቤታ ፣ አይድዒ ፣ ለእግዝእትኪ ፣ በምጽአትየ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ አይድዓታ ፡ በምጽአቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ መሰላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ በእንተ፤ ገቢረ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወተሰርገወት ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ዲበ ፣ ዓራታ ፣ ወጸውዓቶ ። ወቦአ ፡ ኅቤ ሃ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይዜምር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እመኒ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ማአከለ ፣ ጽላሎተ ፣ ሞት ፣ ኢይፈርሆ ፡ ለእኩይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ምስሌየ ።
Her story was learned by holy elders, monks in the desert of Scetis. They were sad with great sadness concerning her. They called Abba John the Little, recounted to him what had happened to (Athanasia), and asked him to go to her and perform charity with her, in return for the good she had done with them, in order to save her soul. He obeyed them in this regard and asked them to help him with prayer. Saint Abba John rose, left, and arrived at the place in which she was. He said to the portress of her house: "Announce my coming to your mistress." When she had announced the coming of the saint to her, it seemed to her that he himself was coming to her to commit sin. She adorned herself, sat on her bed, and called him. He entered to her; he was chanting, saying: Though I walk in the midst of the shadows of death, I will not fear evil, for You are with me


[p. 252] {#po.9.p252}
ወዕበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፣ አንበረቶ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓራታ ። ወነጸረ ፣ ኅቤሃ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ለምንት ፡ አሕጸጽኪዮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወኃደጊ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወተመየጥኪ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ምግባር ፡ እኩይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ርዕደት ፡ ወአእመረት ፡ከመ ፡ ልባ ፡ ተመስወ ፣ ወአድነነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ወሰአለቶ፣ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትበኪ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሬእዮሙ ፡-ለሰይጣ”ና ት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትዋነዩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ገጽኪ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ እበኪ ፡ ላዕሌኪ ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወትቤ”ሎ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ መፍትው ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትነሣእ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እወ ። ወትቤ ሎ ፡ ንሥአኒ ፡ ወውስደኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘትፈቅድ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ንዒ ፣፡ ወተንሥአት ፡ ወተለወ ቶ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ።
When the saint had entered to her, she made him sit with her on her bed. He looked at her and said to her: "Why have you belittled Our Lord Christ, have you abandoned good works, and have you turned to these evil works?" When she had heard his words, she trembled, and perceived that her heart was failing. The saint bowed his head and wept. She asked him and said to him: "Why are you weeping?" He answered and said to her: "It is because I see the demons playing on your face. That is why I am weeping over you." She answered him and said to him: "Must I rise?" The saint said to her: "Yes." She said to him: "Take me and lead me where you will." He said to her: "Come." She rose and followed him. They went away and entered the desert.
ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ምሴት ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ኑሚ ፡ ባሕቲትኪ ፡ ወሰከበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርሑ ቀ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ጸሎቶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ከ መ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ ርእየ ፤ ራእየ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይበርህ ፣ ላዕሌሃ ፣ እምድር ፡ እስከ ፣ ሰማይ ፤ ወርአዮሙ ፣ ለመላእክተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ ይጸውሩ ፡፣ ነፍ ሳ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ ወረከባ ፡ በዘ ፡ አዕረፈት ። ወሰገደ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወ
When the evening hour arrived, he said to her: "Sleep alone." He lay down far from her, after he had finished his prayer. When he rose in the middle of the night to pray, he had a vision: a column of light shone upon her from the earth to the heaven. He saw the angels of the Lord carrying her soul. He rose and went toward her. He found her dead. He


[p. 253] {#po.9.p253}
ሰአለ ፡ እምኃበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቆ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ቃ ል ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ እስመ ፡ ንስሐሃ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ዘነስሐት ፡ ባቲ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እ ምእለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ንስሐ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ዓመታተ ፡ ወኢነስሑ ፡ በከመ ፡ ላህበ ፡ ንስሐ ሃ ። ወሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለአረጋዊያን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ኩሎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮ ነ ፡ አምኔሃ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወገነዝዋ ፡ ወቀበርዋ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይትወከፍ ፣ ንስሐነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ ንስሐሃ ፡ ወይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፣ አሜን ። ሰላም ፣ ለአትናስያ ፡ ለተሊወ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፌማ ፡=፡ በምክረ ፡ ሐጺር ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ከማ ፡፡ ሶበ ፡ መነነት ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ወገደፈት ፡ ትርሲተ ፡ ዘማ ፤ ነፍሳ ፡ ወሰዱ ፡ መሳእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐልዩ ፡ በዜማ ፡=፡
prostrated himself on the ground and asked the Lord to inform him about her. A word came to him, saying: "Behold, the (Lord) has accepted her repentance, at the moment when she repented much more than those who remain in penance for many years and do not repent to the measure of the fervor of her repentance." Saint John went away and told the holy elders everything that had happened to (Athanasia). The saints came, shrouded her, and buried her. May the Lord accept our repentance, as He accepted her repentance, and may He have mercy on us through her prayer! Amen. [Hail to Athanasia, when, to follow Christ the (wise) Artisan, On the advice of John the Little, She had repudiated the world, and rejected its courtesan ornaments! The angels took her soul, singing melodies. On the day (of the feast) of Daminä, martyr, she died without suffering.]
በዕለተ ፡ ደሚና ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ እምፃማ ።፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለደሚና ፡ ሰማዕት ። አግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፤ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈት ፣ ቅድስት ፣ ኢዮልራቅስያ ፡ ወዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፣
On this day also is the memory of Daminâ, martyr. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer! Amen. [On this day also died Saint Eupraxia. This saint was of the royal


[p. 254] {#po.9.p254}
አምዘመደ ፤ ነገሥት ፣ ይእቲ-። ወስሙ ፡ ለአቡሃ ፣ አጢጎኖስ ፣ እስመ ፡ መምክረ ፡ነገ ሥት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእማ ፣ ኢዮጳራቅስያ ። ወይገብሩ ፡ ጾመ ፣ ወጸሎተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦሙ ፡፣ ወልደ ። ወሶበ ፡ ወለድዋ ፡ ለዛቲ ፤ ቅድስት ፣ ሰመይ ዋ ፡ በስመ ፡ እማ፣ ኢዮጳራቅስያ ፤ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡሃ ፣ ወላሐውዎ ፡ ንጉሥኒ ፣ ወንግሥትኒ ። ወለቅድስትኒ ፤ ኢዮጳራቅስያ ፤ ወሰደታ ፡ እማ፣ ውስተ ፡ደብረ ፡ደናግል ፡እንዘ ፣ ሂዓመታ ።
family. The name of her father was Atigonos, for he was the counselor of kings. The name of her mother was Eupraxia. (Her parents) performed fasts and prayers to the Lord, so that He might give them a child. When they had begotten this saint, they called her by her mother's name: Eupraxia. A few days later, her father died. The king and the queen mourned him. Her mother led Saint Eupraxia to a convent of virgins, when she was seven years old.
ወዕበ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ስነ ፡ ጊሩቶን ፣ ለደናግል ፣ ትቤሎን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡፣ ትትቀነ ያ ፡ ከመዝ ። ወይቤላሃ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወትቤሎን ፡ አይቴ ፡ሀ ሎ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአርአያ፣ሃ ፡ ስአለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወበጺሓ ፡ ሰገደ ት ፡ ወሰአመት ፡ አገሪሁ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዲያቆናዊት ፡ ወሰአለት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመንኩሳነሃ ፡ ወነገርዋ ፣ ለእማ ፣ ወመጠወታ ፡ እማ ፡ ለዲያቆናዊት ፡ ወአማኅፀነታ ፡ እማ ፡ ለኩሎን ፡ ደናግል ፤ ወኃደገታ ፡ ወሖረት = ወእምድኅረ ፣ ኅዳጥ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ አዕረፈት ፣ ወኢዮ ልራቅስያ ፡ ለብሰት ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ትትጋደል ፣ ወትጸውም ፡
When she had seen the beauty of the piety of the virgins, she said to them: "Why do you practice (piety) thus?" They said to her: "It is for Christ - glory to Him!" She said to them: "Where is Christ?" They showed her the image of Our Lord Jesus Christ. Having come (near it), she adored it, and kissed its feet. She went to the deaconess, and asked her to initiate her into monasticism. They told (this) to her mother, and her mother gave her to the deaconess. Her mother entrusted her to all the virgins, left her, and went away. A few days later, she died. Eupraxia put on the monastic habits, and persevered in practicing asceticism, and in fasting every week.
በበሰሙን ። ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ይቀንዕ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፣ ወያሜክራ ፡ ወቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ይዌግራ ፣ ውስተ፣ማ ይ። ወቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ያቄስላ ፣፡ በጐድብ ፡ እንዘ ፣፡ ተሐጥብ ፡ ፅፀወ ፣ ወቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ከዓወ ፡
But Satan envied her, and tempted her. Sometimes, he threw her into the water; sometimes, he wounded her with (his) axe, while she was splitting wood; sometimes, he poured

on her boiling water. But nothing touched her, and she continued to serve the virgins and the sisters. Sometimes, they ordered her to make bread; sometimes, to draw water. She was not at all nonchalant in these tasks. She always slept on the ground. She spent every Lent standing, so that the virgins admired the strength of her asceticism. The Lord showed through her miracles and wonders, (consisting) in the healing of the sick. The demons departed; the lame walked; the blind saw, when she prayed over them. There was a virgin, called Julia. It was she who taught Eupraxia the Scriptures and the practices of monasticism. She was associated with all her works; they cherished each other much. When Saint Eupraxia had reached the age of forty, the deaconess had a vision, in which (the angels) were taking Eupraxia up into the apartment of the Queen, where the heavenly Bridegroom was, and (where there was) endless joy. Having awakened, she understood that the death of Eupraxia was approaching. The next day, she told it to the virgins. When they had gone to her, they found her suffering greatly from fever. They wept over her, and they asked her to pray over them. When she had prayed over them, she blessed them. She died, and was buried in the tomb of her mother. When Julia had prayed at her tomb,


[p. 256] {#po.9.p256}
እረፈት ፤ አመ ፡ ሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፤ ወተቀብረት ፤ ምስሌሃ ። እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይምሐረነ፤ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፤ አሜን ።
she died on the third day, and was buried with her. May the Lord have mercy on us for ages of ages! Amen.]
ሰላም ፣ ለኢዮጳራቅስያ ፡ እንተ ፤ አርትዓት ፡ ፍናወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተኃፍር ፣ በገድል ፡፤ ጽኑዓነ ፡ ኃይል ፣ ፅደወ ፡=፡ አመ ፡ አቀ'ሰላ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ እስከ ፤ ብዝኃ ፣ ደማ ፣ ተክዕወ ፡፡ እንበለ ፣ ታስተጋብእ ፡ እምተዘርዎ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኅጠበት ፡ ዕፀወ ፡፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰአላሃ ፣ አኃት ፡ ዐበየት ፡ አቲወ ፡
[Hail to Eupraxia, who went straight on (her) path, In order to confound by (her) asceticism, (she a woman), the men strong by energy! When the Enemy had wounded her, to the point that much of her blood was spilled, She did not want to return, when the sisters had prayed her, Before having gathered from (their) dispersion (the pieces of) wood that she
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፻ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
was splitting.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING OF THE 3 NAHASÉ (August 9)
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ክቡር ፣ ወጽሙድ ፣ ወመስተጋድል ፡ አባ ፣ ስምዖን ፡ ዘዓምድ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምደሴተ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ወሶዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፤ መዋዕሊ
On this day died the holy, illustrious, devout, ascetic Father Abba Simeon the Stylite. This saint was from the island of Syria. When he was seven years old, his


[p. 257] {#po.9.p257}
ሁ ፤ ሂዓመተ ፡ ረሰዮ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፅቀብ ፣ አባግዒሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ የሐውር ፣ ኀበ ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ዙሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ወይሰምዕ ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወሆኮ ፡ ጸ ጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፤፣ ወመንኩሰ ጦ፡፡ ወነበረ ፣ ይጸመድ ፡ ወይትጋ ደል ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ዓመታተ ፣ ወይጸውር ፣ መሬተ ፡ ወሐመደ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ርእሱ ፡ ወያመነድ ብ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ በጾም ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወበጽምእ ፡ ውጵሱክ ።
father put him to guard the sheep. He went continually to the church, and all the time he listened to the Holy Scriptures. The grace of the Lord shook him. He went to a convent, and became a monk. He continued to be devout, and to practice asceticism, for many years. He carried earth and ashes on his head. He mortified himself by a considerable fast and by repeated thirst.
ወእምዝ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሐቋሁ ፡ ሐብለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ፣፡ወ ኮነ ፡ ይወጽእ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዕፄ ፣ ወጹናሁ ፡ ጽዩእ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሐዘኑ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣፡ ኩሎ ሙ ፡ አኃው ፣፤ መነኮሳት ፡፣ በእንተ ፡፣ ጹናሁ ፡ ዘጽዩእ ፡ ወኩርህዎ ። ወተጋብኡ ፣ ኩሎ ሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢያውፃእኮ ፡ እምኅቤነ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ስ ምዖን ፣ ወእመአኮ ፡ ነኃድጎ ፣ ነሐውር ፡፤ ኩልነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አበ፣፤ ምኔት ፡ምንተ፣ ዘገብረ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፣ ጸውዖ ፡ ወርኢ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ወጸውዖ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ስምዖን ። ወሶበ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ደመ ፡ ድሙር ፡ ምስለ ፡ መግል ፡እ ንዘ ፣ ይውሕዝ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወአጸቦ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፣ ፈድፋደ ። ወከሠተ ፣ አልባ ሲሁ ፡ ወርእዮ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡፣ ሐብል ፡ ቦአ ፡ውስተ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፣ ወተቁቄጥዓ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፣ አ
Then he tied a rope to his loins, (so tightly) that it entered into his flesh, and worms and an extremely fetid odor came out of it. All the brother monks were sad about him, because of his fetid odor, and turned away from him. They all gathered before the superior of the convent, and said to him: "If you do not expel Simeon from among us, we will abandon him, and we will all leave." The superior of the convent said to them: "What has he done?" They said to him: "Call him, and see what he has done." The superior of the convent called Saint Abba Simeon. When he stood before him, he saw blood, mixed with pus, flowing onto his feet. This was extremely painful for the superior of the convent. He opened his clothes, and he saw the rope, (which) had entered into his flesh. The superior of the convent was indignant


[p. 258] {#po.9.p258}
በ፣ምኔት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፣ ደፈርከ ፣ ወገበርከ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ግብረ ፡ በፈቃድክ ። ወበ ጻማ ፡ ዓቢይ ፤ አውፅእዎ ፣ ለውእቱ ፣ ሐብል ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ መጠነ ፡ መዓልት ፡ እስከ ፣፡ አመ ፡ ተፈወሰ ፣ ፉ“ስላቲሁ ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ሖር ፡ ኀበ ፣ ዘፈቀድከ ። ወወፅአ ፡ እምኅቤሆሙ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ፤ ይቡስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴ ቱ ፡ምስለ ፤ አክይስት ፡ ወዓቃርብት ። ወርእየ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ራእየ ፣ በሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ሰደድኮ ፡ ለገብርየ ፣፡ ስምዖን ፡ ሕሥሦ ፣ ወሚጦ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ይቴይስ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ በፅለተ ፣፡ ደይን ። ወገሠጾ ፡ ተግሣጸ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ፀአቱ ፡ ለአ በባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ እምደብር ። ወበጽባሕ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፣ ለዙሎሙ ፣ አኃው ፡ዝ ከመ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ወዘከመ ፡ ገሠጽዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፣ ስምዖን ፣ ወደንገፁ ፤ አኃ ው ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ሐሩ ፡ ወሕትቱ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩ ሉ ፡ መካናት ፡ እስከ ፡ ትረክብዎ ፡ ወለእመ ፣ ኢረከብክምዎ ፡ ኢትትመየጡ ፡፣ ኀቤየ ። ወሖሩ ፡ ወሐተቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ መካናት ፣ ወኢረከብዎ ። ወ”እምድኅረዝ ፡ ሐሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ግብ ፡ ወአብርሁ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ወርእይዖ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አክይስት ፡ ወዓቃርብት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ። ወአውረዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አሕባ
against him, and said to him: "Why did you dare to do this thing of your own will?" With great pain they removed the rope from his flesh. (The monks) continued to give him care, for fifty days, until he was healed of his wounds. (Then) the superior of the convent said to him: "O my son Simeon, go wherever you wish." He left them, went away, and entered a dry well. He remained there with the snakes and the scorpions. The superior of the convent had a vision at night, as if someone were saying to him: "Why did you chase away my servant Simeon? Seek him, and bring him back, for he will be worth more than you on the day of judgment," and admonished him, with a great reprimand, about the departure of Abba Simeon from the convent. In the morning, the superior of the convent told all the brothers how he had had a vision, and how he had been admonished about Saint Abba Simeon. The brothers were terrified, and were sad enormously. The superior of the convent said to them: "Go, and search in all places, until you find him. If you do not find him, do not return to me." They went away, searched in many places, and did not find him. Finally, they went to a well, lit a lamp, and saw him sitting with the snakes and the scorpions, without food, nor drink. They let


[p. 259] {#po.9.p259}
ለ ፡ ወአውፅአዎ ፡ እምሀየ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፣ ሎቱ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ዘአበስነ ፡ ለከ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ኅድጉ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አበሳየ ፣ እስመ ፣ አነ ፡ አሕዘንኩ ክሙ ፡ ለኩልክሙ ፣ ወተዐደውኩ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፣ መምህርየ ፡ አበ ፡ ም”ኔት ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይነ ብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ግብ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፣ አውፅአዎ ፡ ኢበልዓ ፣ ወኢሰትየ ፣፡ ግሙ ራ = ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ደብር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጸመድ ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡፤ ብዙኃ ። ወሶ በ ፡ አክበርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ደብር ፣፤ ኢፈተወ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወኀደጎ ሙ፡ወቱኙየ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ። ወሖረ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ ፳መዓልተ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ንዋም ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወናዘዞ ፡ ወአይድዖ ፥ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓምድ ፣ ወኑጉጐ ፡ በአመት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይገብር፣ ተአምራተ ፣ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወፈውሰ ፣ ዓቢየ ። ወይሜህሮሙ ፣፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ይመጽ ኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ። ወአቡሁሰ ፣ ኅሠሦ ፣ ወኢረከቦ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይርአዮ ። ወእ ሙሰ ፡ እምድኅረ ፣ ብዙኅ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ ሰምዓት ፣ ዜናሁ ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ወበከየት ፡ ብካየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ኖመት ፡ ታሕ
down ropes to him, and they pulled him from there. They prostrated themselves before him, and said to him: "Forgive us for having sinned against you." Saint Abba Simeon said to them: "Forgive me my sin, for I myself have saddened you all, and I have transgressed the order of my master, the superior of the convent." While he remained in this well, until they had pulled him (out of it), he did not eat, and did not drink at all. They led him to the convent, and he remained (there), performing devotions, and practicing much asceticism. As they honored him in the convent, he did not seek the honors of this world. He abandoned them, and he fled (far) from them. He went away in secret. He reached a rock, and stood upon it, for 60 days, without sleeping. Then, the angel of the Lord came to him, consoled him, and announced to him that the Lord had called him for the salvation of the souls of many (people). Then, he stood, for 15 years, on a column. The height (of this column) was 30 cubits. He performed many miracles and great healings. He instructed all those who came to him. His father sought him, but he did not find him, and died before seeing him. His mother, many years later, learned of his fame. She came to him, while he was standing on the column; she shed many tears, then she fell asleep


[p. 260] {#po.9.p260}
ተ ፡ ዓምዱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወሰአለ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ”አምኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይ ግ”በር ፡ ምስሌሃ ፤ ሠናየ ፡ ወአዕረፈት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንውምት ፡ ይእቲ ። ወቀበርዋ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ዓምዱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ።
at the foot of the saint's column. The saint asked Our Lord Christ to make her happy. She died, while sleeping. They buried her at the foot of the saint's column.
ወቀንዓ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወዘበጦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እግሩ ፣ ዝብጠተ ፡ዕፅብተ ፡ ወደ ወየት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቀውም ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ እግሩ ፣ ብዙኃ ፣ ዓመታተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዓጸ የት ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ዕፄ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ዓምድ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ፈያታዊ ያን ፡ ወነስሐ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፣ ወኀሠሠ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣፤ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወተ ወክፈ ፡ ስአለቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወነበረ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በንስሐ ሁ ። ወሰአለ ፡ አምኅበ ፡ አግዚአነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአንቅቦዐ ፡ ነቅዓ ፡ ማይ ፣፡ ታሕተ ፡ ዓም ድ ፡ በእንተ ፣ አሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ዓጸቦሙ ፡ ለሰቲየ ፡ ማይ ።
Satan envied him, and struck him on one foot with a pernicious sore. (His foot) was sick. He continued to stand on one foot, for many years, until worms got into it, and the worms fell from it at the foot of the column. A chief of bandits came to him, and repented near him. (The saint) interceded with Our Lord Christ for him. (Our Lord) accepted the request of the saint. (The bandit) remained (there) a few days, and died in his penance. (The saint) asked Our Lord Christ to make a spring of water gush forth at the foot of the column for the people who came to him, since it was difficult for them (to find) water to drink.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክልእ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ ነዊኅ ፣፡ ወቆመ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ 8ዓመተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ጣሣወቿዓመት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትጋደል ፣ ወይጸመድ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ፣ እምድኅረ ፣ ሜጦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፣ ከሐዲያን ፡ ወአረማዊያን ፡
Then, he passed onto another high column, and stood there for 30 years. When 48 years had passed, during which he had practiced asceticism, and performed devotions, he emigrated to the Lord, whom he had loved. After he had converted many impious and pagans, that he had brought them into the faith of Our Lord Christ, that he had instructed many people, and had clothed them in the habits of monasticism, he became like an


[p. 261] {#po.9.p261}
ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። ወመሀሮሙ ፡፣ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብ እ ፡ ወአልበሶሙ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክተ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር። ወ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወወረሰ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት = ወሶዕበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ወመኳንንተ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፣ ወአብጽ ሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ በዝማሬ ፡ ወበማኅሌት ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክ ርስቲያን ። ወኮነ ፡ አምሥጋሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፤ ወመንክራት ፡፣ ብዙኃት ፣ ወፈውስ ፡ ዓቢ ይ.። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ፣
angel of the Lord, and died in peace. He inherited the kingdom of heaven. When the patriarch of the city of Antioch had learned that Saint Abba Simeon was dead, he took with him priests and dignitaries, and came to the body of the saint. They carried him with great honors, brought him to the city of Antioch with psalmodies and with chants, and deposited him in the Church. There occurred through his body many miracles and wonders and great healings. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing and his intercession be with us forever! Amen.
ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለስምዖን ፡ እንተ ፡ ለሐቃሁ ፡ አጽንዖ ፡=፡ በሐብለ ፡ በቀልት ፡ ዘይቡስ ፣ ዘይገምዶ ፡ ወይበልዖ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እምደብር ፡ ሶበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ አውፅኦ ፡፡ በቃለ" ተግሣጽ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አውሥኦ ፡፡
[Hail to Simeon, who had fortified his loins With a rope of dry palm, which had cut him and had gnawed him! Also, when the superior of the convent had chased him from the monastery, The Lord spoke to him with words of reprimand, Saying: "Make my servant Simeon return."]

[On this day also died Saint Sophia. This saint was of the family

ዘመደ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ብዕለ ፤ ወዋሪተ ፡ ወነገደት ፣ ብሔረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡-ምስለ ፡ አዋል ዲሃ ፡ ሠላስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣእ ፣ ማዕተቦ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ስብሐት ። ወአእሚሮ ፡እ ንድርያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ወአስተባጽሕዋ ፡ ምስለ ፣ አዋል ዲሃ ፡ ወተስአላ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሀገራ ፡ ወስማ ፣ ወትቤሎ ፣ ዘይቀድም ፡ ስምየ ፡ ክርስቲያና ዊት ፡ አነ ፡ ወዘሰመዩኒ ፡ አዝማድየ ፡ ሶፍያ ። ወጌርተ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ አነ ፣ በአጣልያ ፡ ወ በምሕረቱ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ አብጽሐኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ሀገርከ ፡ ከመ ፡ አቅርብ ፡ ውሉድየ ፣ መባአ ። ወአጽንዓቶን ፣፡ ለውሉዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይፍርሃ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ለመምዓሊ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸማ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ሠላስ ፡ አዋል ዲሃ ፡ ገነዘቶን ፣ ወቀበረቶን ፡ አፍአ ፡ አም”ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፣፡ ወበሰሉሶን ፡ ሖረት ፡ ከመ ፤ ትግበር ፡ ተዝካሮን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ አንስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወበጺሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መቃብሮን ፡ ሰገደት ፡-እንዘ ፤ ትጹሊ ፣ ወትብል ፣ ኦአዋልድየ ፣ ፍጹማት ፡ ወክሉላት ፡ በአክሊል ፡ ዘኢይማስን ፡ ንሥአ ፡ ኪያየኒ ፡ ምስሌክን ። ወዘንተ ፣ አምድኅረ ፡ ትቤ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ በሰላም ፣ ወተቀብረት ፣ ምስለ ፣ ደቂቃ ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለዓለ
of the emperors. She had riches and goods. She left for the land of Rome with her three daughters, in order to receive the sign of Christ - glory to Him! The emperor Hadrian, knowing that she was Christian, had her come with her daughters, and questioned her about her city and her name. She said to him: "My first name is: I am Christian. My parents named me Sophia. I am of an excellent family of Italy. In His mercy, Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - Himself made me come to your city, so that I might offer my children as an oblation." She encouraged her children, so that they would not fear the tortures of the tyrant. After her three daughters had finished their martyrdom, she shrouded them, and buried them outside the city of Rome. The day after next, she went to perform their memory with many women of the city. Having arrived at their tomb, she prostrated herself, praying and saying: "O my daughters, (you who are) perfect and crowned with a crown that will not corrupt, take me with you." After she had said that, she died in peace. She was buried with her children. May the Lord have mercy on us through her prayer for ages of ages! Amen.]

መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ አብዲራ ፡ እምነ ፡ ምድራዊት ፡ ንግሣ፣፤ ተዝካረ ፡ ደቂቃ ፡ ሠላስ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ሠላሳ ፡፡ ድኅረ ፡ ገብረት ፡ ዮም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ማዓሠሣ ፡፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተመጠወ ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ሰላም ፣ አብል ፡ ለመስተጋድል ፡ መርቆሬዎስ ፣ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ይቡስ ፡፡ ኀበ ፡ ይደፍንዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ አለበዎ ፡ መንፈስ ፡፡ ወሰዱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ሐዲስ ፡=፡
Having preferred the kingdom of heaven to earthly royalty! After she had performed memory today of her three children, and not of thirty, In order that He might fulfill her desire, The Lord received her soul.] [I say hail to the ascetic Margorêwos, (Mortifying himself) so much that his body became like dry wood! As the Spirit indicated where to bury him, They transported his body into a new sepulcher, On the back of three mules.]
ዲበ ፡ ዘባኖሙ ፡ ለቶራት ፡ ሠላስ ።፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
"አመ ፡ ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ። " በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ሕዝቅያስ ፡ ወልዶ ፡ አካዝ ፣ እምዘርአ ፣ ዳዊት፣
READING OF THE 4 NAHASÉ (August 10). On this day died King Hezekiah, son of Ahaz, of the posterity of David,


[p. 264] {#po.9.p264}
እምነገደ ፡ ይሁዳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘኢተሠይመ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፤ ንጉ ሥ ፡ ዘከማሁ ፤ እምድኅረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ። እስመ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ አምለኩ ፣፤ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎሙ ፤ ምሥዋዓተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ። እስ መ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፣፡ ሰበሮሙ ፣፡ ለጣዖታት ፣ ወአንሐለ ፡ ምሥዋዓቲሆሙ ፡ ወበ ተከ ፡ ከይሴ ፡ ዘብርት ፡ እስመ ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ አምለክዋ ። ወአሠዮ ፣ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ በመዋፅሊሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምሠናይ ።
of the tribe of Judah. There was no king like this just man, who was established over the children of Israel, after the prophet David. Indeed, all the kings of Israel adored idols and built altars for them, with the exception of this king. For he himself, when he reigned, broke the idols, demolished their altars, and destroyed the bronze serpent (that) the children of Israel adored. The Lord rewarded him, during the days (of his reign), beyond the good that he himself had accomplished.
ወአመ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሰናክሬም ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፣ ወአገታ፣ ለኢየሩሳሌም ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወኃያል ፤ ወጽኑዕ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ዘኢ ኮነ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ወኃያል ፤ ዘከማሁ ፤ ዘይፈርህሀዎ ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ወተ አዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ ሕዝቅያስ ፤ ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ንዋየ ፤፣ ወአምኃ ፡ብ ዙኃ ፡ ወኢሠምረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወኢነሥአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሰናክሬም ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሕዝቅያስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትቄጣፅ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወተካየዶ ፡ ኪዳናተ ፡ ጽልእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ው እቱ ፣ ያጠፍኦ ፡ ወያጠፍእ ፡ ሀገሮ ፡ ወጸረፈ ፡ በልሳኑ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወስቡሕ ። ወይቤ ፣ ኢይክል ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፣ ያድኅንክሙ ፣ እምእ ዴየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ኀበ ፤ ሕዝቅያስ ፣ 8፪መልእክተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጽሕፍ ፡ ውስቴቶ
The fourteenth year of his reign, Sennacherib, king of Persia (Assyria), came, and besieged Jerusalem. He was an extremely great, powerful, and strong king. (No king) in his time was strong and powerful like him. All the kings feared him, and obeyed him. Hezekiah feared him, and sent him silver and many gifts. But he did not accept them, and accepted nothing from him. Sennacherib sent (messengers) to Hezekiah, (saying to him) that he was indignant against him. He made threats of hostility to him, (saying to him) that he himself would annihilate him, and annihilate his city. He blasphemed in his impure tongue against the Most High and glorious Lord. He said: "The Lord will not be able to save you from my hand." Then, he sent to Hezekiah two letters, in which he wrote (words) of blasphemy


[p. 265] {#po.9.p265}
ሙ ፡ ጽርፈተ ፡ ወቀ'ጥዓ ። ወበከየ ፡ ሕዝቅያስ ፡ ወሰጠጠ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወለብሰ ፡ ሠ ቀ ፡ ጸኾር ፡ ወቦአ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብ ል ፤ አንተ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሰናክሬም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወላእከኒሁ ። ወ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ አጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፣ ዕፀው ፡ ወአእባን ። አላ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ እግዚኦ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክ ፡ አ
and indignation. Hezekiah wept, tore his clothes, put on a haircloth, entered the house of the Lord, and prayed before Him, saying: "You, O Lord, You know what Sennacherib, king of Persia (Assyria), and his ministers have said. Since this king is (impious), annihilate all the divinities of the earth, for they are wood and stones, but You alone, O Lord, You are the Lord God Almighty."
ኀዜ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ባ”ሕቲትክ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሕዝቅያስ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወአይድዖ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰናክሬም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወይስእሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወ ተሰጥዎ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፣ ነቢይ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አጽንዕ ፡ ልበከ ፡ ወ ኢትፍራህ ፣ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰናክሬም ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዕፁ በ ፡ ዘኢተሰምዓ ፡ ከማሁ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወረደ፡መ ልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ሚካኤል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወቀተለ ፡ እምትአይንቲሁ ፡ ለሰናክሬ ም ፡ ፲፳ወቿ፳ወ፻፻ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ሰዓት ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሁ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ሰራዊቶመ- ፡ ቅቱላን ፡ ጐዩ ፡ እለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ እምቅትለት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፤ ፋርስ ፣ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወቦአ ፡ ሰና ክሬም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አማልክቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡ ወቀተልዖ ፡ ወድኅነ ፡ ሕዝቅያስ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ወሰብሖ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
Then, Hezekiah sent a letter to the prophet Isaiah. He told him what Sennacherib, king of Persia (Ass.), had said, and asked him to pray for him. The prophet Isaiah answered him by the word of the Lord, and said (to him): "Strengthen your heart, and do not fear, for the Lord is to do against Sennacherib a wonderful thing, of which a similar one is unheard of in all the earth." That night, the angel of the Lord Michael descended from heaven, and killed of the army of Sennacherib 180,500 (men) in one hour. When those who remained of the massacre had awakened, (and) had found the army massacred, they fled toward the land of Persia (Ass.) with the king. Sennacherib entered the temple of his gods, in order to pray. His sons rose against him, and killed him. Hezekiah was saved from his hand, and glorified the Lord.


[p. 266] {#po.9.p266}
ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፣ ጊዜሁ ፡ ለሕዝቅያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሙት ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢ ይ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይደዊ፣ ደዌ ፡ ዕፁበ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፤ ”አዝዞሙ ፡ ለደቂቅከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትመውት ። ወጸለየ ፡ ሕዝቅያስ ፣ ቅድም ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኢ ሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፤ ዳግመ ፡ ወአይድዖ ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፲ወ፳ዓመ ተ ፡ ክልእተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኃሠሠ ፡ እምኢሳይያስ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ዝክቱ፣ ሜጠ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ፲መዓርገ ። ወፈርህዎ ፡ ለሕዝቅያስ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ነገሥት ፣ ወአብ ኡ።ሎቱ ፡ አምኃ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ፳ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ፃወ፪ ዓመት ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአፅረፈ ። ወሎቱ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ዘተናገረ ፡ በመ ንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተፈወሰ ፡ አምደዌሁ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡፤ ጽሕፍት ፣ ውስተ ፣ መጽሐፈ፣፤
When the time of Hezekiah's death approached, the prophet Isaiah came to him. (The king) was suffering from a grave illness. (Isaiah) said to him: "Give instructions (for your last will) to your sons, for behold, you yourself are going to die." Hezekiah prayed before the Lord. The prophet Isaiah sent (a messenger) to him again, and informed him that the Lord would add 15 more years to his days. When he had asked Isaiah for the sign of this (thing), he caused the sun (dial) to recede for him by 10 degrees. All the kings feared Hezekiah and offered him gifts, for they knew that the Lord was with him. He remained in his kingship for 29 years. All the days of his life were 54 years. || pleased the Lord, and he died. (He is the author) of the prayer which he uttered by the Holy Spirit when he was healed of his illness. It is written in the Book of Psalms of David. May the Lord have mercy on us through the prayer of his saints! Amen.
መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፤ ለሕዝቅያስ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ጸለየ ፡ በሀቁ ፡ ዘዝተወሰከ ፡ ዘመኑ ፡ ወፈድፈዴድ ፡ እምነ ፡ ጐልቁ ።፡
[Salutation to Hezekiah! When he had prayed with devotion, A period (of years) was added to him, exceeding the (fixed) number.]


[p. 267] {#po.9.p267}
አመኒ ፡ ተአየርዋ ፡ ለጽድቁ ፡፡ በእደ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወድ”ቁ ፡፡ ወለመልአኮሙ ፡ ቀተልዖ ፡ ደቁ ።
[When they had outraged the holiness (of the Lord), The armies of Persia (Assyria) fell under the hand of the angel, And the sons (of Sennacherib) killed their prince.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፣ ሰማዕታት ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ወአኃዊሁ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ሰንጋር ፡ በደቡበ ፣፡ ግብጽ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለዳዊት ፣ ወለአኃዊሁ ፡ ማኅበር ፡፡ እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ሰንጋር ፡፡ ሣሀለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ይክዓው ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡፡ ጠለ ፡ ሣሀሉሰ ፡ ለአምላክ ፡ ቴር ።፡
On this day also are the holy martyrs David and his brothers, in the city of Sangar, in the north of Egypt. May the Lord have mercy on us through their prayer! Amen. [Salutation to David and his brothers, (his) companions, Who were martyrs in the land of Sangar! May they spread the clemency of Christ upon this city, (For) where the dew of the clemency of the Good God Does not fall, the region is arid!]
ኀበ ፡ ኢይዘንም ፡ ይየብስ ፡ ብሔር ፣ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፣ ማቴዎስ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ገዳመ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይለብስ ፡ ሠቀ ፡ ፀኾር ፡ ከመ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አአመኖ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለመርምህናም ፣ ወለሳራሂ ፣ አንጽሐ ፣ እምለምጻ ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐ ረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ "ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
[On this day also died Abba Matthew. This saint remained in the desert, being clothed in a hair shirt like a sheep. He himself brought to believe in Christ - glory to Him! - Marmehnâm and Sara, whom he purified of his leprosy. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for ever and ever! Amen.


[p. 268] {#po.9.p268}
ሰላም ፣ ለማቴዎስ ፡ ነቢረ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘአንኃ ፡ አምሳለ ፡በግዕ ፡ ጸጐረ ፡ እስከ ፣ ተሞጥሐ ፡፡ ከመ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ጽድቆ ፣ ወተአምሪሁ ፡ ስቡሐ ፡፡ ሐፀበ ፡ በማየ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ አባለ ፡ መርምህናም ፡ ርሱሐ ፡፡ ወአባለ ፡ ሳራ ፣ አምለምጽ ፡ በሀየ ፡ አንጽሐ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
[Salutation to Matthew, who remained a long time in the desert, To the point of being clothed in hair, like a sheep! In order to show his holiness and his glorious miracles, He washed with the water of baptism the sordid body of Marmehnâm, And thereby he purified the body of Sara of leprosy.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ፳ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 5TH OF NAHASÉ (August 11).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡፤ ሐራ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱ ስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትዕይንቱ ፡ ለሉልያ ኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሐዲ ። ወፈነዎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ካልአን ፡ ሐ ራ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመንድቦሙ ፡ ለሐዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መክን ፡ ዘየሐው ር ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አብያጺሁ ፡ ሐራ ፣ ይከሥት ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከመ፣
On this day died the holy ascetic John, the soldier. The parents of this saint were Christians. He himself was a soldier in the army of Loulyânos (Julian), the impious king. This impious king sent him (on service) along with other soldiers, and ordered him to persecute the Christians in every place where he would go. He himself declared before his companions, the soldiers, that he hated the Christians,


[p. 269] {#po.9.p269}
ውእቱ ፡ ይጸልኦሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ወይጸብኦሙ ።፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ይፃብእ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ወይገብር ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሠናየ ፣ ወይሁቦሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘይፈቅዱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ወ ትረ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ ወይጹሊ ፡ ወይሁብ ፡ ምጽዋተ ። ወሐይወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕይወቶሙ ፡ ለጻ ድቃን ። ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ እመቃብሪሁ ፡ ተአ ምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
and that he would make war on them. But, in secret, he fought for them, did them good, and gave them everything they wanted. Continually, he fasted, prayed, and gave alms. He led the life of the righteous. He pleased the Lord, and died. Many miracles appeared at his tomb. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us! Amen.
አሜን። ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡፤ ለዮሐንስ ፣ ሐራዊ ፡፡ እምአብያጺሁ ፡ ዘኀብአ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ።፡ ወለምአእመናን ፡ ያሜኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይቀትል ፡ ወያደዊ ፡፡ ጸውዖ ፡ እንበይነዝ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማኅየዊ ፡፡
[I say salutation to John, the soldier, Who hid from his companions (his) Christian practices! He did good to the faithful, while (seeming) to kill them and make them suffer. That is why the life-giving Lord called him: May the weddings of the heavens be with this bridegroom!]

will. On the seventh (night), while he was sitting on his bed, the grace of the Lord shook him. He left the bridal house. While a light was leading him, he found an empty house. He lived there, walled up the door, and left only a small window, through which he received food. Ten years after he had left the world, his father and mother died, and left him a considerable fortune. He distributed it to the poor and the needy. As for him, he persevered in prayer and frequent fasting. He possessed nothing, except a cloak, a hair shirt, a bowl, and a mat. He practiced asceticism thus for 50 years. Pagans, small and great, were in a city, and no one could convert them to the right faith
ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡፣ ጳጳስ ፣ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ ወጥበቦ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ አንበ ሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ቀሲሷሰ ፡ ወያአምኖሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ አረሚ ፣ ወእምፅፁብ ፡ ሜሞ ፣ ቀሲ ሰ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወገብረ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚጦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ዚአሁ ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣፡ ጣዖቶሙ ፣ ወአውደቆሙ ፡፣ እመናበ ርቲሆሙ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ መልኡ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፡ በሀቁ ፡ ወሰደድ ዎ ፡ እምሀነር ፡ወክዊኖ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጹሊ ፡
One day, the bishop, having remembered the faith and wisdom of Abba Abraham, imposed upon him to become a priest, and made him convert these pagans. With difficulty he ordained him priest. He sent him there. Having arrived at the city (of the pagans), he built a church, and performed the office there. He asked the Lord to convert them to His own knowledge. One day, he entered the temple of their idols, and made them fall from their place. The people of the city, seeing (this), were filled with anger; they struck him much, and expelled him from the city. Night having arrived, they found him in the church, praying. Again,

they tied him with ropes, and dragged him out of the city. They threw stones at him, left him half dead, and departed. After he had risen by the strength of the Lord, they found him praying for their conversion. He remained thus for 10 years, while they tied him and dragged him. When the Lord had seen his perseverance, he converted the heart (of the pagans) to His own faith. They were baptized in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, from the smallest to the greatest, and they were strong in the faith of Christ. Abba Abraham, seeing the strength of their faith, feared that they might attribute vain glory to himself. " prayed to the Lord, signed the city with the sign of the cross, departed, without anyone knowing, and went to another city. Satan, the enemy of good, fought against him with numerous frightening phantoms, but the saint defeated him by the strength of his prayer. He continued to practice asceticism for many years, with great devotion. A day did not pass for him without tears. He did not laugh with his teeth; he did not smile with his lips; he did not anoint his body with oil; he did not wash his face and feet with water; he meditated, as if he were going to die that very day. When his brother according to the flesh died, he left a daughter of 7 years. She


[p. 272] {#po.9.p272}
ወሰድዋ ፣ አዝማዲነሃ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፣ አብርሃም ፣ ወተማኅፀነት ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜ ህራ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ወጾም ፡ ”ወአንብዕ ፡ ወአትሕቶ ፡ርእስ፣ እስከ ፡ ኮና ፡ ፳ዓመተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቀንዓ ፣ ሰይጣን ፣ ላዕሌሃ ፣ ወአስተፋቀራ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፳መነኮስ ፡ እስከ ፡ አማሰነት ፡ ድንግልናሃ ፣ ወወለጠት ፡፣ አልባሲሃ ፣ ወአስኬማሃ ፣ ወሖ ረት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ክልእ ፡ ሀገር ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ርእየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ዓ ቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይውኅጥ ፡ ርግበ ፣ ወጐንድዮ ፡ ኅዳጠ ፣፡ ቄዓ ፣ ታሕተ ፡፣ እገሪሁ = ወበ ሳኒታ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ተኅጥአት ፡ ማርያ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካና ፡ ደንገፀ ፡ አባ ፣ አብርሃም ፡ ወጸለየ ፤ በአንብፅ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፣ ያስተራክቦ ፡ ኪያሃ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፣ ኅበ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ወለጠ ፡ አስኬማሁ ፡ ወገልበበ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ከመ፣ ኢ ያእምርዎ ፡ ወተፅዕነ ፡ ፈረሶ ፡ ወለብሰ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሐራ ።
was called Mary. Her parents brought her to Abba Abraham. She was entrusted to him. (He began) to teach her the fear of the Lord with prayer, fasting, tears, and humility, until she was 20 years old. Then, Satan exercised (his) zeal against her. He inflamed her with love for a monk, so that she lost her virginity, changed her (monastic) clothes and her veil, and went off to another city. That night, the saint had a vision. A large serpent was devouring a dove, and shortly after he vomited it at his feet. The next day, as Mary was missing from her place, Abba Abraham was terrified. He asked the Lord with tears to let him find her. A few days later, when he had learned where she was, he changed his (monk's) habit, covered his face so that he would not be recognized, mounted his horse, and put on a soldier's habit.
ወሖረ፣ ውስተ ፡ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ህየ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሠለወት ፡ ማርያ ወሶ በ ፡ ርእያ ፡ በአልባሰ ፡ መያሲ ፡ ተከዘ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኃደገት ፡ ትሀርምታ ። ወባሕ ቱ ፡ ኢተአምኃ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትኾየዮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ልበዓለ ፣ ቤት ፡ 8ዲና ረ ፡ ዘአምጽአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያስተዳሉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መብልዐ ፡ ወመስቴ ፣ በዘይትፌሣ ሕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ማርያ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመስላ ፡ ዘይፈቅድ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ኅጢአት ። ወእም
He went to the city (in question). Having arrived there, he entered where Mary was. When he had seen her, (decked out) in the clothes of a tavern-keeper, he was very saddened that she had abandoned her devotion. However, he did not greet her, so that she would not flee (far) from him. Then he gave the master of the house a denarius, which he had brought with him, so that he would prepare food and drink for him, to rejoice with Mary, while it seemed to (her) that what he wanted from her was an act of sin. After supper, she introduced him


[p. 273] {#po.9.p273}
ድኅረ ፡ ድራር ፡ አብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ውሣጤ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወአፀወ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ ወአኃዛ ፡እዴሃ ። ወተርእያ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ወሶበ ፣ አእመረቶ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ከመ ፣ በድን ። ይቤላ ፣ ኢትፍርሂ ፡ኦ ወለትየ ፣ እስመ ፡ ኃጢአትኪ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ዘእንበ ለ ፡ በሕቲቱ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ንዒ ፣ ተመየጢ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፣ ሥርዓትኪ ፡፣ ወትቤ ሎ ፡ አሆ ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፡ ነሥኣ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ ወአጽዓና ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፈረሱ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ የሐስ ል ፤ ላቲ። ወሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘማኅረከ ፡ ወለቶ ፡ እምእደ ፣፤ ሰይጣ ን ፣ ወበጺሖ ፤ ውስተ ፣ መካና ፡ ወአፀዋ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፤ወነበረት ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ትኔስሕ ፡ በለቢሰ ፣ ሠቅ ፡ ወትህርምት ፡ ወአትሕቶ ፡ ርእስ ፡ እስከ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰም ዑ ፡ገዐራ ። ወአርአየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕለ ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ወአውዕኦ ፡ አጋንንት ፣ ወፈውሶ ፣ ድውያን ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፲ዓመት ፡ ርእዮ ፡፤ ንስሐ ሃ ፣ አዕረፈ ፣ አባ ፣ አብርሃም ፣ በጽክረማት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሴብሖ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወነ በረት ፡ ማርያ ፡ ካልእተ ፣ ፳ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታፈደፍድ ፡ ትሀርምተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንብዕ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ወእለ ፡ ርእይዋ ፡ ወሀቡ ፣ ስብሐተ ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብዘኃ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ገጻ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ " ይምሐረነ ፡
into the interior of the house. He closed the door, took her by the hand, and revealed himself to her. When she had recognized him, she became like a corpse. He said to her: "Do not fear, O my daughter, for your sin will be upon me. No man is without sin, except the Lord alone. Come; return to your former way of life." She said to him: "Yes." The next day, he took her with him, and made her mount his horse. He himself led her. He went, rejoicing that he had snatched his daughter from the hand of Satan. Having arrived at the place (where he lived), he locked her in (his) house. She persevered in doing penance by wearing the hair shirt, devotion, and humility, to the point that all those who heard her groans were astonished. The Lord made miracles and wonders appear through her: the expulsion of demons and the healing of the sick. Ten years after he had seen the penance (of Mary), Abba Abraham died, (aged) 70 years, glorifying the Lord. Mary persevered for five more years in increasing (her) devotion with tears, day and night. Then, she died. Those who saw her gave glory to the Lord because of the intensity of the light that was on her face. May the Lord have mercy on us through the prayer (of Abba Abraham) for ever and ever!]
በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ሰላም ፣ ለአብርሃም ፡ ዘተሰደ ፣ ወተዘብጠ ፡፡

Until he had converted the pagans from perdition and infidelity! He snatched the soul of his captive daughter from the jaws of the serpent. Indeed, he did not grow weary of (divine) service and perfect asceticism; Fifty years were for him but a few days.]

[Salutation to Philip, who was able to make cross The man who had called him the waves of the torrent (of the season) of rains! While those who observe his rule, and who walk in his way, Recount his power and praise his work, (I say) salutation, salutation to his good sons.]

At the moment when I was placed back on the earth, filled with darkness, From the bottom of my heart, he said, I wept bitterly."] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ሄለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 6TH OF NAHASÉ (August 12).
በዛቲ፣ ዕለት ፡ ኮነት ፡ቅድስት ፣ ኢየሉጣ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዛቲ ፡ መስተጋድልት ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቂሳርያ ፣ ዘቀጳዶቅያ ፣ ወወረሰት ፡ እምአበዊ፣ሃ ፡ ንዋየ ፣ ብዙኃ ። ወዓመፃ ፡ ፳ዓማቂ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ ንዋያ ፡ ወጥሪታ ፡ ወአግብርቲሃ ፡ ወአእማቲሂ ፡ በሕልያን። ወዕበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዓማዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ትፈቅድ ፣ ታስተዋድዮ ፡ ወትክሥ ት ፡ዓመፃሁ ፡ወቃሎ ፡ ዘሐሰት ፡ አስተዋደያ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅበ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶ ቅያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ። ወትቤ ፡ በነፍሳ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዝ"ንቱ ፡ ዓለ ም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ዓመፁኒ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ በግ ፍዕ ። ወለእመ ፡ አነ ፡ አጥረይኩ ፣ እሎንተ ፣ መኃድረ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ መኑሂ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥኦሙ ፡ እምኔየ ። ሽ
On this day is Saint Juliet, martyr. This ascetic was from the city of Caesarea of Cappadocia. She inherited much wealth from her parents. A dishonest man acted iniquitously toward her, and took all her wealth, her fortune, her servants, and her handmaids by a bribe (given to the judge). When this dishonest (man) knew that the saint wanted to accuse him and unveil his iniquity and his lying words, he himself accused her before the judge of the city of Cappadocia, (saying) that she was a Christian. She said to herself: "The goods of this passing world are nothing; behold, one has acted iniquitously toward me, and has taken them (from me) by force. If I myself acquire the dwellings that are in heaven, no one will be able to take them from me."


[p. 276] {#po.9.p276}
ወዕበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወቆመት ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡፣ ወተአምነት ፡ ወትቤ ፡ አነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ገሃደ ፡ አአምን ፡ በእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወልደ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ወ”ምድር ፡ ወኩሉ ፣ ዘውስቴቶሙ ፤ ወተም ዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ወወገርዋ ፣ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወመጠወት ፡ ነፍሳ ፡ ውስተ ፡እ ደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወኢለከፈት ፡-እሳት ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምሥጋሃ ፡ ግሙራ። አላ ፣ አውፅእዋ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይወፅእ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ማይ ። ወነሥአት-.፡ ሀ የንተ ፡ ንዋያ ፡ ወጥሪታ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ዘአልቦ ፣ ማኅለቅተ ። ወወደሳ-፣ቅ ዱስ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፣ ብዙኃ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፣ ወበረከታ ፡፣ የሀሉ፣
When she had arrived before the judge, and had stood before him, she confessed (her faith), and said: "I am a Christian. Publicly, I confess my Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living Lord, creator of the heavens, of the earth, and of all that is in them." The judge became angry, and ordered (her to be burned). She was thrown into the fire, and she rendered her soul into the hand of the Lord. The fire did not touch her body at all, but she was brought out of the fire, as if she were coming out of water. She received in exchange for her goods and her fortune the eternal kingdom, which has no end. Saint Basil praised her much. May the Lord have mercy on us through her prayer, and may her blessing be with us! Amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለኢየሉጣ ፡ እንበይነ ፣ ምግባራ ፡ ሠናይ ፡፡ አመ ፤ ውስተ ፦ እቶን ፣ አብእዋ ፡ በውዴተ ፡ ብእሷ ፡ እኩይ ፡=፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢሀሎ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፣ ዓሠረ ፡ ዋዕይ ።፡ መፅአት ፡እምአሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ዘወፅአ ፣ እማይ ፡፡ አምሳለ ፡ ፍቱን ፡ ወርቅ ፣ ወብሩር ፣ ጽሩይ ፡፡
[Salutation to Juliet because of her good works! When she had been made to enter the furnace, upon the accusation of a wicked man, While there were no traces of burning on her, She came out of the fire, as if she were coming out of water, Like gold tested and silver purified.]


[p. 277] {#po.9.p277}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ጽሙድ ፡ ወመስተጋድል፣ አባ ፡ ዊፃ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አባ ፣ ሲኖዳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅ ዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፣ ብዙኃ ፣ ዓመታተ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ይትኤዘዝ ፣ ሎቱ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፣፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፣፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፣ ብዙኃተ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እ ምነ ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ሎቱ ፣፡ ስብሐት ። ወውእቱ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ለ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ወዘከመ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ሑ ረቶ ፣ ሠናይተ ፤ ወአስመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡
On this day also died the holy, illustrious, devout, and ascetic Father Abba Wisâ, disciple of the great saint Abba Sinodâ. This saint remained with Saint Abba Sinodâ for many years, while he obeyed him. Saint Abba Sinodâ revealed many holy mysteries to him, as he himself (had) learned them from Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! He wrote the combat of Saint Abba Sinodâ, according to (what) he had seen, and according to (what) he had learned from him. When he had finished his good race, he pleased the Lord, and died in peace. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer! Amen.
ይምሐረነ ፣ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለአባ ፣ ዊጻ ፣ አርአያ ፣ ገብር ፣ ተመሲሎ ።፡ ረድአ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፡ዘኮነ ፡ አመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ሀሎ ፡=፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትኤዘዝ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ በኢተሀይዮ ፣ ወኢተአንትሎ ፡፡ ወአመ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ገድሎ ፡፡
[Salutation to Abba Wisâ, who, resembling the type of the servant, Was the disciple of Sinodâ, when he was in the desert, While he obeyed him without negligence and without impatience! When he was dead, he wrote his combat, According to what he had been taught by him, and had retained everything.]
ከመ፣ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ተምሀረ ፡ ወአጽንዓ ፣፡ ዙሎ ፡ገ ። ሰላም ፣ ለቅዳሴ ፣ ቤትከ ፣ ወፍልሰትከ ፡ ኅቡር ።
[Salutation to the dedication of your church, and jointly to the translation (of your body),]


[p. 278] {#po.9.p278}
አመ ፡ ፪ዘኮነ ፡ ለወርኃ ፡ ማአረር። ሄሮዳ ፡ መዋዒ ፡ብሰይፈ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሕፁር ። ኢክህሉ ፣፡ ሥሒጦተከ ፡ በአስናን ፡ ወፅፍር ። አራዊተ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወነምር ።
When it took place on the 6th of the month of harvest, Heroda, victor with the short sword of Michael! The wild beasts, the lions and the tigers, were not able to wound you with (their) teeth and (their) claws!]
ሰላም ፡ ለኢዮልያ ፡ ዘተለወታ ፡ መዊተ ። ለእንተ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ኅረየት ፡ ሕይወተ ። አፅረፈት ፣ዮም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ዘንተ ። ኢዮጳራቅስያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጸለየት ፡ ጸሎተ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸውዓኒ ፡ ሊተ ።
[Hail to Juliet, who followed in death her who once had chosen life with her! She died today, saying these (words), in accordance with the prayer that Eupraxia was making: "Lord, call me."]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ተክለ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ ሐዋርያዊ ፡፣ ሰማዕ ት ፡ ወተቀትለ ፡ በእደ ፡ አረሚ ። በረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሴነ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died Saint Takla-Iyasus the Apostolic, martyr. He was killed by the hand of the pagans. May his blessing be with us! Amen.]
ሰላም ፣ እብል ፣ መንፈሳዌ ፣፡ መምህረ ፡፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ሕዝቡ ፡ዘዖረ ፡= መስቀሎ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ሶበ ፡ ተዘከረ ፡፡
[I say hail to the spiritual Doctor, who bore the sin of his people! When he remembered the cross of the Son,


[p. 279] {#po.9.p279}
ተክለ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ በአብን ፦ ዐፅመ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ሰበረ ።፡ ወበጋጋ ፣-ሐዩዒን ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሐቃሁ ፡ አሰረ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
Takla-Iyasus broke one of his ribs with a stone, and also girded his loins with an iron girdle.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 7TH OF NAHASÉ (August 13).
አመ ፡ ጻለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፣ ዓቢየ ፡፣ ገብርኤል ፡ ወዜነዖ ፡ ለ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፣ ብእሲትከ ፡ ሐና ፡ ትፀንስ ፣ ወትወልድ ፡ለከ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወመድኃኒተ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወውእቱኬ ፣ በእ ንተ ፣ እግዝእትነ ፣ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ በሥጋ ። እስ መ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሐና ፡ ብአሲቱ ፡ ልሀቁ ፡ በመዋዕሊ ሆሙ ፡ ወኢወለዱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐና ፡ ኮነት ፡ መካነ ። ወበእንትዝ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ የሐዝ ኑ ፡ ፈድ”ፋደ ፣ እስመ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይትዓየርዎሙ ፡ ለአለ ፣ ኢወለዱ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ኦዘኃጣእከ ፣ በረከተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወበእንተ ፣ ሐዘነ ፡ ልቦ
On this day the Lord sent his great angel Gabriel. He announced to Joachim the Just, and said to him: "Behold, your wife Anne shall conceive, and shall bear you a child, through (whom) the joy and salvation of the whole world shall come about." This was therefore concerning Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, Mother of our God according to the flesh. Indeed, the just Joachim and his wife Anne were advanced in their days and had not begotten a child, for Anne was barren. That is why they were extremely sad, for the children of Israel despised those who did not beget a child, and said to him: "O you, who are deprived of the blessing of the Lord." Because of the sadness of their heart, this just man and his wife fasted continually, prayed, and besought the Lord,
ሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፣፡ ወለብእሲቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይጸውሙ ፡ ወይጹልዩ ፡ ወያስተበ ቶፉዑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሌሊተ ፣ ወመዓልተ ። ወበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ርሥዕና ፣ ወልህ ቁ ፣ወአእሙንቱ ፡ ወትረ ፡ የኃሥሠ ፡ ወይስእሉ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአብፅዑ ፣ ሎ ቱ ፡ ብፅዓተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘወለዱ ፡ ይረስይዎ ፡ ላእከ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።
night and day. They had reached senility and old age. They implored continually, prayed to the Lord, and made a vow to him that the child they would beget, they would place as a minister in the temple of the Lord.


[p. 280] {#po.9.p280}
ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፣ ይጹሊ ፣ ወያስተበቀ'ዕ ፡ ወ ትረ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ድቃስ ፡ ወኖመ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐና ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ሀለዋ ፡ ትፀንስ ፡ ወትወልድ ፡ ወል ደ ፡ ዘይትፌሣሕ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡፣ ወይትሐሠዩ ፡ በእን ቲአሁ ፣ ወይድኅኑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶበ ፣ ነቅሃ ፣ እምንዋሙ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፡ ሐና ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ”ወአምንዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ወፀንሰት ፡ እምውእ ቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወወለደታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወተመክሐት ፡ ላ ፅለ ፡ ኩሎን ፡ አንስተ ፡ ዓለም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀ ሉ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለፅንሰትኪ ፡ ፅንስ ፣ መንፈሳዊ ፡፡
While Joachim the Just was on the mountain (of the temple), praying and beseeching continually, behold, sleep descended upon him, and he slept. The angel of the Lord Gabriel appeared to him, and announced to him that his wife Anne would conceive, and would bear a child through whom he would rejoice, and concerning whom likewise the whole world would rejoice and exult, and through (whom) it would be saved. When he had awakened from his sleep, he came to his house, and told his wife Anne what he had seen. They believed in this vision. She conceived from that moment and bore Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary. She was glorified (far) above all the women of the world. May the Lord have mercy on us through her prayer, and may her blessing be with us! Amen. [Hail to your conception, spiritual conception,


[p. 281] {#po.9.p281}
ዘኢይትኃበሮ ፡ ስዑብ ፡ ወኢይትኃወሎ ፡ ዘማዊ ፡፡ ለውዳሴኪ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እንተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እጻሙ ፣ ኃረሳዊ ፡= ገራህተ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ዝናመ ፡ ጽድቅኪ ፡ ያርዊ ፡፡ ወኅጠተ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ከፅበ፡ ጊሩትኪ ፡ ያስዊ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ በዓለ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፣ ክቡር ፣ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ርእሰ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡እ ስመ ፡ በከመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተአመነ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ አርድአት ፡፣ ወይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ዕበ ፣ አውፅኦሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ "ቂሳር ያ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወተስእሎሙ ፡፣ ምንተ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ በእንቲአሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ወአልባበ ፡ ሰብእ ። አላ ፡አ ውፅአኦሙ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ባሕቲቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትከሀሎሙ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ ዘሐለዩ ፡ በ ውስተ ፡ ልቦሙ ፣ በእንቲአሁ ። እስመ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ናፈቁ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በበይና ቲሆሙ ፡ ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እለ፣ ይቤሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኤልያስ ። ወቦ ፡ ይቤሉ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፳እምነቢያት ፣ ወተምዓ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ። ወሶበ ፡፣ አአመረ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ዘ
In which the defiled did not participate, and with which the debauched was not associated! At the moment when, (I) a plowman, I labor for your praise, Mary, May your justice rain upon the field of my heart, And may your goodness also ripen the seeds of my word!] On this day also is the feast of the illustrious apostle Peter, head of the apostles. Indeed, it is on this day that he confessed in the midst of the disciples, and that he said that Christ was the Son of the Lord. When Our Lord had taken them outside the city of Caesarea, as the holy Gospel says, he asked them: What do men say concerning (the Christ)? Indeed, he himself knew all the thoughts and (all) the feelings of men. But he took them alone outside the city, so that it might be possible for them to express what they thought in their heart concerning him. Indeed, the apostles were divided among themselves concerning him. Some of them said that he was Elijah. Others said that he was one of the prophets. Peter became angry with them, and said to them: "But the Christ is the Son of the living Lord." When Our Lord
