had known this, Our Lord took them alone outside the city, and asked them: What do men say concerning me? They said (the thought) that was in their soul: Some say that you are Elijah; others say that you are one of the prophets. Our Lord said to Peter: But you, what do you say? || confessed, as he had spoken to the apostles, while they were alone, and he said: You are the Christ the Son of the living Lord. That is why Our Lord declared him blessed and gave him the keys of the kingdom of heaven, in order to loose and to bind. From that day, the apostle Peter became the head of all the apostles, and he held the place of Our Lord in the city of Rome. He is established over all the princes of the world, the patriarchs, the metropolitans, the bishops, the priests, the deacons, and all the priestly clergy. Glory to Our Lord Jesus Christ for ever and ever! Amen.
ክህነት ። ወስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ስዩም ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣ -ወካህናተ ፣ ዙሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡=፡ ዕበ ፡ ልክርስቶስ ፡ አምኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዮም ፡=፡ ኢከሠተ ፡ ለከ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ወደም፤ እንበለ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ እምቅድም ።
[I say hail to Peter, established (prince) Of the apostles and priests of the whole world! When he had confessed today that Christ was the Son of God: "It is not flesh and blood that revealed it to you," (Jesus) said to him, "But my heavenly Father, who exists from eternity."]


[p. 283] {#po.9.p283}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡፣ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘሀገ ረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወኒ ። ለዝ ንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኃረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተሰይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስ ክንድርያ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ እፅረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ። ወበጽሖ ፡ ምንዳ ቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴተ ፡ ጋግራ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዝእምቅድሜሁ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ስደት ፣ ዓመተ ። ወእም
On this day died the holy Father Abba Timothy, patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He is of the number of the Fathers the patriarchs the twenty-seventh. The Lord chose this saint. He was instituted patriarch of the city of Alexandria, after the death of Father Dioscorus, the ascetic. A great persecution came upon him. He was expelled to the island of Gangra, where they had expelled Saint Dioscorus, the patriarch who was before him. || remained in exile for 7 years. Then King Leo sent (a rescript) and brought him out of exile, in great glory
ዝ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ልዮን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሜጦ ፡ እምስደት ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መርቅያን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጫመ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሊቀ ፣ ጳጳሳት። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ መርቅያን ፣ ተንሥኡ ፡ ሰብኦ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሊ ቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣፡ ዘሜሞ ፡ መርቅያን ፣ ወቀተልዎ ፣ ወሜምዎ ፣ ዲቤሆሙ ፣ ለጢሞቴዎስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተአዘዘ ፡ ለአብ ፣ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ልዮን ፣ ወልደ ፡ መርቅያን ፡ተ ሰይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘይብልዎ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ። ወለአብ ሰ፣ሊቀ ፡ጸጳጳሳት ፡ ሰደዶ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴተ ፣ ጋግራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ለ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ፲ዓመተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ልዮን ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ዘይኑን ፡ ጐየ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ አላዊ ፡ እምእስክንድርያ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፣ አብ ፣ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ።
[Then, King Marcian instituted a patriarch in (Alexandria). After Marcian was dead, the people of the city of Alexandria rose up against the patriarch whom Marcian had instituted, and killed him. They put Timothy at their head, because he had been faithful to Father Dioscorus. When Leo, son of Marcian, reigned, (another) patriarch, who was called Timothy, was instituted over the city of Alexandria. But the (king) expelled the Father patriarch to the island of Gangra, in the place where they had expelled Dioscorus. He remained there for 10 years, until Leo was dead. Zeno reigned. The impious Timothy fled from Alexandria, and Father Timothy returned there by the order of the king, in great glory.]


[p. 284] {#po.9.p284}
ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡ ለሕዝቡ ፡ ወይጌሥጾሙ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፣ ለመሃይም ናን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በሀድአት ፣ ወሰላም ፡ ዙሎ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ፳ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕ"ረፈ ፣ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለእግዝእ ትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሐዋርያ
Continually he instructed his people, exhorted them, and strengthened the faithful in the right faith. || remained in calm and peace, all the days of his patriarchate. He sat on the seat of Mark the Evangelist for 22 years. It pleased the Lord, and he died in peace. May the Lord have mercy on us through the prayer of (Our)-Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, and through the prayer of this Father and of all the holy apostles! Amen.
ት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አሜን። ሰላም ፣ ለጢሞቴዎስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ምርዋጾ ። ለምሂር ፡ ወለገሥጾ ፡=፡ በመሠረተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሐኒጾ ፡=፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በስደት ፡ ቢጾ ፡፡
[Hail to Timothy, who spent his life Instructing and exhorting, By building many souls on a foundation of gold and silver! Because he had been his fellow in exile, His reward was not less than the reward of Father Dioscorus.]
እምዕሜተ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ አብ ፡ ዕሜቱ ፡ ኢሐጾ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ክዕበ ፣ ተወልደ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ራሔል ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፣፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
[On this day also was born Joseph, son of Rachel. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ክዕበ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለመፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ናዖድ ፡
[On this day also is the death of the friend of the Lord King Na'od, orthodox.


[p. 285] {#po.9.p285}
ርቱዐ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አ ሜን ፡9
May the Lord have mercy on us through his intercession for ever and ever! Amen.]
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡፤ ለስፍሕት ፡ የማኑ ። ከመ ፡ ትሥቅ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እለ ፡ ይተነትኑ ፡፡ ናኦድ ፡ ላእክ"ኪ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ መዘምራኑ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ስብሐተኪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግር ፡ ወይዜኑ ፡፡
[I say hail to his extended right (hand), In order to support all those who stumble! The tongue of Na'od, * your servant, rejoices in your holiness. (O) Mary, to say and announce your glory, where the chanters stand.]
በጽድቀ ፡ ዚአኪ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ ልሳኑ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ካህን ፡ አባ ፣ አልዓዛር ። በረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስ ሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለአሮን ፡ አበ ፣ አልዓዛር ፣ በኩሩ ፡=፡ ወአእኅወ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ነቢይ ፡ ዘቡሩክ ፡ ዝክሩ =፡ ለዝ ፡ ክህን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡ ወክብሩ ።፡ ልብሰ ፡ ጸናጽል ፡ ጠበብተ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ገብሩ ፡፡ በቃለ ፡ ጸዓዕ ፡ እስከ ፣ ይደምፅ ፡ እግሩ ።
[On this day also died the priest Abba Eleazar. May his blessing be with us! Amen.] [Hail to Aaron, father of Eleazar, his firstborn, And brother of the prophet Moses, whose memory is blessed! To this priest, because of his greatness and his glory, The sages of Israel made a garment (bordered with) bells, To the point that his foot made a sound, (like) the sound of thunder]


[p. 286] {#po.9.p286}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ቿለነሐሴ ›» ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 8TH OF NAHASÉ (August 14).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ኮኑ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፣ ፪ሰማዕታት ። እሉ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ አልዓዛር ፣ አረጋ ዊ ፡ ወሰሎሜ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፣ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፣ መክብዩ ፡ ወውሉዶሙ ፡ ፪ጸወዝአስማቲሆ ሙ። አንዒም ። ወአኒጦኒቶስ ። ወኦዝያ ። ወአልዓዛር ። ወአሳዮና ። ወሳሙና ። ወመ ርክሎስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አልዓዛር ፣ አረጋዊ ፣ ኮነ ፣ ፳አመምሀራነ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ በመዋዕሊ ሁ። ወኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እምድወ፪መተርጉማን ፡ እለ ፡ ተርጐሙ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ለበጥልምዮስ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፣ መሀሮሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡-ሕገ፣ ኦሪት ። ወዕበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ አንጥዮኮስ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወአገታ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ወነግሠ ፡ ላፅሌሃ ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፣ አይሁ ድ ፡ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአገበሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትዓደው ፡ ሕገ ፡ኦሪት ፣።ወ ይብልፁ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሕሩመ ፡፣ በሊዓ ፡ በሕገ ፣ ኦሪት ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ አሕርው ፡ ወዘአ ምሳሊሁ ። ወብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ ተዓደው ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወተአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ።፡ ወጸንዑ
On this day are the 9 holy Martyrs. They are the old man Eleazar ('Al'azar), Salome, his wife, which means Maccabee, and his seven sons. Here are their names: 'An'im, 'Anitonitos, 'Ozya, 'Al'azar, 'Asayona, Samouna, Marklos. The old man Eleazar was one of the doctors of the Mosaic law, in his time. He was the head of the 72 interpreters who translated the Mosaic law and the prophets for Ptolemy, king of Egypt. This just man taught (his) children the Mosaic law. When Antiochus, king of Rome, reigned over the land of Syria and the land of Judah, he besieged Jerusalem, reigned over it, persecuted the people of the Jews, * inflicted many tortures upon them, and forced them to transgress the Mosaic law, and to eat what it was forbidden to eat in the Mosaic law, that is to say, pork and (animals) like it. There were many who transgressed the Mosaic law, and obeyed him.


[p. 287] {#po.9.p287}
እሉ ፡ ጻድቃን ። ወእንዘ ፡ የዓቅቡ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አምኅበ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ አንጥዮኮስ ፡ ኔኔ ፣ ዓቢየ ፣ ወሰቀሎሙ ፣ ወቀሠፎሙ ፡ ወወ ሰረ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በሞሰርተ ፡ ሐጺን ፣ ወመተሩ ፡ መለያልያቲሆሙ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ጻድቅት ፣ ሰሎሜ ፡ እሞሙ ፡ ታጸንዖሙ ፣ ወታስተዔግሦሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ፈጸ ሙ፡ ስምዖሙ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወደየት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ርአሳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ በ ፈቃዳ ፡ ወኢፀንሐት ፡ በዘይወግራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ። ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታእምር ፡ ኦዘትሰምዕ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ እስመ ፣ አበ ዊነ ፡ መምሀራነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አዘዙነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ በዓለ ፣ ለጻድቃነ ፡ ሕገ ፣ ኦሪት ። እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ኢኃደግነ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ለገዲፍ ፡ አላ ፡ ፈለስነ ፡ እም ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘይቴይስ ፡ ወይፈደፍድ ‹፡ እምኔሃ ። ወንሕነ ፡ ንትወከፎሙ ፡ ለ ጻድቃነ ፡ ብሊት ፡ በበጾታሆሙ ፡ ወኢናከብሮሙ ፡ ፈድፋዶ ፡ እምነ ፡ አበው ፡ ዘሐዳ ስ ፡ እለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ እሙንቱ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በ ጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን።
But these just men were firm in observing the Mosaic law, which had been given to them by the Lord. Antiochus inflicted great tortures upon them, crucified them, flogged them, and sawed their bodies with an iron saw. They cut off their limbs. Saint Salome, their mother, encouraged them; she made them persevere, until they had completed their martyrdom. After them all, she threw herself into the fire, of her own free will, and without waiting for them to throw her into the fire. They received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. You must know, O you who hear this (reading), that our Fathers, the Doctors of the Church, have prescribed for us to celebrate the feast of the just of the Mosaic law. Indeed, we have not abandoned the work of the Mosaic law, to repudiate it, but we have passed from the Mosaic law into a (law) which is preferable and superior to it. For us, we admit the just of the old (law), each in their rank, and we do not honor them more than the Fathers of the new (law), who did more than those did. May the Lord have mercy on us through their prayer! Amen.
ሰላም ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰማዕቱ ።፡
[* Hail, hail to the martyrs of the Lord,


[p. 288] {#po.9.p288}
አልዓዛር ፤ መክብዩ ፡ ወደቂቆሙ ፡ ኒ፣ ለአንጥያኮስ ፡ ርኩስ ፣ እምይትአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ እንዘ ፡ ይትራወጹ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይትባደሩ ፡ ሞቱ ፡፡ በመብስለ ፡ ብርት ፡ ዘውውይ ፡ ፍልሐቱ ፡
To Eleazar, to Maccabee, and to his seven sons! Rather than obey the impure Antiochus, They died, by competing and surpassing one another, In the brazen cauldron, whose boiling was ardent.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ አሞን ። በረከቱ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡
[On this day also is Amon. May his holy blessing be with us for ever and ever!]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈ ፣ አብ ፡ ፅሙድ ፡ አባ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፣ አበ ፣ ምኔት ፣ ዘ ደብረ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ሳድስ ፡ እምአቡነ ፡ ተክለ ፣ ሃይማኖት ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፣ ጽድቁ ፣ ወተጋድሎቱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኃረይዎ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለአ ባ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወተብሀለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ታስተርእዮ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፣ ማ ርያም ፣ ወትጴልል ፡ ክነፊሃ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ርግብ ። ወእምድኅረ ፣ አሥመሮ ፡ ለ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ፃማ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ አመ ፡ ቿለነሐሴ ፣ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ትፍሥሕ ት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። በረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
[On this day also died the devout Father Abba John, superior of the convent of Dabra-Libanos. He was the sixth from our Father Takla-Haymanot. They elected this just man because of the greatness of his holiness and his asceticism, and they placed him on the seat of Abba Takla-Haymanot. It is said of him that Our Lady Mary appeared to him, and that she spread her wings over him, in the image of a dove. After it had pleased the Lord through (his) many austerities, he died on the 8th of Nahasé, and went away toward eternal happiness. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ፪ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 9TH OF NAHASÉ (August 15).


[p. 289] {#po.9.p289}
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኦሪ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀሲስ ፤ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰጠኑፍ ። ወኮነ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ምሕረት ፡ ወሣሀል ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ወብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜያተ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ራእያተ ፡ መለኮታዊት ። ወአስተር አየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ወከሠተ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ዘ ይፈቅድ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኦሪ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ነቅዮስ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውሣር ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለአማልክት ፣ ወለጣዖታት ፣ ወኢተ አዘዘ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአፍርሆ ፡ ወተቁቄጥዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኢፈርሃ ፡ እምቀ፡ጥዓሁ ። ወኩነኖ ፡ ቬኔኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፡ ዙነኔ፣ ዓቢየ ፡ ወገደፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራ ተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወፈወሶሙ ፡ ለድውያን ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ቬናሁ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ብ ዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ" እምኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ። ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዓ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ነገረ ፡ አዘዘ፣ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማ
On this day is Saint Abba 'Ori, martyr. This saint was a priest of the people of the city of Satanouf. He was of great mercy and (great) clemency. He was pure in his soul and (in his) body. Many times, divine visions appeared to him. Our Lord Christ appeared to him on the altar, and revealed to him the mysteries, which he desired. The fame of Saint Abba 'Ori was heard by the judge of the city of Naqyos. He had the saint brought, and said to him: "Offer incense to the gods and the idols." He did not obey him. (The judge) terrorized him, and became angry with him. But he was not afraid of his anger. (The judge) inflicted a great torture upon him, then he sent him to the city of Alexandria. There, they inflicted a great * torture upon him, and threw him into prison. He performed many miracles and wonders, and healed the sick. His fame was heard, and many people came to him from all countries. When the judge had learned this fact, he ordered the head of the saint to be cut off with the sword. He received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom

ያት ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ወነሥአ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወገነዞ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገ ሩ ፡ ሰጠኑፍ ፣ ወአምድኅረ ፣ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ፣ ተሐንጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ፣ ክርስ"ቲያን ፣ ሠናይት ፡ ወአንበሩ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት ። ወሥጋሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በህየ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይገብር ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ ድውያን ፡ እለ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፣ በአሚን ። እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፣፡ አሜን።
of heaven. Saint Julius came, took his body, buried it, and sent it to the city of Satanouf. After the completion of the days of persecution, a beautiful * church was built for him. They deposited his body there. Great miracles and wonders occurred there. His body remains there until now, by performing great healings for all the sick who come to him with faith. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.
ሰላም ፡ ለኦሪ ፣ ለቅድስናሁ ፡ ትሩፍ እንተ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተናበቦ ፡ በአፍ ፤ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ ዮም ፡ አመ ፡ ክላሌሁ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡፡ ከመ ፡ አስከሬን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዜና ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ጽሑፍ ። ባሕርየ ፡ አፅሙ ፡ ጸዊራ ፡ ሰጠኑፍ ፡፡
[Hail to 'Ori, of remarkable holiness, to whom Christ appeared and spoke mouth to mouth! Satanouf, enclosing the pearl of (his) bones, rejoices today at his crowning by the sword, (as indicated by) the account of (his) combat, written on the reliquary.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ካፅበ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፣ ለሏላጦስ ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡
[On this day also is the memorial of Philotheus, patriarch. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for ever and ever!]

በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.

READING OF THE 10TH OF NAHASÉ (August 16). On this day is Saint Matra, martyr, in the days of Abba Demetrius, patriarch of the city of Alexandria, and in the days of Decius, * the impious king. When this saint had heard the reading of the letter in which (Decius) ordered to deny Christ and to worship idols, this saint went away, stole the arm of the idol Apollo, (which) was of gold, and broke it into pieces, which he gave to the poor. When they searched for the arm of the idol and did not find it, they arrested many people regarding this. At that moment, the saint came to the judge, confessed before him, and said: "It is I who stole the arm of the impure idol, and gave it to the poor." When the judge had heard this, he inflicted upon him an extremely great and painful torture. Then they threw him into a furnace of fire. The Lord delivered him from it, and brought him out safe and sound, without injury. Then, they cut off his hands and feet, roasted his flesh in the fire, and crucified him head down on the wood. Much blood flowed from his mouth and nose onto the earth. At that moment, the angel of the Lord descended from heaven, delivered Saint Matra from the crucifixion, touched his body with his


[p. 292] {#po.9.p292}
ቅለት ፡ ወገሰሰ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወፈወሰ ፡ ቀሩሰላቲሁ ። ወመጽአ ፡ 8ብእሲ ፡ ዕው ር ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ደም ፡ ዘውሕዘ ፡ እምአሩሁ ፡ ወአንፉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣፡ ወቀብአ ፣ ቦቱ ፡ አፅይንቲሁ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በጊዜሃ ። ወሶበ.፣ ደክመ ፣ መኩንን ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘ ዘ፣ ወመተሩ ፤ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ በመንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያት ። ወኮነ ፣ እምሥጋሁ ፣ ፈውስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ለድውያን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡
hand, and healed his wounds. A blind man came, took some of the blood that had flowed from the mouth and nose of the saint, anointed his eyes with it, and saw immediately. When the judge was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off with the sword. He received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Through his body, great healings of the sick occurred. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us! Amen.
ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለመጥራ ፣ መዝራዕተ ፡ አኦሎን ፡ ሠሪቆ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ዘረወ ፣ ወላ”ዕለ ፡ ምስኪናን ፣ ወርቆ = ወሶበ ፣ አምነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ በተሳልቆ ።፡ በብዙኅ ፡ምንዳቤ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ መኩንን ፣፡ አጽአቆ ፤ በአፈ ፣ መጥባሕት ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ወሠለጠ ፡ጻሕቆ ።
[Hail to Matra, who stole the arm of Apollo, and distributed the gold to the poor and * to the needy! When he had confessed that he had done this in derision. After the judge had tortured him with many torments, he finished and consumed his desire (to be a martyr) by the edge of the sword.]

[On this day also is Saint Christopher, martyr, in the days of the impious Maximian. When they had brought the saint before him, he said to him:


[p. 293] {#po.9.p293}
ክት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሐርስጥፉሩስ ፡ አንሰ ፣ አመልኮ ፡ ለእግዚእየ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወሎቱ ፡-እሠውዕ ፤፣ ወተምዓ ፡መክስምያኖስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይዝብጥዎ ፡ በ አብትር ፡ እስከ ፡ ይወድቅ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ። ወወረውጆዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ፪አንስት ፣ ከመ ፡ ያስሕታሁ ፡፣ በላህዮን = ወቅዱ ስ ፡ መሐሮን ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአመሮን ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ተመጠዎን ፡ መክስምያኖ ስ ፣፡ይቤሎን ፡ እፎ ፡ ገበርክን ። ወይቤላሁ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ምእመናን ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ሐርስጥፉሩስ ፡ ወሰሚዖ ፣ መኩንን ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡ ይስቅልዋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ወያውዕይዋ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወለካልእታ ፡ ይዕስሩ ፡ ዕብነ ፡ በክሳዳ ፣ ወይስ"ቅልዋ ፣ ቀሩልቀ ሊተ ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ፈጸማ ፡ ስምዖን።
"Sacrifice to the idols." Christopher said to him: "As for me, I worship my Lord Jesus Christ - glory be to Him! - and it is to Him that I sacrifice." Maximian became angry, and ordered that he be struck with rods, until his body fell to the ground. They threw him into prison. Then, he sent two women to him, in order to seduce him with their beauty. The saint taught them and explained to them the faith in Christ. When Maximian received them, he said to them: "How did you do?" They said to him: "We are believers in Christ, the God of Saint Christopher." Having heard (this), the judge ordered that one be crucified on the wood, and that she be burned in the fire; and that a stone be tied to the neck of the other, and that * she be crucified head down. It is thus that they consumed their martyrdom.
ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ሐርስጥፉሩስ ፣ ወአስተጋብኡ ፡ ፅፀወ ፡ ክርም ፡ ወአን ደዱ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወወረውዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሠር ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወኢለከ ፈቶ ፡ አሳት ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ርእዩ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ ወተቀትሉ ፡ ፳ብእ ሲ ። ወካዕበ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ሰላድወ ፡ ዘሐዒን ፡ ርሱናተ ፣ ወአቀምዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዲቤሆ ሙ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘለከፎ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚአነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡፣ መጠነ ፡ ክልኤ ፡፤ ፳ወ፪፻ብእሲ ፡ ወጓሕፃናት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ሰ ማዕተ ። ወሰበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣ ተምዕዓ ፡ "ላዕ
Then he called Saint Christopher. They gathered vine wood, set fire to it, and threw him (into the brazier), hands and feet bound. But the fire did not touch him. When the people saw this, they all believed. Ten thousand men were killed. Furthermore, they brought red-hot iron plates, and held the saint upon them. Nothing touched him. The people of Rome, seeing (this), believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory be to Him! - (to the number of) about

ለ ፤ ቅዱስ ፣ ሐርስጥፉሩስ ፣ ወአዘዘ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይምትሩ ፡፣ ክሳዶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወወጺኦ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ገብረ ፣ ጸሎተ ፤ ወኀተመ ፡ ገጾ ፡ በትእምርተ ፣ መስቀል ። ወእም ዝ ፡ መተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ በሰይፍ ፣ ወፈጸመ ፣ ስምዖ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፤ በጸሎ ቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
20,200 men and 40 children. They became martyrs. When Maximian saw that all the people believed, he became angry * against Saint Christopher, and ordered his neck to be cut with the sword. (Christopher), upon leaving the king, made a prayer, and signed his face with the sign of the cross. Then they cut off his head with the sword, and he consumed his martyrdom. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for ever and ever! Amen.]
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ከሚትየ ፡ አፈ፤ ለሐርስጥፎሮስ ፡ በስምዕ ፡ ዘኢፈርሃ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡፡ ለበድነ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡፡ ኢተዓደወ ፡ ውጊቲዝ ፡ ወኢሂ ፡ ኃለፈ ።= ዘርአ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ዘያማስን ፡ ዘልፈ ፡=
* [I say hail, opening my mouth, to Christopher, who in (his) martyrdom did not fear the sword! (The place) where they made a resting place for the corpse of his holy body. The torrent, which always devastated the seeds of the region, has not crossed it, nor passed beyond it.]
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለአዕላፍ ፡ ሠላስ ፤ ! ፪፻ወጓምስለ ፡፪አእኑስ ።፡ ዝፄወዎሙ ፡ በገድሉ ፣ ሐርስጥፉሩስ ፡፡ ብሔረ ፡ ግዕዛን ፡ ነሥኦሙ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ ወአሰርገዎሙ ፡ በስብሐት ፣ ሐዲስ ፡
[I say hail to the 3,240 (men), with the two women, whom Christopher made captives by his combat! The (divine) Spirit has received them into the land of freedom, and has adorned them with a new glory.]


[p. 295] {#po.9.p295}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ቢከብስ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፣ እምሀገረ ፣ እስሙንጠናሕ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሐራ ፡፣ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በኅቡእ ። ወነገርዎ ፡ ለአንጥያኮስ ፡ መኩንን ፣ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆዕስ ፡ አባ ፣ አክ ሎዝ ፡ ወበአንተ ፡ አባ ፡ አናሀርው ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ተርሳ ፣ ወአባ ፣ ፊልይስ ፡ ወአስተ"ዋ ደይዎሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ክመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሹሎሙ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እሙንኑ ፡ አንትሙ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወተአመኑ ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በአማን ፡ ገሃደ ፣ ነአምን ፣ በእግዚእነ ፣ ወአምላክ ነ ፣ ወመድኃኒነ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ሕያው ። ወይቤሎሙ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ኅድጉ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወሥዑ ፡ ለአማልክት ። ወአውሥእዎ፣፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፣ ኢንሠውዕ ፣ ለጣዖታት ፡ ርኩሳን ፣ ወኢንሠውዕ ፡ ዘእ ንበለ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ፈጣሬ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወምድር ። ወሶዕበ ፣ ሰም ዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ተምዓ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወለቅዱ ስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢከብስ ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፣ ዓቢየ ፣ ወብዙኃ ፣ ወሞቅሖ ፣ በሐጺን ፣ ወወደ
On this day also is Saint Abba Bikabes, martyr. This saint was from the city of Esmountanah. He was a soldier. He was a Christian in secret. They told the judge Antiochus about him, about the bishop Abba Akloz, and about Abba Anaharw, who was from the city of Tarsa, and about Abba Philip, and they * accused them before the judge of all being Christians. The judge had them brought, and said to them: "Is it true that you are Christians?" They confessed before him, and said to him: "We are Christians, in truth. Publicly, we confess Our Lord, Our God and Our Savior Jesus Christ, Son of the living Lord." The judge said to them: "Withdraw this word, and sacrifice to the gods." The saints answered him, and said to him: "As for us, we do not sacrifice to impure idols; we only sacrifice to Our Lord Jesus Christ, creator of the heavens and the earth." When the judge had heard this word, he became extremely angry, and inflicted a great torture upon them. He inflicted a great and violent torture upon Saint Abba Bikabes. He chained him with iron, put him in wheels, crucified him head down, and cut off his
ዮ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መንኩራኩራት ፡ ወሰቀሎ ፣ ቀሌሩልቀሌሊተ ፣ ወመተሮ ፡ መለያልያቲሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያጸንዖ ፣ ወያስተዔግሦ ፡ ወያነሥኦ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስ ና ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ "ብዙኃን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በረ ሙኒ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ፳ወ፲ኒመዓልዯ ፡ ኢበልዑ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወኢሰትዩ ፡ማ የ ። ወዕበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በረሙኒ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢከብስ ፥ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ መተሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በማሕፄ ፡ ወፈጸመ ፡ ስምዖ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክ ሊለ ፡ ሕይወት ፡፤ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወመጽአ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ባዕል ፣ እምሥዩማነ፣ ሀገረ ፡ በረሙኒ ፤ ወነሥአ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወገነዞ ፡ በአልባሳት ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ወፈነ ዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ እስሙንጠናሕ ። ወበመዋዕለ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ኩነኔሁ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ሰ ማዕታት ፡ ዘኢይትኃለቀ- ። ወበዕለትሰ ፡ ዘፈጸመ ፡ ስምዖ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ጓወ5፳ነፍ ስ ። ወለቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢከብስ ፡ ሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ ወአንበ ሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። እግዚአብሔ ር ፣፡ይምሐረነ ፤በጸሎቶሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ አሜን ። ወ
limbs. The Lord strengthened him, made him persevere, and made him rise safe and sound, without injury. Then, he sent the saint with * many martyrs to the city of Baramouni. They remained in a ship for 27 days, without eating bread or drinking water. When they had arrived at the city of Baramouni, they inflicted a great torture upon Saint Abba Bikabes. Then they cut his body with a saw. He consumed his martyrdom, and received the crown of life in the kingdom of heaven. A rich man from among the nobles of the city of Baramouni came, took the body of the saint, buried him in beautiful clothes, and sent him to Esmountanah, his city. In the days of the duration of the torture of (Bikabes) there were many martyrs; (they are) innumerable. The day he consumed his martyrdom, there were 95 martyrs. They built a beautiful church to Saint Abba Bikabes, and they deposited his body there. Many miracles appeared through him. May the Lord have mercy on us through the prayer of all those who were martyrs with him! Amen. Amen.


[p. 296] {#po.9.p296}
አሜን። ሰላም ፡፣ ለአባ ፣ ቢካቦስ ፣ ዘክኖሙ ፡፣ ተባያጹ ፡=፡ ለጓወ፳ሰማዕታተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ግጋፄ ፡፡

To confound the idols of the Gentiles and their iniquitous king! Terror did not come upon this intrepid (apostle), when they cut his body with the axe.]
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፤ ኪያክሙ ፡ ማኅበረ ።፡ ኦሪታዊያን ፣፡ ቅድመ ፡ ወወንጌላዊያን ፡ ድኅረ ፡፡ ፍሠሐን ፡ አፅላፍ ፡ እለ ፤ ተሳተፍክሙ ፡ በኩረ ፡=፡ ጽዮንሃ ፡ በጺሐክሙ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በጸሎትክሙ ፣ ዝክሩኒ ፡ ወትረ ።፡
[I say hail to you, society (of the saints) of the (Old) Law first, and of the Gospel then, who, with joy, (and) by the thousands, have been the companions of the First-Born! Arrived at the city of (celestial) Zion, in your prayer, remember me continually.]
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።”
* In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ፲ወ፳ጸለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING OF THE 11TH OF NAHASÉ (August 17).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፳፻ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ፣ ሰማዕ ት ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረ
On this day, are the 2,000 martyrs with Saint Fasiladas, martyr, chief of the troops of the city of Antioch. May the Lord have mercy on us through their prayer, and may their blessing be with us! Amen.
ከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈ ፣ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ሞይሳስ ፣ ኤስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡
On this day also, died the holy Father Abba Moysas, bishop of the city

ዘሀገረ ፣ አውሲም ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወንጹሕ ፣ ወድንግል ፣ እምንእሱ-፡ ወተምህረ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ትምሀርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወተሠይመ ፡፣ ዲያቆነ ።= ወእምዝ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፣ ወመንኩሴ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ጻድቅ ። ወነበረ ፡ እ ንዘ ፡ ይትለአኮ ፡ ፲፻ወ፲ዓመተ ፣ ወይጸመድ ፤ ወይትጋደል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጸባብ ፡ወ መብልዑፁ ፡ ወመስቴሁ ፡ ወንዋሙ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ወትረ ፣ ይጸውም ፡ ወይጹሊ ፣ ወይተግሀ ፡ ወብዙኅ ፡ትሕትናሁ ፡ ወፍቅሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፣ ወትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ወጽድቁ ፡ ኀረዮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አውሲም ፡"እ ምድኅረ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ግሙል ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ እምቅድሜሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፣ ተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፣ ሠናየ ፣ ወወሰከ ፡ ገ ቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ይገብር ፣፤ በምንኩስናሁ ። ወዓቀቦሙ ፣ ለመርዔቱ ፡ መሲሐዊያን ፡ እምተኩላተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይተግህ ፡ በጸሎት ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ያጠሪ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ዓ”ለም ፣፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕ
of Awsim. This Father was holy, pure, and a virgin from his childhood. He learned all the teaching of the Church, and was ordained a deacon. Then he went up to the desert of Scetis, and became a monk under a just man. He continued to serve him for 18 years, and to practice devotion and asceticism, in the narrow way, (in) his food, his drink, and his sleep. Continually he fasted, prayed, and kept vigil. His humility and his charity were great. When his fame, his virtues, and his holiness were known, the Lord chose him to become bishop of * the city of Awsim, * after the holy Father Abba Gemoul, who was bishop before him. When this Father was instituted bishop, he practiced a beautiful asceticism, and he practiced holiness even more, far beyond what he had done in his monastic life. He defended his flock of Christians against the wolves of the devil. He kept vigil, praying for them. He possessed none of the goods of this * passing world, during all the days of his life.
ለ፣ ሕይወቱ። ወዕበ ፣ ተሠይመ ፡፣ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ዘኮነ፣ ሰማዕተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ክዕወተ ፡ዶደም ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ቤተ፣፡ሞ
When Abba Michael was instituted patriarch of the city of Alexandria, he who was a martyr without shedding of blood, this Father was with him in prison, and endured


[p. 299] {#po.9.p299}
ቅሕ ፡ ወበጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፣፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወቀሠፍዎ ፣ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣፡ ማሕ ምመ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎ ፡ በሐጺን ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ነዊኃ ፣ መዋዕለ ። ወገ ብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወበእ ንተ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ ጠየቁ ፡ እለ ፣ ኢየአምርዎ ፡ ጽድቆ ፣ ወትሩፋቲሁ ። ወተውህሀበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ወተናገረ ፣፤ በብዙኅ ፡ ምግባራት ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ወይከ ውን ፡በከመ ፡ ተነበየ ። እስመ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለአባ ፣ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፣ አስመ ፤ ንጉሠ ፡፤ ግብጽ ፣ ኢይትመየጥ ፡ አምንግደቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ በከ መ ፡ ተነበየ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ምግባራት ፡ ወ ተፈጸመ ፡ ትንቢቱ ። ወፈወሶሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ድውያን ፣ ወሕሙማን ፡ በጸሎቱ ። ወሶበ ፣ ፈጸመ ፡ ገድሎ ፣ በርሥዕና ፣ ሠናይት ፣ ወአሥመሮ ፣ ለአግዚአብሔር ፤ ሐመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ። ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ለሕዝቡ ፡ ወባረኮሙ ፡ ወፈ ትኖሙ ፡ ወሰአሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸልዩ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፣ ወበከዩ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡፤ ይጸሊ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ።፡ ወጸለየ ፣ ሳዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፤ ተዓቀቡ ፥ ወጽንዑ ፣ በሃይማኖትክሙ ፡ ርትዕት ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ወበከዩ ፡ላ
great torments with him. They made him undergo many (and) painful flagellations; they chained him with irons on his feet, many times, for long days. The Lord worked many miracles through the intermediary of this Father. Because of his miracles, those who did not know him noticed his holiness and his virtues. The grace of prophecy was granted to him: he announced many events before they happened, and they happened as he had prophesied. It is he who said to Abba Theodore, bishop of the city of Cairo, that the king of Egypt would not return from his journey, and it happened as he had prophesied. He prophesied many things about the prefect of the city of Cairo; his prophecies were realized. He healed by his prayer many sick and infirm people. When he had finished his combat, in a beautiful old age, and it had pleased the Lord, he was ill for a short time, and knew the moment of his death. He called his flock, blessed them, gave them absolution, and asked them to pray for him. All (the people) wept, and asked him to pray for them himself. He prayed for them and said to them: "Maintain yourselves, and strengthen yourselves in your orthodox faith." When he had said this, he died. Many tears were shed over him.


[p. 300] {#po.9.p300}
ዕሌሁ ፣ ብካየ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወገነዝዎ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፣ በዝማሬ ፣ ወበማኅሌት ፣ በከመ ፣ ይ ደልዎሙ ፡ ለኤዲስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ። ወአስተርአየ ፣ አምሥጋሁ ፣ ተአምራት ፣ ብዙኃት ፡ወ ፈውስ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ መቱ ፡ ፳ዓመት ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድ ። እግዚ አብሔር ፡ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለሞይስስ ፡ ፍጹም ፣ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ዘብሔረ ፡ አውሲም ።፡፡ በጸሎት ፡ዘተግሃ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ በጾም ፡፡፡ ሕመ ፡ ርስእናሁ ፡ በንስቲት ፡ ሕማም ፡=፡ አምጻማ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ዮም ፡
They held his funeral with great pomp, amidst psalms and chants, as is fitting for bishops. Many miracles and great healings appeared through his body. All the days of his episcopate were 20 years and more. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us! Amen. [I say hail to Moysas, the perfect, Bishop of the land of Awsim, who kept vigil, making prayers, and (who) practiced asceticism through fasting! During his old age, in a small illness, (Moving away) from the tribulations of the world, he died today.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፣ አብጥልማዎስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፣ ዘእምሀገ ረ ፣ምኑፍ ፡ ላዕላይ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አስተዋደይዎ ፡ ኅበ ፣ መኩንን ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፣፤ ወአብጽሖ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፣ ወተአመነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩኔኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊለ ፣ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also is Saint Abba Abtelmawos [Ptolemy], martyr of the city of Upper Menouf. They accused this saint before the judge, (saying) that he was a Christian. (The judge) had him brought before him. (Abba Abtelmawos) confessed Our Lord Christ. (The judge) inflicted a great torture upon him, and cut off his head with the sword. He received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer! Amen.


[p. 301] {#po.9.p301}
ሰላም ፡ ለአብጥልማዎስ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰማዕቱ ።፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሰጥኑፍ ፡ ፀዓቱ ፡፡ ዕበ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዕልዋን ፡ አምነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፣፤ ሎ”ቱ ፡፡ ፡ በአፈ ፣ መጥባሕት ፡ ዮም ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሞቱ ፡:
[Hail to Abtelmawos, martyr of the Lord, native of the city of Satnouf! When before the impious he had confessed that he was a Christian, (And) after they * had inflicted many tortures upon him, his death was consumed today by the edge of the sword.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፲፪ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING OF THE 12TH OF NAHASÉ (August 18).
በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፣ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘይተነብ ል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ። አስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ አስተር አዮ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዙ ሉ ፡ ጸላአቱ ፡ ወሞኦሙ ፡፣ ወጸንዓት ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወአንሀለ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖቶሙ ፤ ወሐነጸ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወአሰርገዎሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ሠርጓት ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አ ዘዙነ ፡ አበዊነ ፤ መምህራነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፣ በዓሎ ፣ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ለለወርጉ ፡ ትንብልናሁ ፡የሀሉ ፡ም
* On this day is the memorial of the glorious angel Michael, the archangel, who intercedes for us before the Lord, at every moment. [It is on this day that he appeared to Constantine the Just, (king) of the city of Rome, and gave him strength against all his enemies. (Constantine) defeated them, and his kingship was strengthened. He destroyed the temples of the idols, and he built churches, (which he) embellished with all (kinds) of ornaments. This is why our Fathers, the Doctors of the Church, have ordered us to celebrate the feast of the glorious angel, the archangel Michael, on the 12th of each month.] May his intercession be with us! Amen.
ስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፣ መልአክ ፡ ኪዳኑ ፡ ወምክሩ ።፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡ ዘግሩም ፡ ግብሩ ፡=፡ በረድኤትከ ፡ ረዋጺ ፡ ዘአሠረ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ አሠሩ ፡።፡ አመ ፡ላዕሌየ ፡ እኩየ ፡ መከሩ ፡፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ፀርየ ፡ ሚክኤል ፡ ዝሩ ፡፡
[Hail to you, angel of the testament and of the counsel of the Most High Lord, whose work is august! By your help, (O) swift (messenger), (you) whose paths are the paths of the wind, when they have meditated evil against me, disperse, (O) Michael, the assembly of my enemies.]


[p. 302] {#po.9.p302}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡፣ ነግሠ ፣ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ «. ሮሜ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መ”ንግሥታተ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ወሀገረ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ወኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወመንግሥተ ፡ መሲሐዊተ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕበ፣ ነግሠ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ቆንስጣ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በርነጥያ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወ ሶበ ፣ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ፣ ወነግሠ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ በሀገረ ፣ በርነጥያ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ደወ ላ። ወአጥፍአ ፡ ዓመፃ ፡ ወገባርያነ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ወዓማዊያነ ፡ አኩያነ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ መንግ ሥቱ ፡ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ፍትሑ ፡ ወጽድቁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ምድር ። ወፈነው ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባልሖሙ ፡ እምንዓመፃሁ ፤ ለመክሲምያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሐዲ ፣፤ ወያድኅኖሙ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፡ ዚአሁ ።
On this day also reigned Constantine, the just king, over the city of Rome, over all the kingdoms of the lands and regions of Syria, (over) Jerusalem, (over) the land of Egypt, and (over) the Christian kingdoms. It is he (who) reigned with his father Constantius, in the city of Byzantium, for many years. When his father was dead, he reigned alone over the city of Byzantium and (over) all its territory. He caused iniquity, evildoers, the perverse, (and) the wicked to disappear from his kingdom. His justice and his holiness were known to all the ends of the earth. The nobles of the city of Rome sent (messengers) to him, asking him to deliver them from the iniquity of Maximian, the impious king, and to deliver them from his slavery.


[p. 303] {#po.9.p303}
ወዕበ ፡ አንበበ ፣ መልእክቶሙ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሖሙ ፣ እምዓመፃሁ ፡ ለ ውእቱ ፡ ከሐዲ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ እፎ ፡ ይበልሖሙ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መስቀ ል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወተራድአ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፀብኦቱ ፡ ለመክሲምያኖስ ፡ ወሰበሮ፣ ወሞኦ ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወተበትከ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ተንከተም ፡ በውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በእኩይ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወተሰጥሙ ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወውእቱኬ ፡ በሳ ብፅ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተቀበልዎ ፡ ሊቃውንተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወኩሎ ሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፣ በፍሥሐ ፣ ወበሐሜት ። ወገብሩ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ዓ ቢየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መዊኦቱ ፡ ፲መዓልተ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ማእምራነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወጠቢባነ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ እለ ፡ ጥዑም ፣ ቃላቲሆሙ ፣ ወአንብቦቶሙ ፡ ይዌድስዎ ፣፡ ለመስቀል ፣ ክቡር ወይሰምይዎ ፣ መድኅነ ፣ ሀገሮሙ ፣ ወይዌድስዎ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፣ ወይሰምይዎ ፡መ
When he had read their letter, he was saddened by what was happening to them as a result of the iniquity of that impious man. He pondered how he would deliver them. The glorious cross appeared to him. He leaned upon it, and set out to fight Maximian. He defeated him, and overcame him. (Maximian) fled (far) from him. A bridge broke under him; (he fell) into the river, and died a horrible death. All his troops were swallowed up in the river. It was the seventh year of his reign. Immediately, the nobles of the city of Rome and all the people (of the city) went out to meet him, in great pomp, in joy and in gladness. A great feast was held on the occasion of his victory, for seven days. The scholars of the city of Rome and its sages, whose speeches and songs were harmonious, praised the glorious cross, and called it the deliverer of their city. They (also) celebrated Constantine, and called him the victor and the strong one by the power of the life-giving cross.
ዋኢ ፡ ወጽኑዕ ፣ በኃይለ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ማሕየዊ ። ወበ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ተጠምቀ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለሰልጴ ጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፣ ወተዘከረ ፣ ስሙ፣ ዲበ ፣ መ”ሥዋዕት ፡ ቅድስ ት ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወአመሰ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መስቀል ፣፡
In the fourteenth year of his reign, he received Christian baptism from the hand of Sylvester, Pope of the city of Rome, and his name was mentioned at the holy * Sacrifice, on this day. How the glorious cross appeared to him,


[p. 304] {#po.9.p304}
ክቡር ፣ ወዘከመ ፡ ፀብኦ ፡ ለመክሲምያኖስ ፡ ወዘክመ ፣ ተሰጥመ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ከሐዲ ፡ወ ናሁ ፣ ጸሐፍናሁ ፣ አመ፳ወ፳ለወርኃ ፣ መጋቢት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ። ወስብሐት ፤ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኃኒነ። ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን።
how he fought Maximian, and how that impious man was swallowed up (in the river), behold, we have written it on the 28th of the month of Magabit, the day of the death of this just king. Glory to Our Lord and to Our Savior Jesus Christ for ever and ever! Amen.
ሰላም ፣ እብል ፣ መዋዔ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወሱላሜ ፣ በኅልፈተ ፡ ሐዘን ፤ ዘኮነ፣ ወእምድኅረ ፣ ብዙኅ ፣ ሕማሜ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ይሰብክ ፣ ወዘመስቀሉ ፣ ትርጓሜ ፡=፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ዮም ፡ ነግሠ ፣፤ በሮሜ ፡=፡
[I say greeting to the victor, (cause) of joy and concord, Who brought an end to affliction, and (who), after many calamities, Preached the faith of Christ, and the symbol of the cross, (To) Constantine, (who) today has reigned in Rome, When the impious Maximian had perished]
ዘመክስምያኖስ ፡ ዕልው ፡ ሶበ ፣ ኀልቀ ፣እድሜ ፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፳፻ሰብእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡፣ በክፅወተ ፣ ደሞሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also are the 2,000 martyrs with Saint Basilides. May the Lord have mercy on us through their prayer (and) through the shedding of their blood, and may their blessing be with us! Amen.


[p. 305] {#po.9.p305}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 13TH OF NAHASÉ (August 19).
አመ ፡ ፲፻ል4ነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በዓለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡-ክርስቶስ፡፣ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ተ ወለጠ ፡ አርአያሁ › ዲበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፻አርዳኢሁ ፡ እለ ፤ እሙንቱ ፤ ጴጥሮስ ፣ ወያዕቆብ ፣ ወዮሐንስ ፡ እለ ፣ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ፣ሀለው፣ እለ ፣፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ዝየ ፡ አለ ፣ ኢይጥዕምዋ ፡ ለሞት ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሬእይዎ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እ ጓለ ፡ አመ”ሕያው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ በስብሐቲሁ ዞ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ዘርአይዎ ፣ ውስቴ ቱ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ እስመ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ፡ ወተወለጠ ፡ አርእያ ሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ መብረቅ ፡ ዘይበርህ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ሙሴ ፥፡ ወኤልያስ ፡ መጽኡ ፥ ኀቤሁ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይትናገሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ክመ ፡ ያእምሩ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግ ዚኡ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ወዘአንሥኦ ፡ እሙታን ። ወአምላኩ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፣ ወዘአውረዶ ፡ እም
On this day is the feast (of the Transfiguration) of Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - (where one commemorates) how his appearance changed on Mount Tabor. With him were his three disciples, that is to say Peter, James, and John, concerning whom Our Lord said: Those who will stand there will not taste death until they have seen the Son of * man coming in his glory. This is the glory in which the apostles saw (Our Lord). Indeed, his garments had become white as snow; his appearance had been transformed, and had become like lightning that shines. Behold, Moses and Elijah came to him, (and remained) to converse with him, so that all men might know that he was the Lord of Moses, whom he had raised from the dead, and the God of Elijah, whom he had brought down from the heavens.
ሰማያት። ወበእንተዘ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ትፈቅድኑ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ክመ ፡ ንንበር ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወንግበር ፡ ይማኅደረ ፡ ፳ለከ ፡ ወ8ለሙሴ ፡ ወአለኤልያስ ፡ በዝንቱ ፣ ቃልሰ ፣፤ ድ ካም ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ትምህርት ፣ ወትሕትና ። ድካከምሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ኤስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡
Because Peter said to Our Lord: Lord, dost thou wish that we remain here, and that we make three dwellings: one for thee, one for Moses, and one for Elijah? By this word (he showed) weakness, and (he gave) a lesson in humility. (He showed) weakness. (The word) that Peter said,


[p. 306] {#po.9.p306}
ነጸሮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ወሐለየ ፣ ከመ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ይነብር ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር ፣ ወይኅድ ግ፣ምክን”ያተ ፡ዘዝበእንቲአሁ ፡ወረደ ፡ እምሰማይ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበ ሩ፤ሎቱ ፡ ዘይዔውሮ ፡ እምፀሐይ ። ወትምህርትሰ ፣ ወትሕትና ፡ እስመ ፣ ጴጥሮስሰ ፡ ኢፈቀይደ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፣ ወለአብያጺሁ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ በከመ ፣ ፈቀደ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወለሙ ሴ ፡ ወለኤልያስ ፡ አላ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ነፍሶ ፣ ወለአብያጺሁ ፡፣ ሐዋርያት ፣ በከመ ፣ አግብር ት ፡ ለእሎንቱ ፣ አጋእዝት ። ወኢታንክር ፡ እምሕጹጸ ፣፤ አእምሮቶሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፡ ፍጹማነ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ። ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ናሁ ፣ ደመና ፣ ጸለሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርእዮ ፡፤ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ከ መ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ዘተገብረ ፡ በእደ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እም ደመና › ዘያጸንዕ ፤ መለኮቶ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፣፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡፣ ለሐዋርያት ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡፤ ዝንቱ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ወልድየ ፡ ዘአፈቅር ፣ ዘዝኪያሁ ፣ ሠመርኩ ፡ ወሎቱ ፡ ስም
(he said it) because he had looked at Our Lord. He thought that (Jesus) would remain above the mountain, and would abandon the * cause for which he had descended from heaven. He wanted one to make for him (a dwelling), to shelter him from the sun. (He gave) a lesson in humility. Indeed, Peter did not want for his companions, the apostles, and for himself, as he wanted for Our Lord, for Moses, and for Elijah, but he looked upon his companions, the apostles, and himself, as the servants of these Lords. Do not be astonished at the lack of knowledge of the apostles, for they were not perfect in knowledge at that time. When (Peter) had said this, behold, a cloud overshadowed them, in order to show Peter that (Our Lord) did not want a dwelling made by the hand of man. A voice came from the cloud, which strengthened (the faith in) the divinity of Our Lord in the soul of the apostles, saying: This is my Son, whom I love, (and) in whom I am well pleased; listen to him.
ዕዎ። ወኮኑ፣ሙሴ ፣ "ወኤልያስ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይትአመኑ ፡ በጸጋሁ ፡ ወበጊሩ ቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ተጠየቀ ፡ ትንቢቶሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፣ በምጽአቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ። ወበዕ
Moses * and Elijah were conversing with (Our Lord), and confessing his grace and his goodness. Indeed, the prophecies of the prophets were verified by the coming


[p. 307] {#po.9.p307}
ርገቱ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር ፤ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፣ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ነቢያ ት ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ርአ”ዩ ፡ ትስብእቶ ፣ ዘኢኮኑ ፡ የአምርዎ ። ወተፈሥሑ ፣ ሐዋር ያት ፡ ክዕበ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አብ ፣ ዘኢኮኑ ፣ ሰምዕዎ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ወሰምፅዎ ፡ በውእ ቱ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወበቃለ ፡ አብ ፡ ጠየቁ ፡ ትስብእቶ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ኅቡአ ፣ እምኔሆ ሙ ፡ ወበጊዜ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አብ ፡ አስተርአየ ፣ ስብሐተ ፣፤ ትስብእቱ ። ወአእመሩ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፤ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአንሥአ ፣ ነፍሶ ፣ ለሙ ሴ ፡ውእቱ ፡ ዘአዕረጎ ፡ ለኤልያስ ፡ እስመ ፣ በአማን ፣ ኢኮነ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ የአምር ፡ መቃ ብሪሁ ፤ ለሙሴ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዘቀበሮ ፣ ወኢኮነ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ የአምር ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ኤልያ ስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ዘአዕረጎ ። ወኢይክል ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያሕይዎሙ ፤፡ እሙታን ፡ዘእንበ ለ ፡ እግዚአ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ስሉጥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ።
of Our Lord. The prophets and the apostles rejoiced at his ascent of the mount (Tabor). The prophets rejoiced to * see his Incarnation, which they did not know. The apostles also rejoiced to hear the word of the Father, which they had never heard, and (which they) were hearing this time. By the word of the Father they became certain of the Incarnation of the Son, which was hidden from them. At the moment they heard the word of the Father, the glory of the Incarnation (of the Son) appeared (to them). The apostles knew that it was Our Lord Jesus Christ who had raised the soul of Moses, and who had brought Elijah up (to the heavens). Indeed, no one truly knew the tomb of Moses, except (the angel) who had buried him; no one, (either), knew (the place) where Elijah was, except Him who had brought him up (to the heavens); no one (finally) could raise them from the dead, except the Lord of all (things), that is to say Him who has power over heaven and earth.
ወኮነ ፡ ደብረ ፦ ታቦር ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ቤት ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወአስተጋብአ ፣፤ እግዚእነ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ፪ሕግ ፡ ብሊት ፡ ወሐጻዳስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ”ቃለ ፡ አብ ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ወኮኑ፣፡ከ. መ፣፤ምውታን ። ወተመይጠ ፡፤ ሙሴ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሪሁ ፡ ወዓርገ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ሰ ማይ ። ወእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፡
Mount Tabor is the symbol of the Church. Our Lord gathered there the two Laws: the Old and the New. When (the apostles) heard * the word of the Father, they fell (to the ground), and became as dead. Moses

ኮነ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፦ ወነቢያት ፡ ደንገፁ ፣ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ወድቱ ፡ በገጾሙ ። እስመ ፦፡ ኡብ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ስመ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝህልው ፣ ዘለዓለም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወበእ ንተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ሰበኩ ፣፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ፣ ወተአ ምራቲሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፣ ዘበአማን ፣ ወሥጋ፣ ዘለብሰ ፡ እምዘመደ ፡ እጓለ ፣ እመሕያው ፤ ውእቱ ። ወወንጌል ፡ ሰበከ ፣ በመድኃኒነ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ልአመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ለማርያም ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ምስሌኪ ። ወለእመ ፣ ኢኮነ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘሰከበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጎል ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ለመኑ ፡ ሰብሑ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወረዱ ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ በፅለተ ፡ ል ደቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፣፤ ስብሐት ፣፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በአርያም ፡ ወሰላም ፡፣ በምድር ፡ ሥ ምረቱ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፣ ሥጋ ፣፡ መኑ ፡ ዘአጥመቆ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በዮርዳኖስ ። ወለአመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፣ አምላክ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘአንሥኦ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፣ እመቃብር ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንትአመን ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ወሰብእ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ አሐዱ ፡ገጽ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ውላጤ ፣ ወዘእንበ
returned to his tomb, and Elijah went back up to heaven. Our Lord Jesus Christ remained alone. Indeed, the word (of the Father) was made (to be heard) because of him. The prophets were terrified, and the apostles fell on their faces, for the Father had revealed the name of his Son, (and had said) that (the Son) existed eternally with his Father. Because of this word, the apostles preached that (the Son) was true God, and that his miracles were the proof that he was true God. (He is also true man, by the flesh) with which he clothed himself, (taking it) from the family of men. The Gospel preaches Our Savior, and says: If he did not become flesh, why did Gabriel say to Mary: The Lord is with thee? If he did not become flesh, who (then) lay in a manger? If he is not God, whom did the angels glorify, when they descended from heaven, the day of his birth, saying: Glory to the Lord in the highest (of heavens) and peace on earth to men of good will? If he did not become flesh, whom did John baptize in the Jordan? If he is not God, who raised Lazarus from the tomb? He is the Christ, the only-begotten Son, the Word of the Lord. We confess that he is perfect God and perfect man, one single person, without change, and without separation.
ለ፣ፍልጠት። ወዕበ ፣ ወድቁ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ ከመ ፡ ምውታን ፡ ገሰሶሙ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ማሕየዊት ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፤ ተንሥኡ ፣ ወኢትፍርሁ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወአንሥኡ ፣ አፅይንቲሆሙ ፡፣ ወኢርእዩ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ባ
When the apostles had fallen to the ground, and were as dead, Our Lord touched them with his life-giving hand, and said to them: Arise and do not

fear. They arose immediately, lifted their eyes, and saw only Our Lord Christ alone. To Him be glory, honor, and adoration with his good Father and the life-giving Holy Spirit! May his clemency and his mercy be
ሰላም ፡ ለታቦር ፣ እንተ ፣ ተሰምየ ፣ ወተጸውዓ ፡=፡ ደብረ ፡ ጥሉለ ፡ ወደበረ ፡ "ርጉዓ ፡፡ ነ ባርቅ ፡ ሞአ ፣ በሀየ ፡ ወኃይል ፣ ሷሳራ ፣፡ ተሞአ ፡፡ ወቦቱ ፡ አሪጎ ፣ አመ ፣፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ተሰብአ ፡= ምሥጢረ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ዳግም ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ገ

It is there that Barak triumphed and that the troops of Sisera were defeated. By ascending Tabor, while he had become man, Jesus Manifested the hidden mystery of his second coming.]
ሰላም ፡ ለዕርገትከ ፡ ዓቀበ ፡ ደብረ ፣ ታፀር ፣ ጽምወ ፡ እምብዙኃን ፡ ነሚአከክ ፡ እለ ፣ ኃረይከ ፡ ፅደወ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ዘኮንከ ፣ እምቤተ ፣ ይሁዳ ፡ ሥግወ ፡፡ ርእየተ ፡ ገጽከ ፡ አምሳለ ፣፡ መብረቅ ፣ ሐተወ ፡፡ ወከመ ፡ በረድ ፡ ልብስከ ፡ ፃዕደወ ፡፡
[Greeting to thy ascent on the slope of Mount Tabor, (in) the calm! When thou hadst taken the men whom thou hadst chosen from among many, Jesus, thou who didst become incarnate from the house of Judah, The appearance of thy face shone like lightning, And thy garments became white as snow.]

[On this day also is the martyrdom (of) Saint Afra, and the birth of the prophet Moses. May their blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.] [On this day also died Abba Galyon, the ascetic. This saint was an ascetic. He did not cease to pray, day and night. He (took) food only once, each week. Since he had become a monk, while he was a young man, he did not cross the door of the enclosure (of the convent), until he had become an old man. None of the brothers saw him, except at the time of prayer. When Satan had seen his asceticism, he took the appearance of monks. (These) came to him, in the middle of the night, and said to him: "We are retired solitaries. As one of us has died, we want to take thee with us, and we have come to thee (for that)." It seemed to him that their word was true. He went

with them, until they had brought him * above a deserted mountain, where there was no food, nor water. When he saw them mocking him, he realized that they were demons. He made the sign of the cross on his face, and they dispersed (far) from him. When he was returning, (going) from one side to the other, he no longer found a path by which to go. He made a long prayer. Then, there appeared to him holy monks from the convent of Abba Sinoda, who were traveling from monastery to monastery. They were reciting the psalms of David. They questioned him about his story. When he had told them his story, they took him with them. He remained an entire year living with them on fish cooked in the sun. Abba Isaac, his master, who had made him a monk, when he no longer had news of him, asked the Lord to show him the face of (Galyon). Abba Galyon, having known it in spirit, went to him. When (Abba Isaac) saw him, he rejoiced, and said to him: "Where hast thou stayed?" He told him everything from the beginning to the end. Then, at the moment of his departure (from this world), the brothers came to him, shrouded him, and buried him. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for ever and ever! Amen.] [Greeting to Abba Galyon, who practiced a rigorous asceticism, By not going out at all from the enclosure of the monastery! When the troops of Satan * wanted to make him go out from this (place),


[p. 312] {#po.9.p312}
እንዘ ፡ የዓትብ ፡ ሳዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ መልክን ፡፡ ተፃብዖቶሙ ፡ በመስቀል ፡ ሞአ ፡
By making (the sign) of the cross over them and (over) his own person, He defeated by the cross their attack.]
ሰላም ፡ ለብንያም ፡ በልብሰ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ "ዑጹፍ ፡፡፡ ወበወርቀ ፡ ክህነት ፡ ግሉፍ ፡፡ አንበይነ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ጽሩይ ፡ ወሕሊናሁ ፡ ንጡፍ ፡፡ ኅበ ፡ ይሬእዮሙ ፡ ለኃይላተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ አአላፍ ፡=፡ እምህላዌሁ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፣ ይትመሰጥ ፡ ለዘልፍ ፡፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡
[Greeting to Benjamin, * clothed in (his) liturgical vestments, And chiseled in the gold of (his) priestly (garments)! Because of his pure heart and his immaculate intelligence, When he saw the Virtues of heaven by the thousands, He was enraptured forever from his bodily existence.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ለነሐሴ ። ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 14TH OF NAHASÉ (August 20).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ አባ ፣፡ ስምዖን ፣ ሣሉሲ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፣ ወእሉ ፡-ቅ ዱሳን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ምእመን ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ አበዊሆሙ ፡፣ አብዕ ልተ ፡ ወክቡራነ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ለበዐለ ፣ መስቀል ፣፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግዱ ፣ ውስተ ፣ መክናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ወፈጺሞሙ ፡ በፀለ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ተፅዒኖሙ ፣
[On this day died Abba Simeon Salousi and John, his beloved. These saints (lived) under the reign of Justus, the faithful king. Their parents were rich and noble. They set out for Jerusalem, for the feast of the cross, in order to adore it at the Holy Places. Having finished the feast, while they were returning


[p. 313] {#po.9.p313}
ላዕለ ፡ አፍራሲሆሙ ፡ ቀርቡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢያሪሆ ፣ ወርእዮ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፡ አድባራተ ፣ብሐቅ ለ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፣ ዘመነኮሳት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለስምዖን ፡ ኦእተየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሉ ፡ አ ድባራት ፡የሐድሩ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ንክል ፡ ከ መ ፡ ንርአዮሙ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ አመሰ ፡ ኮነ ፣፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ እወ ፣ ንሬከዮሙ ፤ ወእምዝ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ አፍራሷሆሙ ፡ ወወሀብዎሙ ፡ ለወዓልያኒሆሙ ፡ ወይቤ ልዎሙ ፡ ሑሩ ፡ በበንስቲት ፡ እስከ ፡ ንረክበክሙ ፡ ወረሰዩ ፡ ርአሶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙን
to their country, having mounted their horses, they approached Jericho. John, having caught sight of convents of monks in the desert of the Jordan, said to Simeon: "O my brother, it is in these convents that the angels of the Lord dwell." Simeon said to him: "Will we be able to see them?" John said to him: "If we (stay) with them, certainly, we will see them." Then, they dismounted from their horses. They gave them to their servants, and said to them: "Go slowly, until we rejoin you." They themselves pretended to satisfy a natural need.
ቱ ፡ ይፌጽሙ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ዘሥጋ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርቡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ተባሀሉ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ንኡ ፡ ንግበር ፡ጸ ሎተ ። ወይቁም ፡ ፳እምኔነ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ፍኖተ ፡ ሀገርነ ፡ ወ8አምኔነ ፡ ይቁም ፡ ውስተ ፍኖት ፡ እንተ ፡ ታበጽሕ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፣ መነኮሳት ፡ ወንትአፀው ፡ ዕፃ ፣ ወኀበ ፡ ሠም ረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንሖር ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ቆመ ፡፣ ስምዖን ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ፍኖተ ፣ ዮርዳኖስ ፣ወ ዮሐንስ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ሰብኦሙ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ተዓፀዉ ፡ ወወ ፅአ ፡ ዕፃ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ስምዖን ፣፡ ወተሐቀፉ ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፣ ወተሰዓሙ ፡ ወሖሩ፣ በፍኖተ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወ፪፳ይጌሥጽ ፡ ካልኦ ፡ ወያተግሆ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ገ ቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወይፈርህ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይግባእ ፡ ቢጹ ፡ ድኅሪተ ፣ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ይፈርህ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምዖን ፣ ኢያገብኦ ፡ ድኅሪተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አበዊሁ ፣ ወስምዖንሂ ፡፣ ይፈርህ ፡ በ
When they were near the road to the Jordan, both said to each other: "Let us go and make a prayer. Let one of us stand on the road to our country, and let the other of us stand on the road that leads to the convent of the monks! Let us cast lots and go where the Lord wills." Then, Simeon stood on the road to the Jordan, and John stood on the road by which their men were going. When they had cast the lots, the lot fell on the road of Simeon. They embraced, and kissed one another. They went off on the road to the Jordan, rejoicing. One encouraged the other, and excited him to the accomplishment of the good (resolution); (one) feared that his companion might turn back. John feared, concerning Simeon, that the love of his parents might make him turn back; Simeon, for his part, feared

እንተ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አውሰበ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ብእሲተ ፣ ላህይተ ፣ ወባዕል ተ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ኦእግዚእነ ፡ ወአምላክነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘተሐርዮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንመንኩስ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ይከውነነ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ንረክብ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ደብር ፡ ርኅወ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ደብር ፡ ትሩፈ ፡ ዘይብልቻ ፡ ኒቆን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተአም ራተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወተውሀቦ ፡ ሀብተ ፣ ትንቢት ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእ ሲ ፡ ዘይብሎ ፡ አርሁ ፣ አንቀጸ ፡፣ ደብር ፣ ከመ ፣ ይባኡ ፡ አባግፅየ ። ወዕበ ፣ በጽሑ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሠናይኬ ፤ ምጽአቶሙ ፡ ለአባግዓ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ተወክ ፎሙ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አለ ፡ ተፈነዉ ፥ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ደብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይላጺ ፣፡ ርእሶሙ ፡ ወያልብሶሙ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ እስመ” ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ለ፳መነኮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእሱ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወመላእክ ት ፡ የዓውድዎ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ጽሕቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመንኩሱ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፡ ዕበ ፣ አልበሶሙ ፡ሊቀ ፡ ደቭር ፣ አስኬማ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ በ”ጸጋ ፣ መንፈ ስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይሬእዩ ፣፤ በሌሊት ፣ ገጻቲሆሙ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ዘ ይትረአዩ ፣ በመዐልት ፡ ወርእዩ ፣ አክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ላዕለ ፣ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ር እዩ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ርአሰ ፡ መነኮስ ።
concerning John, for (John) had married, these days, a pretty and rich woman. Then, they prayed, and said: "O Our Lord and Our God, the convent that thou hast chosen for us to become monks in, may we (recognize) it by the (following) sign: the convent whose door we shall find open." The superior of this convent, who was called Nigon, was virtuous; he performed many miracles, and the gift of prophecy was granted to him. He saw, that night, a man who was saying to him: "Open the door of the convent, so that my sheep may enter." When (Simeon and John) arrived near him, he said to them: "Welcome to the sheep of Christ!" Then he received them with him, as people who were sent from the Lord. They asked the superior of the convent to shave their heads and to clothe them in monastic habits. Indeed, * they had seen A monk who had on his head a crown of light, and (whom) the angels surrounded. That is why they desired to become monks quickly. The next day, when the superior of the convent had clothed them in the holy scapular (schema), their faces shone with * the grace of the Holy Spirit, to the point that they saw, at night, their faces mutually, as if they were seeing them by day, and that they saw a crown of light on their heads, as they had seen (one) on the head of a monk.


[p. 315] {#po.9.p315}
ወእምዝ ፡ መጽኦሙ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈለጡ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ መነኮሳ ት ፡ ወይፃኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ብእሲ ፣ ብርሃናዊ ፡ ለ ሊቀ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አርጐ ፣ አንቀጸ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፃኡ ፡ አባግዓ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። ወነቂ ሆ፣፡ወረደ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ደብር ፡ ርኅወ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐዝን ፡ ወይቴክዝ ፣ ናሁ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ አግብርተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡፣ ይፃኡ ፡ ወርእየ ፣ በቅድሜሆ ሙ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ሕፅዋን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውሩ ፡ መኃትወ ፡ ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ዘይጸውር ፡ አብትረ ፡ ዘነገሥት ፡ ወሶበ ፣ ርአዮሙ ፣ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ጥቀ ፣ ወተራከቦሙ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ዘ ሐለዩ ፡ በልቦሙ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ ጸሎተ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ በከየ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ነ ዋኃ ፡ ወሜጠ ፡ ገጾ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወአቀሞ ፡ ለስምዖን ፣ በየማኑ ፡፣ ወለዮሐን ስ ፡ በፀጋሙ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡፣ እደዊሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ጸሎተ ። ወአማኅፀኖ ሙ ፡ ኅበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፣ ይዕቀቦሙ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አኩይ ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ በሰ
Then, a spiritual thought came to them: to separate themselves from the midst of the monks, and to set out for the desert. That night, a luminous man appeared to the superior of the convent, and said to him: "Open the door, so that the sheep of Christ may go out." Having awakened, he went down immediately, and he found the door of the convent open. While he was saddened and afflicted, behold, the servants of Christ came, wanting to leave. He saw before them as it were eunuchs carrying torches; one of them was carrying the royal scepter. When he saw (Simeon and John), he rejoiced greatly; he went to meet them. They told him what they had resolved in their hearts, and they asked him to make a prayer for them. Then, he wept for long hours, turned his face toward the East, placed Simeon at his right and John at his left, lifted his hands to heaven, and made a prayer. He put them under the protection of the Lord, so that he might protect them from all evil, and he sent them off in peace.
ላም፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ምውት ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፣ አርኖን ፤ወረከቡ ፡ በዓተ ፡ዘኮነ ፣ የኃድር ፡ ውስቴታ ፣ አረጋዊ ፡ ገዳማዊ ፣ ወአዕረፈ፣ በይእቲ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወቦ ፡ ውስቴታ ፣ ዘይፈቅድዎ ፣ አቅማሐተ ፡ እክል ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ አረጋ ዊ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወተፈሥሑ ፡፣ ጥቀ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ሎሙ-0፡
Then they went to the side of the Dead Sea and arrived at the river called the Arnon. They found a cave in which an old hermit had lived, (who) had died in those days, and in which there were vegetable provisions, which they needed, (vegetables) on which the old man fed. They rejoiced greatly in the Lord, who had prepared this for them


[p. 316] {#po.9.p316}
ዘንተ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ በተጋድሎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የሐድር ፡ ርጉቀ ፡ እምነ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ሙጋረ ፡ እብን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይትቃተሎሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ኒቂዮስ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ በራእይ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፣ ወይሜ ህሮሙ ፤ መዝሙረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ንውማነ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይነቅሑ ፡ ያነብቡ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘ መሐሮሙ ፡ ወይትፌሥ”ሑ ፡ ዋቀ ፡ ወተውህቦሙ ፡ ራእይ ፡ አምላካዊ ፡ ወጎቢረ ፡ መን ክራት ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ፳ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትዔገሠ ፡ ቀረ ፡ሌ ሊት ፣ ወዋዕየ ፡ መዐልት ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞአዎ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በኃይለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብ ሐት።
(cave). They remained (there), (practicing) asceticism, for many days. One lived far from the other, the distance of a stone's throw. Satan fought much against them. Saint Niqyos, their father, came to them in a vision; he prayed for them, and he taught them the psalms while they were asleep. When they awoke, they recited everything he had taught them, and they rejoiced greatly. (The grace) of divine visions and the accomplishment of miracles was granted to them. They remained in this desert for twenty-nine years, enduring the cold of the night and the heat of the day, until they had defeated Satan through the power of Christ - glory to Him!
ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ለእሁ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፡ ምንተ ፣ ንረብሕ ፡ በንብረትነ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ነዓ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ንብቀሩፆሙ ፣ ወናድኅኖሙ ፡ለ ከልአን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኦእጉየ ፣ እስመ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ሀልዮ ፣ ይመስለኒ ፡ እምቅንዓ ተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ እስመ ፣ አግዚአብሔር ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሳለቅ ፣፡ ለዓለም ፡ ነዓ ፡ ንጸሊ ፣ ጸሎተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለዩ ፣ ወተሐቀፉ ፡ ወበከዩ ፡ እስከ ፡ አር ሐሱ ፤ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፻ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹ ሊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅባእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመውት ።
Then, Simeon said to his brother John: "What do we gain during our stay in the desert? Come: let us go into the world, so as to be useful to others and to save them." John said to him: "O my brother, behold, this thought seems to me (to come) from the jealousy of Satan." Simeon said to him: "It is that the Lord has ordered me to mock the world. Come: let us pray." Then, they prayed, embraced, and wept, to the point of wetting their clothes. Simeon went away to Jerusalem. He remained for three days asking the Lord to hide his story until he was dead.
ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወረሰየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ አብደ ፡ ቦ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አመ ፡ ይፌ
Then he went into the city and acted the fool. Sometimes, he healed the mad; sometimes,

he carried fire in his hands. He found a dead dog at the gate (of the city); he dragged it by his belt as if he were playing (with it), so that people insulted him, saying: "(This) monk is mad," and they ran after him and slapped him. On the first day (of the week), that is to say the day of Sunday, he took nuts, entered the church at the moment of the Sacrifice, broke the lamps, and struck the women, so that he was expelled from the church. Sometimes, he took women as if he were sleeping with them, so that their husbands beat him. Then, when the moment of his death was near, the Lord ordered his angel to announce to him the day of his death, as well as (that of) the death of John, his brother. He placed himself at the foot of a vine and delivered his soul into the hand of the Lord, as did John, his brother. May their blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.
ሰላም ፣ ለስምዖን ፣ ዘተመሰለ ፡ አብደ ፡፡ ወለዮጠንስ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ዘተፀምደ ።፡ ዕበ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡፡ እመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርአሶሙ ፡ ዘዖደ ፡፡ አክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ነጸሩ ፡ ገሃደ ።፡
* [Salutation to Simeon, who feigned madness, And to John, his beloved, with whom he practiced devotion! When they had entered into the monastic life, as their heart desired, They saw clearly a crown of light, Which, thanks to the Holy Spirit, was around their head.]

On this day the Lord performed a great miracle in the city of Alexandria. Thus, many Jews believed through the intermediary of Saint Abba Theophilus, patriarch of the city of Alexandria, (and) brother of the mother of Saint Cyril. Here is (the fact): there was in the city of Alexandria a Jew, extremely rich, called Philoxenus. He feared the Lord and practiced the Law of Moses according to his means. There were (also) in the city of Alexandria two poor Christians, who were wage-earners for the work of their hands. Satan made a thought of blasphemy come into the heart of one of them, (who) said to his companion: "O my brother, why are we ourselves, (who) worship Christ, poor, while the Jew Philoxenus is extremely rich?" The companion of (the latter) replied, saying to him: "O my brother, know that the goods of this world are nothing before the Lord. If the goods of this world were held in esteem by the Lord, He would not give them to those who worship idols, to the debauched, to robbers, and to murderers. The prophets, themselves, were poor and persecuted, * as were the apostles. Our Lord said: * My brothers, these are the poor."

Satan, the enemy of good, did not permit this man to accept any of the

words (of his companion), but he agitated him. (This man) went to the Jew Philoxenus, asked him, and said to him: "Allow me to serve you." The latter replied and said to him: "(No one) must serve me, except he who professes my religion and (who) is (one) of my kin. If you want alms, I will give them to you; (then), go away." The poor man replied and said to him: "Take me into your house, (for) I myself will enter into your religion, and I will do everything you order me." The Jew Philoxenus replied to him and said to him: "Be patient with me until I have taken counsel with my master." The Jew went away and told his master the story of this Christian. The master said to him: "If he has apostatized his religion, and (if he) has apostatized his Messiah, receive him and circumcise him." The Jew returned and told this Christian what his master had said to him. The poor man accepted this word. (The Jew) took him and led him to the synagogue (of the Jews). The leader (of the Jews) asked this poor Christian before all the Jews: "Do you truly deny your Messiah and become a Jew?" He said to him: "Yes." This wretch and this miserable man apostatized Our Lord Christ, our God, before the Jews, and added to his indigence of the goods (of this world) the indigence of faith.
ኖት። ወአዘዘ ፡፣ ሊቆሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዘዕፅ፣ ወገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ወወሀብዎ ፡ ሕለተ ፡ ወዲቤሃ ፡ ስፍንግ ፡ እንተ ፡ ምል እት ፡ ብሒአ ፡ ወክኦ፡ናተ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ትፋእ ፣ ምራቀ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወአ ቅርብ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ብሒአ ፡ ወርግዞ ፡ በዛቲ ፣ ኩናት ፡ ”ወበሎ ፡ ናሁ ፣ ረገዝኩከ ፡ ኦክርስቶስ ። ወነሥኦሙ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕጉል ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዝዎ ። ወዕበ ፡ ረገዞ ፡ በእዴሁ ፣፡ ርግምት ፡ ለመስቀል ፣ ክቡር ፡ ውሕዘ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ደም ፡ ወማ ይ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡እስከ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ነዊኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወርድ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ወየብሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ እብን ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ላዕለ ኩሎሙ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ ዓቢይ ፡፤ ወጸርጉ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ። አሐዱ፣ ውእቱ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ነአምን ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ሊቆ ሙ ፤ እምውእቱ ፡ ደም ፡ ወዓተበ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ዘኮነት ፣፡ ዕውር ተ ፡ ወርእየት ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወአምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ እ ምነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ቴዎፊሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወ
The leader of the Jews ordered that a cross be made for him, like (the cross) of wood (of Christ). They did as he had ordered. They gave (to this wretch) a reed, at the end (of which) was a sponge full of vinegar, and a lance, and they said to him: "Spit on this cross, offer it the vinegar, pierce it with this lance, * and say to it: 'Behold, I have pierced you, O Christ.'" This miserable man took these (objects) and did as he had been ordered. When he had pierced the glorious cross with his cursed hand, blood and water flowed from it in abundance, to the point of spreading on the earth and continuing, for a long time, to spread. Immediately, this apostate fell, died, and became dry like a stone. A great fear came upon all the Jews. They all cried out, saying: "One is the God of the Christians. We ourselves believe in Him." Then the leader (of the Jews) took some of this blood and signed with it the eyes of his daughter, who was blind; she saw immediately. He believed, as did all the people of his house and many among the Jews. Then they went to Saint Abba Theophilus, patriarch, and they recounted what had happened


[p. 320] {#po.9.p320}
አይድዕፅዎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ። ወተንሥአ ፡ አባ ፣ ቴዎፊሎስ ፣ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ወ

priests and laymen. They went to the synagogue of the Jews. The patriarch saw the cross: blood and water were flowing from (the cross). Saint Theophilus took some of this blood, with (which) he blessed (himself), and made the sign of the cross on his forehead and (on) the forehead of all the people. * He ordered that this cross be carried in great pomp and (amidst) chants, until it had been brought to the church and deposited there. He sponged the blood from the earth and put it in a vial for the blessing and healing of the sick. Then, Philoxenus followed the patriarch, (he) as well as all the people of his house with him (and) many Jews. They confessed before (the patriarch) Our Lord Christ, whom their ancient fathers had crucified. Then he baptized them with the Christian baptism in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, made them participate with him in the office, and gave them the holy Mysteries. They went away to their houses, rejoicing, giving thanks to Our Lord Christ, and glorifying Him, He to whom belong glory and honor for ever and ever. Amen.

To the great miracle (of the cross), So that many Jews have believed through it! The cross of the Son made blood flow, From (the place) where a villain and an apostate had pierced it.]

[On this day also died Demyat, martyr (and) wonder-worker, who was stoned with his disciples. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.]

[I say salutation to the great miracles (of Demyat), He who was the healer of the blind, and who made those whose (limb) was broken rise from their bed! Salutation to Demyat with his good disciples! His house became for him a tomb forever, For he was stoned there.]

[On this day also is the martyrdom of Saint Basligos. While this saint was in prison, Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - appeared to him,

ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሑር ፡ ተፋነዎሙ ፡ ለአዝማዲከ ፡ እስመ ፡ናሁ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ስምዕከ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአሎሙ ፡ ለሐራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወሐዊሮ፣፡ም ስሌሆሙ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤቱ ፡፣ "ወቤተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ያስተፋንዎሙ ፡ ለእሙ ፡፣ ወለአዝ- ማዲሁ ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፣ አምጽእዖዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ባስሊቆስ ፣ ወአሰርዎ ፡ በ፪አዕማድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዘብጥዎ ፡ ወገብሩ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ቅትራተ ፡ዘብርት ፡ ወአስተሰአንዎዖ ፡ እስከ ፡ በረረ ፡ ቅት ራት ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወውኅዘ ፥ ደሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ዘርእዮ ፡ ይበ ኪ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አሰርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ፅፅ ፣ ይቡስ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይትጋፍዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልክፉ ፡ ዘፈረ ፡ ልብሱ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ርእዩ ፣ ተአምራተ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ በላዕለ ፣ ድውያ
and said to him: "Go say goodbye to your parents, for behold, the moment of your martyrdom has arrived." Then, he asked the soldiers to go with him. Having left with them, he entered his house, * and he spent the night saying goodbye to his mother and his parents. The next day, (the soldiers) took Saint Basligos and tied him to two columns, striking him. They made nails of brass for him and nailed (the soles of his feet), so that the nails went through his feet and his blood flowed to the ground. All those who saw him wept over him. Then he was tied to a dry tree. People jostled to touch the fringe of his garment, when they had seen the miracles he performed on many sick people.
ን ፣፣ብዙኃን፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ሀገረ ፣ ዋርሲኖስ ፣ በሐመር ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ብ ላዕ ፡ እክለ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢትሙት ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ "ብፁዕ ፡ ባስሊቆስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ጽጉብ፡እ» መብልዕ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ወኢያበድር ፡ መዋቴ ፣ መብልዓ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ አብጽሕጦዖ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውዕ ፣ ለአማልክት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ባስሊቆስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እሠውዕ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለአብ ፣ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ። ወእምዝ ፣ አዘዘ ፣መ ኩንን ፡ ያብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አማልክቲሁ ፤ ወቀዊሞ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግ
Then, he was brought in a ship to the city of Warsinos. The soldiers said to him: "Eat some food, so as not to die." * The blessed Basligos said to them: "As for me, I am satisfied with heavenly food, and I will not prefer mortal food (to it)." The next day, they brought him to the judge. (The judge) said to him: "Sacrifice to the gods." Basligos said to him: "As for me, I offer the sacrifice of glory to the Father, to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit." Then, the judge ordered that he be made to enter the temple of his gods. Standing (in the
