temple), the saint prayed to the Lord (so ardently) that fire descended from heaven and consumed the idols. The judge, frightened, fled and went outside. He was filled with anger and ordered him to be killed. Saint Basligos made many prayers and acts of thanksgiving to the Lord. They cut off his head. We saw his soul, while many angels were making it ascend (to the heavens). Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - called him (from the height) of heaven and said to him: "Come, ascend (toward me), Basligos, for I am not a liar; I will accomplish for you everything I have said." It is thus that he finished his martyrdom. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for ever and ever! Amen.]
ሰላም ፡ ለባስሊቆስ ፡ ዘአርአየ ፡ መንክረ ፡=፡ በአስርዖ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ይቡስ ፡ እንተ ፣ በዲቤሁ ፡ ተዓሥረ ፤ ከዕበኒ ፡ ድኅረ ፣ በሰይፍ ፣ ተመትረ ፡=፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዘርኡ ፡ አተረ ፡፡ በቁለ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፣ ወፈረየ ፡ ሥሙረ ፡
[Salutation to Basligos, who made a prodigy seen, By making the dry tree on which he was tied grow! Again also, after he had (his head) severed by the sword, One day when beans were being sown at his tomb, They grew immediately and produced abundant (fruits).]


[p. 325] {#po.9.p325}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። › አመ ፡ ፲ወ፳ ፡ ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God. READING OF THE 15TH OF NAHASE (August 21).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ክርስጢና ፡ ወለተ ፡ ርባኖስ ፣፡ መኩንን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ያመልክ ፣ ጣዖተ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ተአጥን ፣" ሎሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ መሐራ ፡ አቡ ሃ ፤ ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልባ ፣ ፍርሃተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወተመይጠት ፡ መንዝለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወሰአለት ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይምርሐ ፡ ፍኖተ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ወመሐራ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሚነ ፡ ሥላሴ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ አቡሃ፣ይ ቤላ ፡ እፎ ፡ ሀሎኪ ፡ ወለትየ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ በሕይወተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ ወደንገፀ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ምንት ፡ ወለጠ ፡ ልበኪ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ አስመ ፡ተ
[On this day is the martyrdom of Saint Christina, daughter of the judge Rebanos. Her father worshipped idols; she herself * offered them incense, as her father had taught her. One day, the fear of the Lord entered her heart. She turned toward the East and asked (God) to lead her in the way of the kingdom of heaven. The Holy Spirit taught her faith in the Trinity. When her father came, he said to her: "How do you find yourself, my daughter?" She said to him: "I find myself in the life of Christ - glory to Him!" Her father was terrified. He said to her: "What has changed your heart?" She said to him: "It is that I have been taught by the God of heaven."
መሐርኩ ፡ እምአምሳከ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃዝን ፡ ወተንሚአ ፡ ጸለየት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ክርስጢ ና ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወይቤ ላ ፡ ጽንዒ ፡ አስመ ፡ ሀለወኪ ፡ ትትኩነኒ ፡ እም፻ደመኳንንት ፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐተማ ፡ መልአ ከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በማኅተመ ፡ መስቀሉ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወ መጠዋ ፡ እምሥጢር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሕብስተ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ወቦአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አማል ክት ፣ ወቀጥቀጠቶሙ ። ወርአዮ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይቅሥፍዋ ፡ ለወለቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወፅ
Then, her father went away, being sad. Saint Christina, having arisen, prayed to the Lord. Immediately, the angel of the Lord came to her and said to her: "Be strong, for you will have to be tortured by three judges." Then the angel of the Lord signed her with the sign of the cross of Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - and gave her the holy Mystery: the Bread of Life. She entered the temple of the (false) gods and broke them. Her father, having seen (this), ordered his daughter to be flogged. (It was done),


[p. 326] {#po.9.p326}
አ ፡ አምሥጋሃ ፡ መዐር ፡ ሀየንተ ፣ ደም ። ወካዕበ ፣ አስከብዋ ፡ በዓራተ ፡ ሐጺን ፣ ወአ ንደዱ ፡ ታሕቴሃ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘለከፋ ፡ ወአሳትሰ ፡ አውዓየ ፡ እምነ ፡ አረሚ ፡፻ ፳ወእ”ምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ይውግርዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ በራግናት ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ት ፡ህየ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ምስለ ፣ መላእ ክቲሁ ፣ አጥመቃ ፡ ወመጠዋ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ በጐጠተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሞ ፡ ለክርስ ቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣፡ ወአንጽሐ ፣ እምርሥሐተ ፣፡ አማልክት ።
to the point that honey came out of her flesh in place of blood. Furthermore, she was made to lie on a bed of iron, and fire was lit under her; nothing touched her. But the fire consumed 30,000 pagans. * Then, her father ordered her to be thrown into the sea on a raft. When (the saint) had prayed there, Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - came with his angels (and) baptized her. Michael gave her, with tongs of fire, the body and blood of Christ - glory to Him! - and purified her from the defilement of the (false) gods.
ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡፣ ሞተ ፡ ርባኖስ ፣ አቡሃ ፣ ወመጽአ ፡ ካልእ ፣ መኩንን ፣ ዘስ ሙ ፡ ድዮስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ያምጽእዋ ፡ ለቅድስት ፣ ክርስጢና ፣ ወይስቅልዋ ፡ ወይቅስፍዋ ፡ ወያንድዱ ፡ እሳተ ፡፣ ታሕቴሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእያሃ ፡ አንስት ፡ ፅራቃ ፣ ረገማሁ ፣፡ ለመኩን ን ፡ ወኪያሆንሂ ፡ መተረ ፡ ርእሶን ፣ በሰይፍ ፣ ወለይእቲሰ ፣ ወደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ፣ ሞቅሕ ፣ ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ መጠነ ያ፳ወድዮስኒ ፡ ዓላዊ ፣፡ ሞተ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ሣልስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ዘስሙ ፡ ዮልያስ ፡ ወአኃ ዛ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ ክርስጢና ፡ ወአገበራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሥዕ ፡ ለአማልክት ። ወሶበ ፡ ረገመቶ ፡ ወደያ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘፍሉሕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አረር ፡ ወመዐረ ፡ ግራ ፣ ወአልቦ፣ ዘለከፋ ። ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ "ይግድፍዋ ፣ ለአራዊተ ፡ ምድር ፣ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ለሐሱ ፡ጸ በለ ፡ እገሪሃ ፣ ወነሰክዎ ፣ ለመሠግረ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ወሞተ ። ወክከዕበ ፡ ሰተርዋ ፣ ወመተሩ ፣
That night, her father Rebanos died. Another judge, called Deyos, came; he ordered Saint Christina to be brought, to be crucified, to be flogged, and for fire to be lit under her. When women saw her naked, they cursed the judge; he cut off their heads with the sword. As for the (saint), she was thrown into prison. Having seen her, the people believed in Christ - glory to Him! - (to the number of) about 30,000 (people). Deyos, the impious one, died. A third judge, called Yolyas, came; he took Saint Christina and forced her to sacrifice to the (false) gods. When she had cursed him, he threw her into a furnace of fire (full) of boiling lead and wax; nothing touched her. Furthermore, he ordered * her to be thrown to the serpents. But these licked the dust from her feet and bit the snake charmer, who


[p. 327] {#po.9.p327}
አጥባቲሃ ፡ ወልሳና ፣ ወነሥአት ፡ ምታረ ፡ ልሳና ፡ ወገደፈት ›፣ ውስተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ለ መኩንን ፡ ወአዖረቶ ፡ ወመሊኦ ፡ መፀሀተ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ይግድፍዋ ፡ ለአራዊተ ፡-ምድር ፡አ ሐቲ ፡ነሰከታ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልባ ፡ ወአሐቲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቦሃ ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ አዕረፈት ፣ በፈቃ ዴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወተከሥተ ፣ እመቃብሪሃ ፡ ተአምራት ፣ ወመንክራት ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቀኑ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡. ሰላም ፣ ለክርስጢና ፡ ዘአጥመቃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ወተሀበያ ፡ መልአኩ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ክቡር ፡፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወንጽሕት ፡ እምነውር ።፡ ያምዕርር ፡ ጐርዔየ ፡ አምስቴ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ መሪር ፡፡
died. Furthermore, she was flayed and her breasts and tongue were cut off. She took the piece of her tongue and threw it into the eyes of the judge, (whom she rendered) blind. Filled with anger, he ordered her to be thrown to the serpents. One (serpent) bit her in the heart; another in the side. It is thus that she died, by the will of the Lord. Innumerable miracles and prodigies manifested at her tomb. May the Lord have mercy on us through her prayer for ever and ever!] [Salutation to Christina, whom the Lord baptized, And whose godfather was Michael, her glorious angel! May the honey, which flowed from the flogged body Of this (martyr), holy and pure from (all) stain, Sweeten my throat against the bitter drink of sin!]
እምነ ፡ ሥጋሀ ፡ ቅሠፍ ፡ ዘውሕዘ ፡ መዐር ፡ ሰላም ፡ እብል ፣ ዘክርስጢና ፡ ማኅበራነ ፡=፡ ሰላሰ ፡ ጽ፳መስተቃትላነ ። ምስለ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስሱ ፡ አንስት ፡ አለ ፡ ተለዉ ፡ መድኅነ ፡፡ በዕለተ ፡ ምንዳቤየ ፡ ይሠጠዉኒ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡፡ ዕበ ፡ በስሞሙ ፡፣ አስተበቀዕዕ ፡ አነ ።፡
[I say salutation to the companions of Christina, The 30,000 combatants, As well as to the twelve women who followed the Savior! May they answer me quickly, on the day of my affliction, When I myself will invoke in their name!]


[p. 328] {#po.9.p328}
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አፅረፈት ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ማሪና ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፣ ወለተ ፡ ፳8ብ እሲ ፡ ባዕል ፣ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ወስማ ፣ ለይእቲ ፡ ማርያም ፣ ወሞተት ፡ እማ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለ ወት ፡ ንእስት ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ወሐፀና ፡ አቡሃ ፡ በዥሉ ፡ ትምሀርት ፡ ሠናይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ልህቀ ት ፡ ከመ ፡ አንስት ። ወፈቀድደ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተዋስባ ፡ ወይሖር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይመ ንኩስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳ደብር ፡ እምአድባራተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ እ ፎ ፡ ታድኅን ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ ወታጠፍእ ፡ ነፍስየ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ እፎ ፡ እገብር ፡ በ ኪ ፡ ወአንቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ኦአቡየ ፡ አነ ፣ አአትት ፣ እምላዕሌየ ፡ አልባሰ፣፡ አ ንስት ፡ ወእለብስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡፣ "ዕደው ። ወተንሥአት ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወላጸየት ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ር እሳ ፡ ወለብሰት ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ዕደው ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ጽንዓ ፡ ልባ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትት ጋደል ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፈጽም ፡ ፈቃዳ ፡ ወወሀበ ፣ ኩሎ ፡፣ ንዋዮ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪናን ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ ንስቲተ ። ወህየንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ለወለቱ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወለጠ ፡ ስማ፣ወ ሰመያ ፡ ማሪና ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወወሰዳ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወቦ አ፡ ኅበ ፡ 8ደብር ፡ ወኀደረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ በዓት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወለቱ ፣ መጠነ ፡ ፲ዓመት ፡ እን ዘ፣ይትጋደሉ።
On this day died Saint Marina. This saint was the daughter of a rich Christian. She was called Mary. Her mother died while (the saint) was young. Her father raised her in all (sorts of) good doctrines, until she had become as big as a woman. Her father wanted to marry her off. As for him, (he wanted) to go away and become a monk in one of the convents of the desert of Scetis. She said to him: "O my father, what! you will save your soul, and you will cause my soul to perish!" He replied and said to her: "What shall I do with you? You are a woman." She said to him: "O my father, I myself will take off the clothes of a woman, and I will put on clothes * of a man." Immediately, she arose, shaved the hair of her head, and put on man's clothes. When he had seen the courage of her heart, while she practiced asceticism in order to accomplish her will (to follow him), he gave all his goods to the poor and the unfortunate; he took a small (part of his goods); and, instead of (keeping) the name of his daughter Mary, he changed her name and called her Marina. Then, he took her with him and led her to the desert of Scetis. He entered a convent and lived in a cave with his daughter for ten years, while (both) practiced asceticism.


[p. 329] {#po.9.p329}
ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወተረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ማሪና ፡ ባሕቲታ ። ወ አመክዓበት ፡ ጾማ ፡ ወጸሎታ ፡ ወትግሃታ ። ወአበ ፣ ምኔትሰ ፈነዋ ፡ ለቅድስት ፣ምስለ ፡ ይመነኮሳት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘደብር ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ያአ ምር ፡ ክመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብአሲት ፣ አላ ፣ መሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ድቁቅ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ብዝኃ ፡ ተጸምዶቱ ።፡ ወዕበ ፡ ሖረት ፡ ምስለ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ኅኀደሩ ፣፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ወበይ እቲ፣ዕለት፣መጽአ ፣ ፳ጸአምሐራ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ወኀኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ነግድ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ወለቶ ፡፣ ለበዓለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ነግድ ፡፣ ወአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናሃ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤለኪ ፣ አቡኪ ፣ ምንተ ፡ ኮንኪ ፡ በልዮ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ እንባ ፣ መሬና ፡፣ መነኮስ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አማሰነኒ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፀንሰት ፣ ወአአመረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ተስአላ ፡ ወይ ቤላ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኮንኪ ፡ ኦወለትየ ፡ ወመኑ ፣ "ዘአማሰነ ፣ ድንግልናኪ ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡
Then the father (of the saint, having become) an old man, died. Saint Marina remained alone. She redoubled her fasts, her prayers, and her vigils. The superior of the monastery sent the saint with three monks to the city for the affairs of the convent. He did not know that she was a woman, but it seemed to him that her voice was weak because of the intensity of her asceticism. When she went with the monks, they stayed in an inn. That day, * one of the king's soldiers came and stayed in the inn that night. He saw the innkeeper's daughter, defiled her virginity, and said to her: "When your father says to you: 'What has become of you?' say to him: 'It is Abba Marina, the young monk, who defiled me.'" When she was pregnant, and her father knew it, he asked her and said to her: "What has become of you, my daughter, and what * has defiled your virginity?" She replied and said to him: "It is Abba Marina, the monk, who defiled me."
ወትቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ እንባ ፡ መሬና ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአማሰነኒ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ዕቤሃ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ደብር ፡ ወአኃዘ ፣፡ ይርግሞሙ ፡ለመነኮ ሳት ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጋብአ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ሰአሎ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትረግሞ ሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ወኢትፈርሆኑ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወለ
His father rose immediately, went to the convent, and began to curse the monks. When the superior of the monastery had come to him, he asked him, and said to him: "Why do you curse the monks? Do you not fear the Lord?"


[p. 330] {#po.9.p330}
ቱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ እንባ ፡ መሪና ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናሃ ፡ ለወ ለትየ ። ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሙ ን ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሰአሎ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለበዓለ ፣ ቤተ ፡፣ ነግድ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅባእ ፡ ዘን ተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወኢተስተኃፍሮሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፣ በቅድመ ፡ ሕዝባዊያን ። ወእምዝ ፡-ጸ ውዖ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ለእንባ ፡ መሪና ፣ ወገሠጾ ፡ ወረገሞ ፡ ወቅድስትሰ ፡ ማሪና ፣ ኢያእ መረት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ ዘረገማ ። ወሶበ ፣ ጠየቀት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፣ በከየት ፡ ወሰገደ ት ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለአበ ፡ምኔት ፡ ወሰአለቶ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ አነ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወአበ ስኩ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ። ወተቁቄጥዓ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ እም ደብር ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ኀበ ፣ አፍአ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ ደብር ፡ እስከ ፡ ወለደት ፡ ወለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ቤ ተ ፡ነግድ ፡ ሕፃነ ። ወጾሩ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ወአብጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እንባ ፡ መሪና ፡ ወወገሩ ፡ ላቲ፣ ወነሥአቶ ። ወኮነት ፣ ተዐውድ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ኖሎት ፡ ዓቃቢያነ ፡ አልህምት ፡ ወአባግዕ ፡ ወ ትስእል ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወይሁብዋ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ ወታሰትዮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ወሰከት ፡ ጾማ ፣ ወጸሎታ ፡ ወተጋድሎታ = "ወነበረት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ማሪና ፡ አፍአ ፡ ፕጥኅ ተ ፡ደብር ፡ የዓመተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ተጋብኡ ፡፣ መነኮሳት ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሐ
(The innkeeper) told him what had happened to his daughter, and said to him: "It is Abba Marina, the monk, who has defiled the virginity of my daughter." When the superior of the monastery had heard this, he was extremely sad. It seemed to him that it was true. The superior of the monastery begged the innkeeper, and said to him: "Hide this matter, and do not dishonor the monks before the laity." Then the superior of the monastery called Abba Marina, reprimanded him, and cursed him. But Saint Marina did not know for what reason he had cursed her. When she learned what had happened, she wept, prostrated herself at the feet of the superior of the monastery, and begged him, saying: "I am a young man; I have sinned; forgive me my sin." The superior of the monastery grew angry with her, and drove her out of the convent. She remained outside the gate of the convent, until the innkeeper's daughter had brought forth (her) child. The parents (of this girl) brought (the child), led him to Abba Marina, and threw him to her. (Saint Marina) took him. She would go to the shepherds who guarded the cows and the sheep, and ask them (for milk). They would give her milk, and she would make the child drink (it). Then, she increased her fasts, her prayers, and her asceticism. Saint Marina * remained outside the gate of the convent for three years. Then, the monks gathered, and asked the superior of the monastery


[p. 331] {#po.9.p331}
ሮ ፡ ለእንባ ፡ መሪና ፡ ወያብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ ስእለቶሙ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ፡ደብር ፡ ወደመሮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወኮነት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትገብር ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ዕፁባተ ፡ ወታበስል ፡ጸብሐ ፡ ወትኩሰትር ፡ ወትጸውር ፡መሬተ ፡ ወትወግሮ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ደ ብር ፡ ወትቀድኅ ፡ ማየ ። ወልህቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ለቅ ድስት ፡ ማሪና ፡ በዓመት ፣ በውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ደወየት ፡ ቦመዓልተ ፡ ወአዕረ ፈት ። ወሶበ ፡ አአመረ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ከመ ፡ እንባ ፡ መሪና ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ፣ ያጥምቅዎ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይቅብርዎ ። ወሶበ ፣ አአተቱ ፡ አልባሲሃ ፡ ረከብዋ ፣ ብእሲ ተ ፡ ወጸርጉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ መሐረነ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈ ድፋደ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወአይድፅዎ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ወ በጽሐ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ። ወእም ዝ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ነግድ ፣ ወአይድዖ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንባ ፡ መሪና ፡ ብ አሷት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ወርእያ ፡ ወአንከረ ።
to have pity on Abba Marina, and to let him return to the convent. He agreed to their request, let him return to the convent, and associated him with the monks. The saint did arduous work: she did the cooking; she swept; she removed the dust and threw it outside the convent; she drew water. The child grew up, and became a monk. When thirty years (of monastic life) had passed for Saint Marina in this convent, she was ill for three days, and died. * When the superior of the monastery knew that Abba Marina was dead, he ordered that he be washed, before burying him. When his clothes had been removed, it was noticed that it was a woman. All the monks cried out, saying: "O Lord, have pity on us." They were extremely astonished, and they glorified the Lord. They told the superior of the monastery what had happened. The superior of the monastery came; seeing (the saint), he was astonished, and wept because of what he had done to her. Then he sent (for), and had the innkeeper come. He told him that Abba Marina was a woman; he introduced him (to the saint; the innkeeper) saw her, and was astonished.


[p. 332] {#po.9.p332}
ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወገነዝዋ ፣ በብካይ ፡ ብዙኅ ፣ በዝማሬ ፡ ወማኅሌት ፡ ወ ሶበ ፡ ተባረኩ ፣ እምኔሃ ፡ ቀ” በርዋ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ለ8ሰይጣን ፡ ወነሥ አ ፡ ለወለተ ፡ ባዕለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ነግድ ፡ ወለውእቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዘአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናሃ ፡ ወኮነ፣ ይስሕቦሙ ፤ ወይኬንኖሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ አብጽሖሙ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መቃብሪሃ ፣ ወተአመኑ ፡ አበ ሳሆሙ ፡፣ በቅድመ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ እመቃብሪሃ ፡ ተአምራት ፣ ወመ ንክራት ፣ ብዙኃት ፣ ዘኢይትኃለቀ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፣ በጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከ ታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፣ ለመሪና ፡ እንዘ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ በባሕርያ ፡= ዘተመሰለት ፡ መነኮሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አበ ፡ ወለት ፡ ሐመያ ፡፡ ወለክርስጢና ፡ ዕግሥት ፣ በብዝኃ ፣ ሥቃያ ፡=፡ ለጉባዔ ፣ አርድእት ፡፣ እሴብሕ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እብል ፡ ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ። ወለወረንስዮን ፡ ዘመሰዖ ፡ ለእሳት ፡ ዋዕያ ፡
Then, (the monks) prayed over her, and buried her with many tears, amidst psalmodies and chants. When they had asked (the saint) for her blessing, * they buried her. But behold, the Lord ordered a demon to seize the innkeeper's daughter, and the soldier who had defiled her virginity, to drag them, and to torture them, until he had brought them to the tomb (of the saint), and they had confessed their sin before all the people. Frequent (and) innumerable miracles and wonders appeared at her tomb. May the Lord have pity on us through her prayer, and may her blessing be with us! Amen [Hail to Marina, while she was a woman by her nature, She who made herself like a monk, while the father of the girl (of the innkeeper) insulted her! (Hail) to Christina, patient in her many tortures! I glorify the society of the disciples, saying: "Alleluia", As well as Lawrence, whom the heat of the fire dissolved.]


[p. 333] {#po.9.p333}
ሰላም ፡ ለመሪና ፡ ለወሬዛ ፡ በአምሳሉ ።፡ እንተ ፡ መከረት ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አኃው ፡ ተሀሉ ፤ እንዘ ፡ ትትናገር ፡ ይእቲ ፡ዕበ ፡ ይሰምፅዋ ፡ ኩሉ ፡=፡ ይትዋሥኡ ፡ ወይትባሀሉ ፡ ትሑት ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ፪
[Hail to Marina, resembling a young man, Who took the wise resolution to dwell at a convent of brothers! When (the monks) heard her speak, all They discussed, and said among themselves: "Her voice is low Due to her many austerities and her intense asceticism."]
እምተፀምዶቱ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወፍድሩድ ፡ ገድሉ ፡- ሰላም ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አርዳኡ ፡ በልበ ፡ ምእመናን ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘዘርኡ ፡፡ ለግንዘተ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ዮም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡=፡ እምነ ፡ ዐዊድ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ መጽኡ ፡=፡ ወእመቃብር ፡ ሙታን ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡
[Hail, hail to the disciples of the Lord, Who have sown in the heart of all the faithful words of life! While (the disciples) have gathered today for the burial of the Virgin, The living (among) them have come from the surroundings, And the dead have risen from the tomb.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕላት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ስ"ምዓ ፡ቅዳዱስ ፣ ለወርንስዮስ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ዳክዮስ ፡ ቄሳር ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዓቢይ ፣፡ ወተሰይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ዲያቆና ት ፡ ወየዓቅብ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወሶበ ፣ ቀተሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ በእ
[On this day also is the * martyrdom of Saint Lawrence, in the days of the Emperor Decius. This saint possessed a lively faith. He was instituted archdeacon. He guarded the goods of the patriarch. When the king had killed the patriarch because of


[p. 334] {#po.9.p334}

the religion of Christ, he ordered (them) to bring Saint Lawrence to him. When they had brought him (to him), they led him to prison. (Saint Lawrence) found a blind man there. He said to him: "(Do you wish) to believe in the Son of the living Lord, and be baptized in his name, so that your eyes may be opened?" (The latter) said to him: "Yes, my lord." Then, (Saint Lawrence) prayed over the water, and baptized him in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit. The eyes (of this man) were opened. He cried out with a loud voice, and said: "Blessed be Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - who has illuminated my eyes through the prayer of his servant Lawrence!" Having learned (this), the king ordered that they bring Saint Lawrence (to him). He forced him to worship the idols. When (Saint Lawrence) had refused him (to do so, the king) broke his teeth with a stone. They tore his clothes; they laid him on an iron bed; and they lit a fire under him, so that his body was dissolved. As for him, he prayed * to his God, until he gave up his soul into the hand of the Lord. The angels went to meet him, saying: "Alleluia", and introduced him into the heavenly Jerusalem. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.]
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ አባላቲከ ፣ ፍቱነ ፡=፡ በመንኅበ ፡ አሳት ፡ ርሱን ፡ እንተ ፡ ይትፈተን ፡ ሐዊነ ፡=፡
[I say hail to your members tested (Like) iron, which is tested by the furnace of the burning fire!


[p. 335] {#po.9.p335}
ለወረንስዮስ ፡ እርአይ ፡ ዘተውሀበከ ፡ መካነ ፡=፡ ሀበኒ ፡በጸሎትከ ፣፡ ለውሣጤ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ዓይነ ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ጸጎከ ፡ ለዕውር ፡ ብርሃነ ፡= በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
So that I may see the place that has been given to you, Lawrence, Give me through your prayer an eye in the interior of my heart, As you granted light to the blind man.] In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ፲ፄለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 16TH OF NAHASÉ (August 22).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕርገተ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፤ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሰማይ ። እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕረፍታ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐዝኑ ፡ ወይቴክዙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍልጠታ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡፤ አሙንቱ ፡ ኮ ኑ ፡ እቤራተ ፡ እምኔሃ ። እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ አሰፈዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀለዎሙ ፡ ይኔጽ ሩ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ በሥጋ ። ወእንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስያ ፡ አመ ፡፲ወ5ለነሐሴ ፣ ተመስጠ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገነተ ፣፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ወ ርእዮ ፡ ለእግዚአነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ኅበ ፡
On this day is the Assumption into heaven of the body of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, Mother of God. After her death, the apostles remained sad and afflicted because of her separation from them. Indeed, they had become orphans of (Mary). Our Lord (however) had promised them that they were to behold her in (her) body. While John the Evangelist was preaching in a city of Asia, on the 16th of Nahasé, he was caught up by the Holy Spirit to the paradise of happiness, and saw Our Lord Jesus Christ sitting near the tree of life, where was the


[p. 336] {#po.9.p336}
ሀለወ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ። ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለ፲መላእክት ፡ ወጸውዕዋ ፡ ለም ድር ፡ ወይቤልዋ ፡ ይኤዝዘኪ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታውፅኢ ፡ ሥ ጋሃ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ንጽሕት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እምውስ ተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ እምታሕተ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወናዘዛ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ንዒ ፡ ኀቤየ ፣ ኦወላዲትየ ፡ ፍቅርት ፡ ከመ ፡ " አዕርጊ ፡ ኀበ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰ ማያት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አድነኑ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ ዕፀወ ፡ ገነት ወአፅረግዋ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፣ መላእክት ፣ ወጻድቃን ፡ ወዳዊት ፡ነቢይ ፡ ሰብሐ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ። ወትቀውም ፡ ንግሥት ፡ በየማንከ ። በአልባሰ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ዑጽፍት ፡ ወ ኀብርት ። ወነበረት ፣ በየማኑ ፡ ለወልዳ ፣ ወአምላካ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወአርገት፣፣
body of Our Lady Mary. (Our Lord) gave orders to seven angels. (These) called the earth, and said to it: "Our Lord Jesus Christ orders you to bring out the body of his pure Mother." Immediately, the body of Our Lady Mary came out of the tomb, below the tree of life. Our Lord Jesus Christ consoled her, and said to her: "Come to me, O my beloved Mother, so that * I may make you ascend to the kingdom of heaven, into eternal happiness." Immediately, all the trees of paradise bowed down. The angels, the archangels, and the just made (Mary) ascend. The prophet David glorified, saying: The queen stands at your right hand, clothed in variegated golden garments. (Mary) * was sitting at the right hand of her Son and of her God, in great glory. She ascended to the heavens, being seated on the chariot of the Cherubim.
ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ እንዘ ፡ትነብር ፡ ዲበ ፣ ሰረገላ ፡ ኪሩባዊት ። ወተባረከ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ አምሰማይ ፡ ወረከቦሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ጉቡአን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቴክዙ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ወዘ ከመ ፣ አፅረጉ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለአግዝእትነ ፡ማርያም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወበሐሜት ዞ። ወዕበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተከዙ ፣ ወሐዘኑ ፡ ፈድፋ
John the Evangelist received her blessing. He prostrated himself before her. (Then) he returned, and came down from heaven. He found the apostles gathered (and) sad concerning the body of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary. John told them how he had seen (Mary), and how (the angels) had made the body of Our Lady Mary ascend to the heavens in great glory, in joy and in gladness. When the apostles had learned this, they became extremely sad and


[p. 337] {#po.9.p337}
ደ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢርእዩ ፡ ወዘኢሰምዑ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይቴክዙ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ወናሁ ፣ አስተርአዮሙ ፡፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ሎቱ ፣ ስብሐት ፣ ወይቤሎ ሙ ፡ በላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ኦውሎደ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትቴክዙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለእ ምየ ፡ ፍቅርት ፡ ማርያም ። ናሁ ፡ አነ ፣ ሀለውኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ አርኢክሙ ፡ ኪያ፣ሃ ፡ ወይት ፌሣሕ ፡ አልበቢክሙ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብ
afflicted that they had not seen nor heard what John had seen (and heard). While the apostles were sad, behold, Our Lord Christ - glory to Him! - appeared to them, and said to them: "Peace be with you, O sons of peace! Why are you sad concerning the body of my beloved Mother Mary? Behold, I myself am going to make you see her, and your hearts * will rejoice." Having said this, he ascended back to the heavens, in great glory.

The apostles remained a whole year (waiting for this vision). When it was the first (day) of the month of Nahasé, John said to the apostles: "Come, so that we may fast for two weeks, pray to Our Lord, and beseech him, so that he may make us worthy, and make us see the body of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, and so that we may rejoice." They fasted, as John had told them. As soon as the 16th of Nahasé had arrived, Our Lord Christ caught up all the apostles to the heavens. They saw the body of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, while she was sitting at the right hand of her beloved Son and of her God, in great glory. She stretched out her hands, and blessed the disciples, one by one. The soul of the disciples rejoiced enormously. Our Lord

Christ celebrated the Sacrifice of his holy body; Stephen, the chief of the deacons, served him: John said (to the apostles): "Stand in fear." All the apostles stood around the altar. There was at that moment a great joy, such as there had never been. When Our Lord had finished the Sacrifice, he gave them the holy Mysteries.

Then, (Mary) ascended before them, being seated on the chariot of the Cherubim. Our Lord Christ said to Our Lady Mary: "Command your sons, the apostles, to preach in the whole world the memory of your Assumption, on this day. Indeed, every being who will make * your memory, (will be saved); I myself will erase all his sins for him; he will never see the (eternal) fire, for (my) mercy will reach every soul that will keep itself (from sin) and will receive the holy Mysteries on this day, that is to say (on the day) of the Assumption of your body into heaven." Our Lady Mary said to him: "Behold, O my Son, that they have seen with their eyes, have heard with their ears, and have taken with their hands. They will see other wonders greater than this one." While the Virgin was expressing herself thus, (Our Lord) gave peace to the apostles, (who) went away to the Mount of Olives. As for us, O our dear brothers, we must make
ነሰ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ኦአኃዊነ ፡ ፍቁራን ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ዕርገታ ፡ ለእግዝእ ትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወበሐሜት ። አስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትተነብል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ፍቁር ፡ ወልዳ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘሎ ቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለም ። ትንብልናሃ ፡ ወጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፣ ወአሜን ።
the memory of the Assumption into heaven of Our Lady Mary in joy and in gladness. Indeed, she intercedes for us, at every moment, with her beloved Son Our Lord Jesus Christ - to whom be glory forever! - May the intercession, the prayer, and the blessing of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary be with us! Amen. Amen

[On this day also is the translation with glory of the body of Saint George, chief of the martyrs, from the land of Persia into the church of Lydda. Indeed, the translation of his body is associated with the Assumption of the body of Our Lady Mary. That is why those who have known his love for (Mary) have painted his image with the image (of Mary), so that he might be the port of salvation for those who intercede in his name. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.]

"Associate me, Mother, with your Assumption."] [On this day also is the martyrdom of Gigar, prefect of Syria. The cause of his martyrdom, (here it is). While Our Lord was with his Mother Mary in the mountains of Lebanon, when (Gigar) had learned that Herod wanted to surround and kill them, he sent horsemen to them, (to warn them) to move away from there. That is why Herod arrested him, inflicted various tortures upon him, and cut off all his limbs. Then, Our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him! - appeared to (Gigar), and gave him * the promise [kidân] that (he himself) would have mercy on the one who would invoke his name, would make his memory, (or) would write his combat. Then they cut off his head with the sword. He was crowned with three crowns. May his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.
ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ሰላም ፣ ለከ ፡ መስፍነ ፡ ሶርያ ፣ ጊጋር ፡=፡ እንተ ፡ አምተረከ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ለኀበ ፣ ብዙኅ ፡ ምታር ፡፡ እስከ ፡ ተካየደከ ፡ ለከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ከመ ፡ ይድኅን ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወፃዕር ፡፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በምድር ፡፡
[Hail to you, prefect of Syria, Gigar, Whom Herod had cut into many pieces, So that the Lord promised you That (every) man who would make your memory on earth, Would be saved, on the day of affliction and torment!]


[p. 342] {#po.9.p342}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፲ወኒለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 17TH OF NAHASÉ (August 23).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እንጣዎስ ፣ አሞራዊ ፡፣ ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅድ መ ፡ ዓላዌ ፡ ወኢያአምሮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ያስተሐምም ፡ ለአስተጋብኦ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ደማስቆ ፡ ቦአ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ምሥራቃ ዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ጉቡአን ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ዕለተ ፡ በዐሎሙ ፡ ወበዊኦ ፡ አውዓ የ ፣ ንዋየ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሰበረ ፣ መስቀለ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዓርገ ፣ ተስላሰ ፡ ርእየ ፡ በ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ አሕፃ ፡፤ እሳት ፡ እምሰማይ ፤ ወይነድፎሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሐራሁ ፤ ወሎቱቪኒ ፡ ነደፈቶ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሐፅ ፡ የማነ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፅዕረ ፡ ወአንጸፍጸ ፈ ፡ ሐፉ ፡ ወጸርሐ ፡ እምዕመቀ ፡ ልቡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ወይብል ፡ አመንኩ ፡ ብከ ፡ ኢ የሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፣ ወበሥላሴክከ ፡ ቅድስት ። ወተካየዶ ፡ በነፍሱ ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢያምልክ ፡ ባዕደ ፡ አምላከ ፡ እምዝ ፣ ዳግመ ፡ ወክርስቲያንሰ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፣፤ ኢያእመሩ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወተወሰከ ፣ አሚኖቶሙ ፡ ወሐዊሮ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እንጣዎስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጳጳስ ፡ ዘስ
[On this day is the martyrdom of 'Entawos the Amorite. This saint was at first an impious man. He did not know Christ. He occupied himself on the contrary with amassing riches. Having arrived at the city of Damascus, he entered the church of Theodore the Oriental, while the people were gathered, and it was a feast day. When he had entered, he set fire to the furniture of the church, and broke the cross. When he had gone up to a floor, he saw with his eyes arrows of fire descending from heaven and piercing all his soldiers. An arrow also pierced his right side, so that he was tortured (by the pain) and the sweat dripped from him. He cried out from the bottom of his heart, weeping, and saying: "I believe in you, Jesus Christ, Son of the Lord, God of Saint Theodore, and in your Holy Trinity." He promised (Jesus Christ) in his soul that he would no longer worship any other God from now on. The Christians, for their part, were at the Sacrifice; they did not know what had happened. Having learned it, they were extremely astonished, and their faith increased. Having gone toward the patriarch, called Abba Elijah, Saint 'Entawos


[p. 343] {#po.9.p343}
ሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጥምቆ ፣ ጥምቀተ ፡ክ ርስትና ።
told him everything that had happened, and asked him to baptize him with the Christian baptism.

When the patriarch had prayed over the water of the baptistery, a column of light, like a rainbow, descended, while all the people saw it. Saint 'Entäwos was baptized, as were those who were with him: Jews and pagans, to the number of 10,798. They received the body and blood of Christ - glory to Him! Saint 'Entäwos said the (following words): "While I was astonished, I saw in (my) sleep a luminous woman. She took me (by) the hand, led me to the altar, and made me approach her hand. Then, I went to Jerusalem, and I received the flesh and blood of Christ. Furthermore, I saw on the altar a white ewe. When the priest had immolated it with the communion spoon, its blood spilled into the chalice. When I received the flesh of Christ, it was a pure bread; its blood also was a choice wine." Then, while he was going to Damascus, the Amorites took him and led him to the king. When the king knew that he was firm in the faith of Christ, he ordered that he be struck (on) the mouth with the heel (of a shoe), so that his teeth were broken, and his mouth filled with blood.
ሁ፡ደመ። ወእምዝ ፡ አሰርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኅርወተ ፡ ጉን”ድ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሂመዋዕል ፡ እንበለ ፡ማይ ፡

days, without (giving him) water, (nor) food. He was pulled from there, and he was thrown into a (furnace) of fire, (filled) with boiling pitch, sulfur, resin, and fat. || there came out of it a sweet odor (and) perfume. When the soldiers saw him, they found him praying, standing in the middle of the fire. They led him to the king. The king said to him: "When did you learn the art of magic?" The saint said to him: "I do not know magic, * but I confound you by the power of the Lord Jesus Christ - glory to Him!" Filled with anger, the king ordered that his head be cut off with the sword. Saint 'Entäwos, standing on the side of the Orient, stretched out his hands, and committed his soul to the Lord. While he was praying, a voice came from heaven, which said: "Come, my servant, in peace, in order to rest with George and Stephen." When (the saint) had finished his prayer, the soldier cut off his head with the sword. When he had completed his martyrdom, innumerable miracles and wonders appeared through his body. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for centuries of centuries! Amen.
ሰላም ፣ ለእንጣዎስ ፡ ለሃይማኖቱ ፡ ዘአጽደላ ፡፡ እምነ ፡ ኦርያሬስ ፡ ወኤብላ ።፡

READING OF THE 18 NAHASÉ (August 24). On this day is Saint James, martyr, from a small town called Manoug, in the district of 'Absou, in the north of Egypt. The parents of this saint were Christians (and) feared the Lord. They begot three daughters before him. When they had grown a little, they gave them to a convent of virgins, so that they might be instructed, and raised in the fear of the Lord. They studied most of the books of the Church, and (the Scriptures) of the Old and New (Testament). When their parents wanted to bring them back from * the virgins, they did not want to return, but they gave themselves as spouses to Christ. (The parents) were saddened by their separation from them. The Lord consoled them, (by giving them) Saint James, (in whom) they rejoiced. When he was * six years old, they sent him to the city of 'Absou, so that he might pursue his studies. He studied all doctrine, and became perfect in all science. (Then), his father sent him to see his goods, his riches, his animals, and his sheep.
ዮ ፡ ወጥሪቶ ፡ ወእንስሳሁ ፡ ወአባግዒሁ ። ወሀሎ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ 5አረጋዊ ፡ ኖላዌ ፡፣ አባግዕ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ትሩፋተ ፤

virtues. * He would descend into a cistern, (filled) with water, in the wintertime, and he would stand there, praying all night. Saint James did as this old shepherd did. He continued (to do) so for many days. When Satan brought persecution against the people of the Christians, and many Christians became martyrs, the old man handed the sheep over to the father of Saint James, and went away to become a martyr. James asked his father to allow him to go with the old man; (he assured him) that he would return. When he had left with him, they both found a judge in Upper Egypt, in the act of torturing Justus, the son of a king, who had abandoned his royalty and was becoming a martyr. The old man said to James: "See, O my son, because this king and (this) son of a king has abandoned (far) from him the royalty of this world, and has followed Christ, he has been separated from his wife and his son. All the more reason (will it be) for us (who are) poor. Console yourself, O my son, and do not be saddened regarding your separation from your parents." Then they approached the judge, confessed before him Our Lord
ዊከ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቀርቡ ፣ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ቅድሜ ሁ ፡ ወረገሙ ፡ አማልክቲሁ ። ወኩጎነኖ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ፍጡ ነ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ።'
Christ, and cursed his (false) gods. (The judge) tortured the old man, and cut off his head quickly. (The old man) became a martyr.

ወለቅዱስሰ ፡ ያፅቆብ ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወቀሠፎ ፣ በጥብጣቤ ፣ ዘአልሀም ት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አርሰነ ፡ አብነ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወአንበሮ ፣ ዲ"በ ፣ ከርሠ ፡ ወአንሥአ ፡ አዕ ይንቲሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወጸለየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአግዚአእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ኢየ ሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ርድአኒ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወአድኃኖ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ አምውእ ቱ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወእምዝ ፡ ረሰዮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሠቀ ፡ ጸኾር ፡ ወወገሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወአዕረጎ ፣ መልአከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምባሕር ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወቆመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወረገሞ ፡ ወረገመ ፡ ጣዖታቲሁ ፡ ርኩሳተ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላፅሌሁ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወፈነዎ ፣ ኀበ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ፈረ ማ ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ በሀየ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወመተሩ ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ ወመልሐ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወ ኩነንዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንኩራኩራት ፡ ወወሰሩ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ በመዋስርት ፡ እስከ ፡ ተ ሰጥቀ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አባሉ ፡ ወኮነ ደሙ ፡ ይውሕዝ ፡ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ከመ ፡ ማይ ። ወወረደ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሱርያል ፡ ወአድኅኖ ፡ ወፈወሰ ፡ ቀነሰላቲሁ ። ወሶ"በ ፣ ደክ መ።፣መኩንን ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስ
As for Saint James, (the judge) inflicted a great torture upon him: he flogged him with ox-hide thongs, then he made a stone red-hot in the fire, and placed it on his stomach. The saint raised his eyes toward Christ, prayed, and said: "O my Lord and my God Jesus Christ, help me in this torture." Our Lord delivered him from this torture, and made him stand up safe and sound, without injury. Then, (the judge) put him in a hair sack, and threw him into the sea. The angel of the Lord brought him up from the sea safe and sound, without injury. The saint stood before the judge, cursed him, and cursed his impure idols. The judge became angry with him, and sent him to the judge of Faramâ. There he was inflicted with a great torture: his tongue was cut out; his eyes were gouged out; he was tortured on the wheels; his whole body was sawn with saws, to the point that his whole body was in pieces, and his blood flowed on the earth like water. The angel of the Lord Souryâl descended, delivered him, and healed his wounds. * When the judge was weary of torturing him, he ordered that his head be cut off with the sword. (The saint) received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom


[p. 349] {#po.9.p349}
ምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወኮኑ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ፪ሰማዕታት ፡ ካልአን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ፈረማ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ዘእምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ገም ኑዲ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ወበጸሎቶ ሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፣ አብርሃም ፣ ወዮሐንስ ፤ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ም ስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
of heaven. There were with him two other martyrs, while he was in the city of Faram: these were Abraham and John, who (were part) of the people of the city of Gamnoudi. May the Lord have mercy on us through the prayer of Saint James and through the prayer of the holy martyrs Abraham and John, and may their blessing be with us! Amen.
ሰላም ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ጸዋሬ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡=፡ ከመ ፡ ተምሀረ ፡ በምድረ ፡ አብሱ ።፡ እብነ ፡ ርሱ”ነ ፡ ዘአንበረ ፣ በዲበ ፡ ከር ፡=፡ ሰላም ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፣ ወለአብርሃም ፡ መዋርሱ ።፡ እለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ እምዓለም ፡ ፈለሱ ፡፡
[Salutation to James, possessing wisdom from his youth, According to how he was instructed in the land of 'Absou! (The judge) put a * burning stone on his stomach. Salutation to John and Abraham, his co-heirs, Who jointly with him have emigrated from (this) world!]
ሰላም ፡ ለአረጋዊ ፣ ለሕሊናሁ ፡ ዘአዕረጎ ፡፡ ሕሊና ፣ መላእክት ፡ ለተዛውጎ ፡፡ በብሂለ ፡ ነገር ፡ ም"ስሌሁ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፡ እስከ ፡ መዘጎ።= ወልድየ ፡ ርኢ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጾረ ፡ ለስቃየ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ጹጎ ፡=፡ ዘመጠነዝ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ኃዲጎ ፡
[Salutation to the old man who raised his thoughts To associate with the thoughts of the angels, So that he snatched James * with him, saying (this) word: "(O) my son, see how Justus has endured the cruelty of the tortures of martyrdom, "After having abandoned such a great royalty!"]


[p. 350] {#po.9.p350}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልደ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፲፪8ለነሐሴሾ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING OF THE 19 NAHASÉ (August 25).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ አልስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘ ሀገረ ፡ ፉስጥንጥንያ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወክቡረ ፡ ወበጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ብ ዙኅ ፡ እምሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ሐደሰ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ግዘቶ ፡ ለአር ዮስ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ወሰደዶሙ ፡ ለሕዝቡ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሖረ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ቀሩኑስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወቦአ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወልደ ፤ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወአስተዋደዮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ አትናስዮ ስ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢረከበ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ዕረፍተ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፣ ከመ ፡ የአዝዝ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አልስክንድሮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወከፎ ፡ምስለ ፡ ክህናት ። ወ ፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አትናስዮስ ፡ ተ ዐደወ ፡ ትእዛዘነ ፣ ወኢተወክፎ ፡ ለአርዮስ ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ዘ
On this day died the holy Father Abba Alexander, patriarch of the city of Constantinople. This Father was holy and illustrious. A great persecution came upon him from the partisans of Arius. In the days (of Abba Alexander), Abba Athanasius renewed the excommunication of Arius. He expelled him, and he expelled his partisans from the churches of the city of Alexandria. Arius went away to the city of Constantinople. He entered before King Constantine, son of Constantine the Just, and he accused Saint Athanasius before the king. As he found no truce with (Saint Athanasius), Arius asked the king to order Father Alexander to admit him with the priests. The king sent (for) this Father, and had him come. He said to him: "Behold, Athanasius has transgressed our order, and has not received Arius. As for you, you will know that it is we ourselves who instituted you (patriarch); do not transgress (therefore) our order, but please my
ዔምናከ ፡ ኢትትዐደው ፡ ትእዛዘነ ፡ አላ ፡ አሠኒ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ወፍትፕ ፡ ለአርዮስ ። ወአው ሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ኢትትወከፎ፣፡"ቤ ተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢያመልክ ፡ ሥሉሰ ፡ ቅዱሰ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ አላ ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ ተአምነ ፡ በሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በቅድሜየ ፣፡ ወአውሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ 0፣ አልስክንድሮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተአምነ ፡ በሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወየአምን ፡ከ መ፡ ወልድ ፡ ፅሩይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ይጽሐፍ ፡ በእዴሁ ‹፡ ዘንተ ።
heart, and absolve Arius." Saint Abba Alexander answered, and said to him: "* The Church does not receive Arius, because he does not worship the holy Trinity." The king said to him: "But he himself has confessed the holy Trinity before me." Saint Abba Alexander answered, and said to him: "If he has confessed the holy Trinity, and (if he) believes that the Son is equal to the Father in His divinity, let him write that with his hand!"


[p. 351] {#po.9.p351}
ወአብጽሖ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ጸሐፍ ፡ በእዴከ ፡ ሃይማኖተከ ፡ ወጸሐ ፈ፣፤ በእዴሁ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ርትዕተ ፣ ወኢአምነ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ በልቡ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ መሐለ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወመ ሐለ ፡ በሐሰት ። ወይቤሎ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ አስክንድሮስ ፡ ኢተረፈ ፤ ምክንያት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ እምድኅረ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፤ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ርትዕተ ፡ በእዴሁ ፣ ወመሐለ ፡ በወንጌል ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፣ እስክንድሮስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አባ ፣ አትናስዮስ ፡ ሐደሰ ፡ አን ብቦ ፡ ግዘቱ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፣ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለአቡከ ፣ ቂቄስጠንጢኖስ ፣ ወጽሑፍ ፡ በ አደዊሆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ የ፻፲ወቿወሰደዶ ፣ ወኩሎ ፡ ሕዝቦ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ አ ላ ፡ ተዓገሥ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ፳ሱባኤ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኢበጽሐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ሱባኤ ፣ ወእመአኮ ፡ አሙን ፡ ውእቱ ፣ "ሃይማኖቱ ፣ ወመሐላሁ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣
The king had Arius come, and said to him: "Write your faith with your hand." (Arius) wrote the orthodox faith with his hand, but he did not believe in the orthodox faith in his heart. Then he swore by the holy Gospel that this (profession) was the orthodox faith, but he perjured himself. The king said to Father Abba Alexander: "There remains no more grievance against him, after he has written the orthodox faith with his hand, and has sworn by the Gospel." Abba Alexander said to him: "Abba Athanasius has renewed the proclamation of the excommunication of Arius, which was written by the hand of your father Constantine, and was written by the hand of the 318 Fathers. (Athanasius) has expelled him, (him) as well as all his partisans, from the city of Alexandria. But, be patient with me for a week. If nothing happens to Arius this week, and if * his faith is sincere


[p. 352] {#po.9.p352}
"ውእቱ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እትዌከፎ ፡ ወእዴምሮ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወይሳተፍ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በመልእክተ ፣ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አእምር ፡ እስመ ፡ አ ነ ፡ እትዔገሥ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ሯመዓልተ ፡ ወለእመ ፣ ኢተወከፍኮ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ወእመአኮ ፡
and his oath * loyal, I myself will receive him, and I will associate him with the priests. He will take part with them in the ministry of the temple." The king answered, and said to him: "Know that I myself will be patient with you for eight days. But, if you do not receive Arius, certainly I will levy a considerable tax on the church."
አነ ፡ እነሥእ ፡ ጸበሕተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ፪ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ አባ ፣ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ፣ ቤቱ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ ውእተ ፡ ሱባኤ ፡ ዙልንታሁ ፡ ወይስእሎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኖ ፡ እምኃጢአቱ ፡ ለአርዮስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሱባኤ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ካህናት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክን ድሮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐዝን ፣፡ ወይቴክዝ ፡ ወኢያአምር ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘይገብር ። ወቆመ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ይውጥን ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ወተሀውከ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካነ ፡ ሙፃአ ፡ ዙስሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩሳሕ ። ወወፅአ ፡ አማዕዋቲሁ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ እምታ ሕቴሁ ፡ ወተረፈ ፡ ፅራቁ ። ወሶበ ፣ ጐንደየ ፣ ኅሠሥዎ ፡ ወሐተቱ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ወረከብ ዎ፣፤ምውተ ፡ውስተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዙስሕ ። ወአይድፅዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወ ሰብሖ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘኢገደፈ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኖ ፡ ቅድስት ።
Then Abba Alexander left the king and went to his house. He remained fasting that entire week, and asking the Lord to deliver him from the sin of Arius. When the week had passed, Arius dressed himself in fine clothes, entered the church, and sat before the altar with the priests. Then, Father Abba Alexander entered, being sad and afflicted. He did not know what he (was going to) do. He stood, before beginning the Sacrifice. The intestines of Arius were agitated. He went to the latrines, in order to relieve himself. His bowels and everything that was in his intestines came out under him; he remained empty (of bowels). As he was delayed, they looked for him, and they went in search of him; they found him dead in the latrines. They told the Father (Alexander) what had happened. He glorified Christ, who had not rejected His holy Church.


[p. 353] {#po.9.p353}
ወአንከረ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ በእንተዝ ፣ ወአእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መሐለ ፡ በሐሰት ፡ወ ጸሐፈ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ በሐሰት ። ወጠየቀ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወጽድቆ ፡ ወርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሰብሖ ፡ ለአብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ ከመ ፡ መለኮቶሙ ፡ ፳ውእቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖ ረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ሑረተ ፡ ክብርተ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርሥእና ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ወፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊለ ፣ ሰማያዌ ፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያ ት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ቋሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለ
The king was astonished at this. He understood (that Arius) had perjured himself, and had written a lie with his hand. The king recognized the holiness of the Father (Alexander), his justice, and his orthodoxy. He understood that Arius was a heretic. He glorified the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, and confessed that their divinity was one. When the Father (Alexander) had followed a glorious path and had reached a beautiful old age, he emigrated toward the Lord, whom he had loved, and he received the heavenly crown in the kingdom of heaven. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us for centuries of centuries! Amen.
ም፣አሜን። ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፣ አለ ፡ እስክንድሮስ ፡ ስዩም ፡፡ በዕብሬትከ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ አርያም ።፡ ዘአርዮስ ፣ ውቁይ ፡ በሰይፈ ፡ መርገም ።፡ እስከነ ፡ ኢተረፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እንበለ ፡ አነዳ ፡ ወዓፅም ፡፡
[Salutation to you, Alexander, instituted (patriarch)! Under your charge descended the strength from above, So that Arius might be cut off by the sword of the anathema. His belly rejected his bowels, like ox excrement, So that there remained to him nothing but skin and bones.]
አማዑተ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ከዐወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽፍዕ ፡ ዘላሀም ።፡ ሰላም ፤ ለዮስጢኖስ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘነበረ ፡፡ ሕፃናተ ፡ ያጥብብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜህር ፡ መዝሙረ ፡፡
[Salutation to Justin, who sat on a mountain, In order to instill instruction to the children, by teaching (them) the psalms!


[p. 354] {#po.9.p354}
አምሕሊናሁ ፡ ወልቡ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አስተናጽሐ ፡ ነውረ ፡፡ ምስለ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ሐዋርያተ ፡ ነጸረ ፡፡ እምኀቤሆሙ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡ ተምህረ ፡፡
When he had purged the stains from his intelligence and his heart, He saw the apostles as well as the prophets, And learned from them the (Holy) Books.]
ሰላም ፡ ለፊንሐስ ፡ ገሳሴ ፡፤ ሥጋ ፡ ነድ ፡ ርሱን ።= ለተዝካረ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ገሃደ ፣ እንተ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ብርሃን ፡=፡ ቀዳማይ ፡ ፊንሐስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አልዓዛር ፡ ክህን ፡፡ በከመ ፡ረገዛ ፡ ለብእሷት ፣፡ ዘሜይን ፡፡ በረጊዘ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ጸንዓ ፣ አዴሁ ፡፣ዘ"ይምን ፡
[Salutation to Phinehas, (whose) body a burning flame has touched! A light descended manifestly for the memory of his name. (Salutation to) the elder Phinehas, son of the priest Eleazar! Just as he pierced the woman of Midian, (Just so) his * right hand was strong, to pierce the (infidel) Jews.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ኮነ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአንድራኒቆስ ፣ ዲያቆን ። አግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፡ገ
[On this day also is the memory of 'Andrâniqos, deacon. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer for centuries of centuries! Amen.]


[p. 355] {#po.9.p355}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፳ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING OF THE 20 NAHASÉ (August 26).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ፍልሰተ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ”አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳ መ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። እስመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምሀገሩ ፡ ሰስዊር ፡ ወሰረቁ ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ። ወነበረ ፡ እስከ ፣ አመ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ተንበላታት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰስዊር ፣ ዘሀሎ ፣ ውስቴ ታ፡፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ለቅዱስ ። ወአፍለስዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ካልእት ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወነበረ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ እስ ከ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ መጠነ ፣ ፪፻ወጓዓመተ ። ወኮነ ፡ አ ባ ፣ሚካኤል ፣ ተሠይመ ፡ መጋቤ ፥-ላዕለ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፣ መቃር ስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹም ፡ ጸመ ፣ ዓቢየ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ፡አ ባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ገዓረ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አነ ፡ እፈቱ ፡ እምእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርድአ ኒ ፡ "ወአእርከብ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ በማእ
On this day is the translation of the body of Saint * Abba Macarius the Great to the desert of Scetis. Indeed, after his death, people from the city of Sasewir came, stole the body of the saint, and built him a church in their city. It remained (there) until the Caliphs reigned over the city of Sasewir, in which the body of the saint was found. (Then), it was transported to another city. It remained there until the days of Abba John, patriarch, for 440 years. Abba Michael was instituted as procurator of the church of Saint Abba Macarius. When the patriarch had gone up, to perform a great fast, to the convent of Abba Macarius, he cried out, and said: "For me, I desire by Our Lord Christ that (Saint Macarius) help me to * find prosperous days, and that the body of Saint Abba Macarius be in our midst, in this convent."
ከሌነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ደብር ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፣፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሖረ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ መጋቤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያ ን ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ደብር ። ወተሀውከ ፣ ውስ
A few days later, Abba Michael, the steward of the church, went away with some elder-monks on monastery business. A spiritual thought stirred


[p. 356] {#po.9.p356}
ተ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መክን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሥጋ ሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥአዎ ። ወተጋብኡ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገ ር፣ምስለ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ አብትርት ፡ ወአስይፍት ፡ ወከልእአዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወኖሙ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፣ መነኮሳት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቴክዙ፣ ወየሐዝኑ ፣ በልቦሙ ። ወበይእቲ ፣ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣፡ መቃርስ ፡ ለ መኩንን ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትከልአኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖ”ር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውሉድየ ፡ ኅድገ ኒ ፡ እሖር ፡፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካንየ ፡ ወኢትክልኦሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውስዱኒ ፣ ምስሌ
in their hearts. They came to the sanctuary in which the body of Saint Abba Macarius was located, in order to remove it. All the people of the city gathered with the judge. They had sticks and swords. They prevented them from removing the body of the saint. The elder-monks fell asleep, being sad and afflicted in their hearts. That night, Saint Abba Macarius appeared to the judge and said to him: "Why have you prevented me from * going away with my sons? Let me go away with them to my sanctuary, and do not prevent them from carrying me away with them."
ሆሙ። ወበጽባሕ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈርህ ፡ ወይርዕድ ፡ ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ለአረጋ ዊያን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ወመጠወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ፍሥሐ ። ወተጽዕኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ተርኑጥ ፡ ወ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡-እንዘ ፣ ያስተፋንው ፡ ሥጋሁ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወሐደሩ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ወቀደሱ ፡ ወተመጠው ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፣ ቅድሳት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጾርዎ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ገዳም ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ፍ ኖት ፡ ዘገዳም ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፉ ፡ ንሥቲተ ፡ እምድክሞሙ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አ
In the morning, the judge arose, afraid and trembling. He called the holy elders and gave them the body of Saint Abba Macarius. They carried it away in great joy. They boarded a ship and arrived at the city of Tarnout. With them were many people from all the regions, who were accompanying the body of the saint. They spent that night (in thanksgiving): they prayed, they celebrated the Sacrifice, and they received the holy Mysteries. Then they carried (the body of the saint) to the desert. While


[p. 357] {#po.9.p357}
ባ፣0ሚካኤል ፡ ሕያው ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኢናዕርፍ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይከሥት ፡ ለነ ፡ አግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወያርእየነ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዘአኃዘ ፡ እዴሁ” ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃር ስ፣ውስቴቱ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ገመል ፡ ዘይጸውር ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡በ ረከ ፡ በብረኪሁ ፡ ወኢተንሥአ ፡ ወአኃዘ ፡ የዐውድ ፡ በርአሱ ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ ወይልሕ ስ ፡ ሣጹነ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወአእመሩ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡መነኮሳት ፡ ከመ፣ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወአንከሩ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ ወውእ ቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ፅውቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርቡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ዙሎሙ፣0: መነኮሳት ፣ ወተቀበልዎ ፡ በዓቢይ ፣ ፍሥሐ ፡ በዝማሬ ፣ ወበማሕሌት ፡ ወምስሌሆመ ፡ መሐትው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበርሁ ፡ ወጾርዎ ፡ ዲበ ፣ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡፤ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ እስከ ፡ አብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ክብር ። ወገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተአምራተ ፣ ብዙኃተ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ፍልሰተ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ክቡረ ፡
they were going, they arrived at the halfway point of the desert and wanted to rest a little from their fatigue. Abba Michael said to them: "As the Lord lives! Let us not rest until the Lord shows us and lets us see the place where a Cherub took * the hand of our holy Father Abba Macarius." When the camel that was carrying the body of the saint had reached this place, it knelt down, did not rise again, and began to turn its head from side to side and to lick the shrine in which the body of the saint was. The elder-monks recognized that it was the place (in question). They were extremely astonished, and they glorified the Lord greatly. This place is famous to this day. When they were near the monastery, all the monks went out and went to meet (the body of the saint) in great joy, amidst psalms and chants. They had lit torches with them. They carried (the body of the saint) on their heads. They chanted psalms until they had brought it into the church with great pomp. The Lord performed many miracles that day. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen. [I bid greeting to the translation of the glorious body


[p. 358] {#po.9.p358}
ለመቃርዮስ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ሐነፀ ፡ ደብረ ፡፡ በከሊአ ፡ በድኑ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ለአለ ፣ አንሥኡ ፡ በትረ ፡=፡ ለምንት ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ትከልኡኒ ፡ ሐዊረ = ምስለ ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ አሀሉ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡
Of Macarius the Great, to the place where he built a monastery! To those who raised the stick to prevent the (translation) of his holy body: "Why," he said to them, "do you prevent me from going to dwell together with my children?"]
እምደቂቀ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ፳በሐሩረ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ዘሰርሐ ፡፡ ይበሰ ፡ ኅብስቱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ በማይ ፡ አላሕልሐ ።፡ ነጺሮ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ከልሐ ፡=፡ ርእዩ ፡ ኤልያስሃ፣ በውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ጸብሐ ።፡
[(When) one of the children of Macarius, who was overwhelmed by the heat of the sun, had dipped his dry bread in water, his companion, seeing him, cried out saying: "See how Elijah ate (his) stew in the desert."]


[p. 359] {#po.9.p359}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፳8ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 21ST OF NAHASÉ (August 27).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ሰላማ ፡ መተርጉም ፡ ወተዝክከራ ፣ ለቅድስት ፡ ጊዛ ዊ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም። ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ለኦመ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ስርዋ = ትእዛዛተ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወወንጌል ፡ እንተ ፡ ዲቤከ ፡ ተክዕዋ ፡፡ ሰላማ ፡፣ ዝክርከ ፡ ከመ ፡ በኅቤነ ፡ ነዋ ፡፡ በከናፍሪከ ፡ ምዑዛት ፣፡ እመዓዛ ፡ “ከርቤ ፡ ወዐልዋ ፡=፡
On this day died Abba Salämâ, the translator (of the Scriptures). (It is also) the memorial of Saint Hizäwi. May their blessing be with us for ever and ever! [Greeting to you, root of the tree of faith, upon which the commandments of the Law and the Gospel have grown, Salâmä, as your fame (bears it) among us! By your lips, sweeter than the perfume of myrrh and aloes, the Scriptures were translated from Arabic into Ge'ez.]
እምነ ፡ ዓረቢ ፡ ለግዕዝ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ተዓልዋ ።፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፉ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ አለ ፡ እምሀገረ ፣ ኤፌሶን ። "እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ሶ በ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዳኪዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ኮኑ ፡ እሉ ፡ ሂአምሐራሁ ፡ ለንጉ ሥ ፡ ወሜሞሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መዛግብቲሁ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ አቆመ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ አስተዋ
On this day died the seven young men of the city of Ephesus. * These saints, who lived in the days of Decius, the impious emperor, were among the seven soldiers of the emperor. He appointed them to (the guard of) his treasures. When he had prescribed the worship of idols, these saints were accused before him;


[p. 360] {#po.9.p360}
ደይዎሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ በኅቤሁ ፡ ወአኃዞሙ ፣ ወሞቅሖሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ን ጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መካን ፣ ፈትሖሙ ፣"እማአሰሮሙ ፣ ወሰደዶሙ ፡ እ ስከ ፡፣ አመ ፣ ይትመየጥ ፡፣ ወሐለየ ፡ በልዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፣፡ እምክሮ ሙ ፡ሠናይ። ወሶበ ፥ ወፅአ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ መነኑ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ግብረ ፣ ሐራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይስግዱ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ርኩሳን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ በዓት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወ ፈጸሙ ፡፣ አሩሃ ፡ ለበዓት ፣ ወኖሙ ፣፡ ውስቴታ ። ወሀሎ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብሩር ፡ ዘጽሑ ፍ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ስሙ ፡፣ ለዳኪዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ወኮነ ፡ ለለ ፡ አእምኔ”ሆሙ ፡ ይገይሥ ፣ ኩ ሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ወይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወይሣየጥ ፣፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ወይሰምዕ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፣ ከመ ፡ ዳኪዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኤ ፌሶን ፣ ፈጸሙ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዓት ፡ ወኖሙ ።
he arrested them and put them in prison. When the emperor wanted to leave for another place, he released them * from their chains and exiled them until he returned. He thought in his heart that they would return from their good purpose. When he had left the city, the saints renounced the profession of soldier, so as not to worship the impure idols. Then they went to a cave in a mountain; they blocked the entrance of the cave, and they fell asleep there. They had with them money on which was inscribed the name of the Emperor Decius. One of them, each (in turn), would leave in the morning every day, enter the city, buy food for them (all), and listen to the news of the emperor. When they learned that the Emperor Decius had arrived in the city of Ephesus, they blocked the door of the cave upon themselves and fell asleep.
ወኮነ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ ሐራ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ የአምር ፤ መክኖሙ ፡ ወፀንሖሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባ ኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወኢቦኡ ። ወተንሥአ ፣ ውእ ቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዓት ፡ ወረከቦሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ አን ቀጸ ፡ በዓት ፡ እምውስጥ ፡ ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ጽላተ ፡ ዘብር ት ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ በመጥባሕት ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ኮነ፣፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ወወ
One of the soldiers, a believer, knew their retreat. He expected (to see them) enter the city after the arrival of the emperor; but they did not enter. This soldier arose and left; he arrived at the cave and noticed that they had blocked the door of the cave from the inside. It seemed to him that they were dead. He took a bronze tablet, wrote their struggle on it with (his) knife


[p. 361] {#po.9.p361}
ገረ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጽላተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዓት ። ወለቅዱሳንሰ ፡ አኅዞሙ ፡ ሐዘን ፡ ወኖ ሙ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፻ያ፻ፎወ፪ዓመተ ። ወሞተ ፡ዳኪዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፤ ወነግሠ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ ነገሥት ። እስከ ፣ አመ ፡ መዋፅሊሁ ፡፣ለቴዎዶይስዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ አመ ፡ ቋወቿዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ። ተባሀሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ይከውን ፡ትንሣኤ ፣፡ ምው ታን ፡ ወተለውዎሙ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ጽድቀ ፡ ወያጠይቆሙ ፡ለሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ሙታን ፡ሀሎ ፡ ይኩን ። ወአንቅሆ ሙ ፡ ለአሉ ፡ ጳደቂቅ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወወሀቡ ፡ ለጳእምኔሆሙ ፡ እምነ ፡ ብሩር ፡ አለ፡ም ስሌሆሙ ፡ ወአዘዝዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ወይሣየጥ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ፡ ወይስማዕ ፡ ዜ ናሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዳኪዮስ ፡ ዓላዊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ ላዕ”ሌሁ ፡ ግብራ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ መስቀላተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወዲበ ፡ አረፍታቲሃ ። ወሰምዖ ሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ አንዘ ፡ ይምሕሉ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተስአእ ለ ፡ እምነ ፡ አጸወይቤሎ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ ወአውሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይቤ ሎ ፡ አወ ። ወአውፅአ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ብሩረ ፡ ዘምስሌሁ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ለ፳ብእሲ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ምስ ያጥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሣየጥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ።
and their story, and threw this tablet into the cave. As for the saints, sadness took them, and they slept, by the command of the Lord, for 372 years. Decius, the impious emperor, died. Many emperors reigned after him, until the days of the Emperor Theodosius. In the 38th year of his reign, men were arguing among themselves, saying: "There will be no resurrection of the dead," and many people adhered to (their opinion). The Lord wanted to manifest the truth and prove to (these) men that the resurrection of the dead would take place. He awakened these seven holy young men. (The latter) gave one of them money that they had with them and charged him to go and buy food for them and to listen to the news of Decius, the impious emperor. When he had entered the city, its appearance was changed * for him: he saw crosses on the gates of the city and on its walls; he heard the people swearing by the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ. He questioned someone and said to him: "Is this not the city of Ephesus?" The latter answered him and said to him: "Yes." He took out the money he had with him and gave it to a merchant in order to buy food for himself.


[p. 362] {#po.9.p362}
ወዕበ ፡ ርእየ ፣ በዐለ ፣ ምሥያጥ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ብሩረ ፣ ኢረከቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብሩር ፡ ዘው እቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ”ዘይሣየጡ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለዳኪዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፣ አኀዞ ፡ ወአሰሮ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፣ አንተ ፣ ተኃሥሥ ፡ መዛግብተ ፣ ዘቀዲ ሙ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ብሩር ። ወእንዘ ፣፡ ይትባአሱ ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፣ ኀቤሆሙ ፡፣ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ወተስእልዎ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡እ ምአይቴ ፡ አንተ ። ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አነ ፣ እምዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፣ መነ ፡ ተአምር ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እገሌ ፡ ወእገሌ ፡ ወአሙንቱ ሰ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ የአምሮሙ ፣ ኢተረፈ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ 8፳ግሙራ ፡
When the merchant saw this money, he did not take it for the money of the time with * which one traded, but he noticed that the name of Decius, the impious emperor, was inscribed on it. He arrested him and chained him. He said to him: "You have dug up ancient treasures; here is the proof of that with you through this money." While they were fighting each other, many people gathered around them. They questioned (the saint) and said to him: "Where are you from?" He answered them and said to them: "I am from this city." They said to him: "Who do you know among the people of this city?" He said to them: "Such and such a person." But of the people he said he knew, none of them existed anymore. They dragged him and became angry with him.
ወሰሐብጾሎ ፡ ወተምዑ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወተሰምዐ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ኤ"ኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ቴ ዎዶስዮስ ። ወአብጽሕቻ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘእምኒደቂቅ ፡ ወተስእልዎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወኤ ሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንግረነ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔከ ፣ ወእምአይ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አንተ ። ወአው ሥኦሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ሂፅደው ፡ ወኮነ ፣ እምሐራሁ ፡ ለዳኪዮስ ፡ ንጉ ሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መካን ፡ ወወፃእነ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ኤፌሶ
His story was heard by * Bishop Abba Theodore and the Emperor Theodosius. They brought this man, who (was one of) the seven young men. The emperor and the bishop questioned him and said to him: "Tell us what happened to you, and what city you are from." He answered them and said to them: "As for us, we are seven men (who were part of) the army of Decius, the impious emperor. When he left for another place, we


[p. 363] {#po.9.p363}
ን ፡ ወቦእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ በዐት ። ወፈጸምነ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ በዐት ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ወሰከብነ ወኖምነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወናሁ ፡ አብያጺየ ፡ ሀለው ፡ ይሰክቡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት ፡ ወወ ፅኩ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡፣ ውስተ ፣ ይእቲ ፣ በዐት ፣ ወረከብዎሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይነብሩ ፣ ወውእቱ ፡፣ ጽላት ፣፡ጽሑ ፍ ፡ ወግዱፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ። ወአንበቡ ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጽላት ፣ ወረ ከቡ ፡ ሐሳቦ ፡ እመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዳኪዮስ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ዓላዊ ፣፤ ወውእቱ ፡ ፻፻፳ወ፪ዓመት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፣ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአለ፣ ኢአምኑ ፡ በትንሣኤ ፣ ምውታን ፡ አምኑ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተስአልዎሙ ፡ ለደቂቅ ፡ ወ አይድፅዎም ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ አምኔሆሙ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ወሰከቡ ፡ ወመጠው ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፥ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወገብረ ፡ ሎሙ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ሣጹናተ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ወገ ነዞሙ ፡ በአልባሳተ ፡ ሐሪራት ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ወአንበርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሙንቱ ፡ ሣጹና ት ፡ ዘወርቅ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሆሙ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። ወዝው እቱ ፣ አስማቲሆሙ ። መክሷማኖስ ። ታሙኪግርሮስ ፡ መርዲምስ ። ዮሐንስ ። ቄስጠን
left the city of Ephesus ourselves. We entered a cave; we blocked the door of the cave upon us; we lay down, and we slept until now. Here are my companions lying in the cave." The emperor, the bishop, and all the people (who were) with them left and came to the cave. They found the saints sitting, and the written tablet thrown into the cave. They read what was written on this tablet: they found its date under the days of Decius, the impious emperor; it had been 372 years. When the emperor, the bishop, and all the people had seen this, they were extremely astonished, and they glorified the Lord greatly. Those who did not believe in the resurrection of the dead believed in it on the spot when they had seen this miracle. When the seven young men had been questioned and (when they) had told everything that had happened to them, they returned to lie down, and they surrendered their soul into the hand of the Lord. The emperor made golden shrines for them and buried them in beautiful silk garments. They were placed in these golden shrines. Many miracles and wonders occurred through their bodies. Here are their names: Maximianus, Tâmoukigros,


[p. 364] {#po.9.p364}
ጢኖስ ። እንጦንዮስ ። ዲዮናስዮስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፣፤ ይምሐረነ ፣ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከ ቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ሂዕደወ ፡፡ አዝማነ ፡ ፅልወታት ፡ ያኅልፉ ፡ አለ ፡ አድምፁ ፡ ብፃወ ፡፡ አፀደ ፡ አባግዕ ፣ ይሕንጽ ፡ አመ ፡ ብእሷ ፡ ፈተወ ፡=፡ ማዕፃ ፣፡ በፀቶሙ ፣ በሥምረተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ተርኅወ ፡፡
Mardimes, John, Constantine, Anthony, Dionysius. May the Lord have mercy on us through their prayer, and may their blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen. [I bid greeting to the seven men who found sleep to let the ages of impiety pass! When a man desired to build a fold, the door of their cave opened by the will of God, so that, having awakened, they might make their way through it.]
ነቂሆሙ ፡ እንተ ፡ህየ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ፍናወ ፡ ሰላም ፡ለያ"ዕቆብ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ምግባር ፡ በሚመቱ ።፡ ወርቱዕ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በሕገ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ክህነቱ ፡፡ ብሔረ ፡ ዓግዓዚ ፡ በጺሖ ፣ ውስተ ፡ብሔረ ፡ግብጽ ፡በተመይጦቱ ፡፡ ረከበ ፡ እሳቶ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ጥፍአት ፡ በቤቱ ፡=፡ እምአመ ፡ ተደፍነ ፡ በዓመት ፡ ኒቱ ፡
[Greeting to * James, upright (in his) works during his office, and also righteous according to the canonical law of his priesthood! When he arrived in the land of Ethiopia, on his return from the land of Egypt, he found his fire not extinguished in his house, although it had been covered for seven years.]


[p. 365] {#po.9.p365}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፳አለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 21ST OF NAHASÉ (August 28).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በዐላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላ ዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወትተነብል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ዙ ሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካራ ። ትንብልናሃ ፡ ወጸሎታ ፡ ወ
On this day is the memorial of the feast of Our Lady the Holy Virgin Mary, Mother of God, through whom she is the salvation of the whole world, and intercedes for us at all times, and for all those who make her memorial. May her intercession, her prayer, and * her blessing be with us! Amen.
በ"ረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለኪ ፣ እንተ ፡ እምኔኪ ፡ ወፅአ = ዘያድኅን ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወሰብአ ።፡ በቀስለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ እስመ ፡ በኅብጐ ፡ ወፄዓ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ፳ደ ፡ በፉዓ ፡፡
[Greeting to you, from whom came forth the Lord, who saves animals and men! Since through the wound of sin my body rots and stinks, I would have, (8) Mary, no use, if you did not make me a new figure.]
እመ ፡ ኢገበርኪ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ሐዲሰ ፡ መልክዓ ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አዕረፈት ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ቲሪኒ ፡ ዘበትርጓ"ሜሃ ፡ ሰላም ። ዛ ቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ሊኪያኖስ ፣ ወሐነጸ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ማኅፈደ ፡ ወረሰ
On this day also died Saint Irene, (a word) which in its interpretation (means): peace. This saint was the daughter of King Licinius. He built for her


[p. 366] {#po.9.p366}
የ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አረፍታቲሁ ፡ ቿመስኮተ ፡ ወአውዱ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፡ አቅጻራት ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ላቲ፣ ማዕደ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፣ ወጽዋዓተ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፣ ወብሩር ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ አረጋዌ ፡ከ መ ፡ ይምሐራ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንበራ ፡ ውስተ.፣ ውእቱ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ በኅቤሃ ፣ ያአእማ ተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መልእክታ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አጸወ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ፕኅተ ፣ ማኅፈድ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ው እቱ ፣ አረጋዊ ፡ መምሀር ፡ ይሜህራ ፡ እምአፍአ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሃ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊ
a tower, and made sixty windows in its walls; around it (there were) twelve enclosures. He made for (Irene) a table of gold and cups of gold and silver. He ordered that an old man instruct her. Then, he placed in the tower beside her three servants for her service; then he closed the door of the tower upon her. The old man, (her) master, instructed her from the outside. The age (of Irene) at that time was six years.
ዜ ፡ ፪ዓመት። ወእምዝ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ርእየት ፣፡ር ግበ ፡ ወውስተ ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ቄፅለ ፣ ዘይት ፡ ወአንበረታ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ማዕድ ። ወእምዝ ፣፡ መጽ አ ፡ ንስር ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አክሊል ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ማዕድ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ቋዕ ፡ ወምስ ሌሁ ፣ ከይሲ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ማዕድ ፡ ወደንገፀት ፣ እምዛቲ ፡ ራእይ ፣ ወአይድዓ ቶ፣ ለመምህራ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ርግብሰ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትምሀርተ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወቄፅለ፣ ዘይ”ትኒ ፡ ይእቲ ፣ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ። ወንስርሰ ፡ መዊእ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አክሊልኒ ፡ ክብ ሮሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ውእቱ ። ወቋዕሰ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወከይሷኒ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ውአቱ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ መምህራ ፡ አንቲሰ ፣ ሀለወኪ ፡ ከመ ፣ ትትጋደሊ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለክርስ
Then, one day when she was sitting on the tower, she saw a dove; in its beak was an olive branch; it placed it on the table. Then came an eagle, carrying a crown; it placed it on the table. Came a raven, carrying a serpent; it placed it on the table. She was terrified by this vision, and she told it to her master. He answered and said to her: "The dove is the doctrine of the Law; the olive branch is the baptism of Christianity; the eagle is victory; the crown is the glory of the righteous; the raven is the king; the serpent is torment." Her master said to her: "As for you, you must practice asceticism for the name of Christ."
ቶስ ። ወአቡሃሰ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐውጻጳ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኦወለትየ ፣ አንሰ ፣ እፈ
Her father, the king, came to visit her. He said to her: "O my daughter, as for me, I


[p. 367] {#po.9.p367}
ቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ አስተዋስብኪ ፡ ለ8እመኳንንት ፡ ልሂቃን ። ወሰአለቶ ፣ ወትቤሎ ፣ ኦአቡ የ ፡ ተፀገሥ ፡ላዕሌየ ፡ ይመዓልተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፣ እትማከር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍስየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ እምኅቤሃ ፡ ላጸየት ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ወቆመት ፣፡ በቅድመ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ወሰአለቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምክርዋ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብሳብ፣ ወኢተናዢዝርዋ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወአንሥአት ፡ አፅይንቲሃ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወትቤ ፡ ኦአምላከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ምርሐኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ስምረትከ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወይቤላ ፣ ናሁ ፣ በሳኒታ ፡ ይመጽእ ፣ 8እምአርዳኢሁ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ኀ በ፡ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወያጠምቀኪ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስትና ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ላቲ ፡ ጸእምአርድእቱ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፣ ወአጥመቃ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ።
want to give you in marriage to one of the noble princes." She asked him and said to him: "O my father, be patient with me for three days, until I have taken counsel with myself." When her father had gone away from her, she shaved the hair of her head. She stood before the idols that were in the tower and asked them to give her counsel regarding marriage; they did not speak to her at all. She lifted her eyes toward heaven and said: "O God of heaven and earth, lead me toward your good pleasure." An angel of the Lord came to her and said to her: "Behold, tomorrow one of the disciples of the Apostle Paul will come to this city and will baptize you with the baptism of Christianity." The next day, the Lord sent her one of the disciples of the Apostle Paul, (who) baptized her with the baptism of Christianity.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፻መፀልት ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተካየደቶሙ፣ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ አአምን ፡ በእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወ ልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፤ ፈጣሬ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወምድር ፡ ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፣ አቡ ሃ ፡ ወአውፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእስርዋ ፡ ወይግድፍዋ ፣ው ስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ወይኪድዋ ፡ በአፍራስ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ወኢበ ጽሐ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እምእኩይ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በ
Three days later, her father and mother came to her, as she had agreed with them. She said to them: "I am a Christian. I believe in my Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living Lord, creator of heaven and earth." Her father became angry with her, made her go out into the middle of the city, and ordered that she be chained, thrown on the road, and trampled by horses. They did that to her, as he had ordered. Nothing bad happened to her at all. When her parents saw that, they believed in Our Lord Christ,


[p. 368] {#po.9.p368}
እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣፡ ወኀደጉ ፡ መንግሥቶሙ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ”ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ።
were extremely astonished, abandoned their kingdom, went to the tower, and dwelt there.
ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዓ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ዘቅሩብ ፣ አምብሔሮሙ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ "ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እም ኔሆሙ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡፣ ሀገር ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፤ ተመየጡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መንግሥት ክሙ ፡ወኢፈቀዱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወኩነና ፡፣ ለቅድስት ፡ ጊ ሪኒ ፤ ወሰደደ ፣ ላዕሌሃ ፣፡ አናብስተ ፡ ወአክይስተ ፡ ወኢበጽሐ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እምእኩ ይ ፤ ግሙራ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰራ ፡ በሞሰርት ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዳ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓቢ የ ። ወኮነ ፡ አግዚአነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ያድኅና ፡ ወያነሥአ ፡ ጥዕይተ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ዙኔኔ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወአበዊሃሰ ፡ ጸውዕዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ካህን ፡ ዘአጥመቃ ፡ ወአ ጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ ይያ፻አምአግብርቲሆሙ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡፤
When the king, who was near their country, had learned their story (and had known) what had happened to them, he came to the city and said to them: "Return to your kingdom." (But) they did not want to. He reigned over the city. He tortured Saint Irene, sent lions and serpents against her; (but) nothing bad happened to her at all. Then, he sawed her with a saw, and he put a large stone around her neck. Our Lord Christ saved her and made her emerge safe and sound from all the tortures, without injury. Her parents called the priest who had baptized her, and he baptized them with the baptism of Christianity; with them (he baptized) three hundred of their servants and many of their relatives and people of the city.
አምአዝማዲሆሙ ፡ ወአእምሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰምዐ ፣ ዜናሃ ፣ ኑማርያኖስ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ወአብጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወወሰዳ፣ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቃላኒካክ ፡ ወኩነና ፡ በህየ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሞቅሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብዕራ ፡ ዘብ ርት ፡ ወሰበሮ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብፅራ ፡ ዘብርት ፣ ወአውፅአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ወ ሞተ ፡ ኑማርያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ሶፎር ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሃ ፣
Then, King Numerian learned her story. He had her brought to him and led her to the city of Qâlänikä [Callinicus]. He tortured her there with a great torture. He chained her in a bronze ox. (But) the Lord broke this bronze ox and brought her out of it. King Numerian died. In his place reigned King Sapor. He


[p. 369] {#po.9.p369}
ለቅድስት ፡ ሂሪኒ ፣፡ ወአምጽአ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡-ወ”ረገዛ ፣ በረምህ ፡ በአዴሁ ፣ ወመጠወት ፤ ነፍሳ ። ወአንሥአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እሞት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ሳፎር ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘን ተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ወአምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወ ምስሌሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ሀገር ። ወጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአለ ፡ አምኑ በእንቲአ ሃ ፡ ፻ጽ፲ወ፻ጽወአዕረፉ ፡ አቡሃ ፣ ወእማ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፣ ወለቅድስትሰ ፡ ጊ ሪኒ ፡ ጾረት ፡ ኅይለ ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ኤፌሶን ፡ ወገብረት ፡ በሀየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ተአምኖታ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወነገሥተ ፣ መቄይንያ ፡ ወነገሥተ ፡ ቃላኒካ ፡ ወቄስጥንጥንያ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ት ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወሐነጹ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፣ ሠናይተ ፡ ወአንበሩ ፡ ሥጋ ሃ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፣ ብዙኃት ፣ ወፈው ሰ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ለድውያን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ም
learned the story of Saint Irene, had her brought to him, and pierced her with a spear from his hand; she gave up her soul; (but) Our Lord Christ resurrected her from the dead. When King Sapor had seen this miracle, he was extremely astonished, prostrated himself before (Irene), and believed in Our Lord Christ. With him (believed) many people of the men of the city; the number of those who believed because of (Saint Irene) was 113,000. Her father and mother died in the tower. As for Saint Irene, the divine power carried (her) to the city of Ephesus; she performed many miracles there; she confessed Our Lord Christ before the kings of Persia, the kings of Macedonia, and the kings of Qâlänikä [Callinicus] and Constantinople. Then, she died in great glory. They built a beautiful church for her and placed her body there. Miracles and numerous wonders and great healings of the sick appeared through (her body). May the Lord have mercy on us through her prayer, and may her blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen
ስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለጌሬኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ለስማ ፡፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ዘርእየት ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ዘይት ፡ በሕልማ ።፡ ጊዜ ፡ ነጸሩ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ በጽንዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ቀዊማ ፤
[Salutation to Irene [peace], as her name signifies, who saw in her dream the mystery of baptism, in the form of an olive tree! At the moment when they looked at her, while she was fearless in the strength of faith,


[p. 370] {#po.9.p370}
ሶበ ፡ ኢክህለ ፡ ሙቃሔ ፡ ወእግረ ፡ ፈረስ ፡ ኢያሕመማ ፡፡ አሠረ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ተለዉ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ።
When the chains could not (break her), and (when) the feet of the horses did not cause her to suffer, her father and mother followed in her own footsteps.] In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God.
አመ ፡ ፳፪ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
READING FOR THE 22ND OF NAHASÉ (August 29).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቢይ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ሚኪያስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዩኤል ፡ ወይሰመይ ፤ አቡሁ ፡ ከዕበ ፡ ዓራም ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ በመዋዕሊ”ሁ ፡ ለኢዮሳፍጥ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወወልዱ ፡ ኢዮራም ፡ ወአካዝያ ፡ ወገታልያ ፡ ንግሥት ። ወነበበ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ፡ በአፉሁ ፡በ”ምግባራት ፡ ዓበይት ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወ ይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ አመካኑ ፡ ወይወርድ ፤ ወይከይድ ፣፡ ዲበ፣ምድ ር ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ "በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡ወ ይቤ ፡ ወአንቲኒ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ልሔም ፡ ቤተ ፣ ኤፍረታ ፡ ኢትቴሐቲ ፡ እምነ ፡ ነገሥተ ፡ ይሁ ዳ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምኔኪ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘይሬእዮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብየ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወፀአ ቱ ፡ አምቅድመ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወተነበየ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ንስተቱ ፣፤ ለምኩራበ ፣ አይ
On this day died the great prophet Micah, son of Joel. His father is also called 'Arâm. This righteous man prophesied in the days of Jehoshaphat, king of Judah, of his son Joram, of Ahaziah, and of Queen Athaliah. The Lord uttered great things through his mouth. He prophesied concerning the Incarnation of Our Lord, and he said: Behold, the Lord will come out of his place, and will descend and walk upon the earth || he prophesied concerning the birth of Our Lord in Bethlehem of Judah, and he said: For you, Bethlehem, house of Ephrathah, you are not the least (of the lands) of the princes of Judah, for from you shall come forth the King, who will shepherd my people Israel, (he whose) origin is before the creation of the world.


[p. 371] {#po.9.p371}
ሁድ ፡ ወአንሥኦተ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ምድር ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፀአተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ ሕግ ፣ እምጽዮን ፡፣ ወቃለ ፡ እ ግዚአብሔር ፤ እምኢየሩሳሌም ። ወተነበየ ፡ ለአካብ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትመውት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፀብአ ፡ ኤይም ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ገላኣድ ። ወዕበ ፣ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡፣ ዓመታተ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ፣ር ሥእና ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ፈለሰ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ አምቅድመ ፡ ት ስብእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ፳፻ዓመተ ፣ ወተቀብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓጸድ ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ምራታ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፣ ወስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
He prophesied concerning the destruction of the temple of the Jews and the building of another temple in its place in all the ends of the earth. He prophesied concerning the spread of the Gospel, and he said: The Law shall go forth from Zion and the word of the Lord from Jerusalem. He prophesied to Ahab, king of Israel, and he said to him: You yourself shall die in the war against Edom, king of Gilead. When many years had passed for this prophet, and he had reached a beautiful old age, he emigrated to the Lord, whom he had loved. He prophesied 800 years before the Incarnation of Our Lord Jesus Christ. He was buried in a village called Merâtâ. May the Lord have mercy on us through his prayer! Glory to the Lord for ever and ever! Amen.
ሰላም ፣ ለሚኪያስ ፡ ነባቤ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ በልሳኑ ፡፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በበዘመኑ ፡=፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ጊሩተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ይዜኑ 5፡ ከማከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ እምእለ ፡ አማልክት ፡ አኮኑ አበሳ ፡ ርስቱ ፡ ዘይትዓደው ፡ መኑ ፡፡
[Salutation to Micah, who uttered prophecies by his tongue, (On) every (event) that would happen in his time! He announced the goodness of God, saying thus: Who among the gods is like you, O Lord, is it not so? Who is it that forgives the sins of his inheritance

በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። አመ ፡ ፳፻ለነሐሴ ፡ ምንባብ ።
In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. READING FOR THE 23RD OF NAHASÉ (August 30).
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፻፳ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ እምአኃዊነ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ እምሀገ ረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰደዶ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ መርቅያኖስ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴተ ፡ ጋግራ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ሁከት ፡ ወጋ እዝ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወሶበ ፣ ሞተ ፤ መርቅያኖስ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ልዮን ፡ ጫሜ ሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ሮም ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አብሩታርዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድር ያ ፡ ወየአምን ፡ በማኅበረ ፡ ከልቄዶንያ ። ወኢተወክፍዎ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፤ ዘእንበለ ፣ ውሐዳን ፡ ሰብእ ። ወእለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ይትሜ ጠው ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ እምነ ፡ ቀሳውስት ፡ አለ ፣ ጫምዎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቄርሎ
On this day are the thirty thousand martyrs of our brothers the Christians of the city of Alexandria. When the impious King Marcian had exiled our father Dioscorus to the Island of Gâgrâ [Gangra], there was in the city of Alexandria trouble and a great quarrel, for many years. When Marcian was dead and Leo reigned, the people of Rome [Rôm] established over them as patriarch a man from among the people of the city of Alexandria, called 'Abroutäryos [Proterius]. He was a priest in the city of Alexandria; he believed in the Council of Chalcedon. (Most) of the people of the city of Alexandria did not receive him, except for a few people. The rest of the people of the city received the holy mysteries from the priests whom our father Cyril and our father Abba Dioscorus had ordained.
ስ ፡ ወአቡነ ፡ አባ ፣ ዲዮስቆሮስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ አብሩታርዮስ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብያጺሁ ፡ እለ ፡ የአ ምኑ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ወአውገዞ ፣ ለአውታኪ ፡፣ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተቶስሐ ፡
When 'Abroutäryos was instituted (patriarch), he held a council with his colleagues, who believed in his word. || he excommunicated the priest Eutyches, who
